namespace Infragistics.Shared
{
	internal sealed class SR
	{
		private static Infragistics.Shared.SR loader;

		private ResourceManager[] resources;

		private SR()
		{
			string text = "Infragistics.Win.UltraWinTabControl.strings";
			string[] array = text.Split(';');
			int num = 0;
			string[] array2 = array;
			foreach (string text2 in array2)
			{
				if (text2 != null && text2.Length > 0)
				{
					num++;
				}
			}
			resources = new ResourceManager[num];
			Assembly assembly = GetType().Assembly;
			int num2 = 0;
			string[] array3 = array;
			foreach (string text3 in array3)
			{
				if (text3 != null && text3.Length > 0)
				{
					resources[num2++] = new ResourceManager(text3, assembly);
				}
			}
		}

		private static Infragistics.Shared.SR GetLoader()
		{
			if (loader == null)
			{
				lock (typeof(Infragistics.Shared.SR))
				{
					if (loader == null)
					{
						loader = new Infragistics.Shared.SR();
					}
				}
			}
			return loader;
		}

		public static string GetString(string name)
		{
			return GetString(null, name);
		}

		public static string GetString(string name, params object[] args)
		{
			return GetString(null, name, args);
		}

		public static string GetString(CultureInfo culture, string name)
		{
			return GetString(culture, name, null);
		}

		public static string GetString(CultureInfo culture, string name, params object[] args)
		{
			return GetStringHelper(allowCustomizedStrings: true, culture, name, args);
		}

		private static string GetStringHelper(bool allowCustomizedStrings, CultureInfo culture, string name, params object[] args)
		{
			string text = null;
			if (allowCustomizedStrings)
			{
				text = global::ResourceCustomizerLocator.Customizer.GetCustomizedString(name);
			}
			if (text == null)
			{
				Infragistics.Shared.SR sR = GetLoader();
				if (sR == null)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				text = sR.GetString(name, culture);
				if (text == null)
				{
					text = string.Empty;
				}
			}
			if (args != null && args.Length > 0 && text.Length > 2)
			{
				try
				{
					text = string.Format(null, text, args);
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
			return text;
		}

		private string GetString(string name, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			ResourceManager[] array = resources;
			foreach (ResourceManager resourceManager in array)
			{
				string text = resourceManager.GetString(name, culture);
				if (text != null)
				{
					return text;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static string GetUncustomizedString(string name)
		{
			return GetUncustomizedString(null, name);
		}

		public static string GetUncustomizedString(string name, params object[] args)
		{
			return GetUncustomizedString(null, name, args);
		}

		public static string GetUncustomizedString(CultureInfo culture, string name)
		{
			return GetUncustomizedString(culture, name, null);
		}

		public static string GetUncustomizedString(CultureInfo culture, string name, params object[] args)
		{
			return GetStringHelper(allowCustomizedStrings: false, culture, name, args);
		}

		public static object GetObject(string name)
		{
			return GetObject(null, name);
		}

		public static object GetObject(CultureInfo culture, string name)
		{
			return GetLoader()?.GetObject(name, culture);
		}

		private object GetObject(string name, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			ResourceManager[] array = resources;
			foreach (ResourceManager resourceManager in array)
			{
				object obj = resourceManager.GetObject(name, culture);
				if (obj != null)
				{
					return obj;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	[AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.All, AllowMultiple = false, Inherited = true)]
	internal sealed class LocalizedCategoryAttribute : CategoryAttribute
	{
		public LocalizedCategoryAttribute(string category)
			: base(category)
		{
		}

		protected override string GetLocalizedString(string value)
		{
			return Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetUncustomizedString(null, value);
		}
	}
	[AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.All, AllowMultiple = false, Inherited = true)]
	internal sealed class LocalizedDescriptionAttribute : DescriptionAttribute
	{
		private bool loaded;

		public override string Description
		{
			get
			{
				if (!loaded)
				{
					loaded = true;
					base.DescriptionValue = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetUncustomizedString(null, base.Description);
				}
				return base.Description;
			}
		}

		public LocalizedDescriptionAttribute(string description)
			: base(description)
		{
		}
	}
	[AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.Class, AllowMultiple = true)]
	internal sealed class LocalizedUltraPropertyPageAttribute : UltraPropertyPageAttribute
	{
		public override string TabCaption => Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetUncustomizedString(null, base.TabCaption);

		public LocalizedUltraPropertyPageAttribute(string tabCaption, Type ultraPropControlType)
			: base(tabCaption, ultraPropControlType)
		{
		}

		public LocalizedUltraPropertyPageAttribute(string tabCaption, string ultraPropControlType)
			: base(tabCaption, ultraPropControlType)
		{
		}
	}

namespace Infragistics.Win.UIAutomation
{
	[SecurityPermission(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Flags = SecurityPermissionFlag.UnmanagedCode)]
	[SecurityPermission(SecurityAction.InheritanceDemand, Flags = SecurityPermissionFlag.UnmanagedCode)]
	internal class UiaProviderControlNativeWindow : NativeWindow
	{
		private const int WM_GETOBJECT = 61;

		private IProvideUIAutomation uiaProvider;

		private Control Control => uiaProvider as Control;

		public UiaProviderControlNativeWindow(IProvideUIAutomation uiaProvider)
		{
			this.uiaProvider = uiaProvider;
			Control control = Control;
			if (control.IsHandleCreated)
			{
				AssignHandle(control.Handle);
			}
			else
			{
				control.HandleCreated += control_HandleCreated;
			}
		}

		protected override void WndProc(ref Message msg)
		{
			int msg2 = msg.Msg;
			if (msg2 == 61 && msg.LParam.ToInt64() == -25 && uiaProvider.UIAutomationProvider != null)
			{
				Utilities.VerifyAutomationCache();
				msg.Result = AutomationInteropProvider.ReturnRawElementProvider(msg.HWnd, msg.WParam, msg.LParam, uiaProvider.UIAutomationProvider);
			}
			else
			{
				base.WndProc(ref msg);
			}
		}

		private void control_HandleCreated(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			Control.HandleCreated -= control_HandleCreated;
			AssignHandle(Control.Handle);
		}
	}

	public class WinTabRole : UltraControlRole
	{
		private StyleUtils.ComponentType componentType;

		private string[] cachedRoleNames;

		internal StyleUtils.ComponentType ComponentType => componentType;

		internal WinTabRole(UltraControlBase ultraCtrl, StyleUtils.ComponentType componentType)
			: base(ultraCtrl, StyleUtils.GetComponentName(componentType))
		{
			this.componentType = componentType;
			((ComponentRole)this).PropertiesCacheCount = 9;
		}

		protected override string[] GetRoleNames()
		{
			if (cachedRoleNames == null)
			{
				cachedRoleNames = StyleUtils.GetSortedRoleNamesForCaching();
			}
			return cachedRoleNames;
		}

		protected override void OnStyleChanged()
		{
			((UltraControlRole)this).OnStyleChanged();
			if (((ComponentRole)this).Component is UltraTabControlBase ultraTabControlBase)
			{
				ultraTabControlBase.OnAppStyleChanged();
			}
		}
	}
	public class WinTabAssemblyStyleInfo : AssemblyStyleInfo
	{
		public override UIRoleDefinition[] GetRoles()
		{
			return StyleUtils.GetRoleDefinitions(StyleUtils.ComponentType.All);
		}

		private ComponentRoleDefinition CreateComponentRoleDef(StyleUtils.ComponentType componentType)
		{
			//IL_000c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0012: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_005b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			ComponentRoleDefinition val = new ComponentRoleDefinition(StyleUtils.GetComponentName(componentType), StyleUtils.GetComponentType(componentType));
			Type componentEditorType = StyleUtils.GetComponentEditorType(componentType);
			if (null != componentEditorType)
			{
				val.EditorType = componentEditorType;
			}
			val.RelatedRoles = StyleUtils.GetRoleNames(componentType);
			val.CustomProperties = StyleUtils.GetCustomProperties(componentType);
			val.UsesScrollbars = StyleUtils.UseScrollbars(componentType);
			val.UsesToolTips = StyleUtils.UseToolTips(componentType);
			val.SupportedProperties = StyleUtils.GetSupportedProperties(componentType);
			val.SharedObjects = StyleUtils.GetSharedObjects(componentType);
			return val;
		}

		public override ComponentRoleDefinition[] GetComponents()
		{
			ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
			for (int num = 1; num <= 2; num <<= 1)
			{
				arrayList.Add(CreateComponentRoleDef((StyleUtils.ComponentType)num));
			}
			return (ComponentRoleDefinition[])arrayList.ToArray(typeof(ComponentRoleDefinition));
		}

			}
			return obj;
		}

		}

		{

			if (componentRole != null)

			return ResolveValueHelper(controlVal, defVal, resolveVal);
		}

		private static object ResolveValueHelper(object val, object defVal, object resolveVal)
		{
			if (defVal != resolveVal && object.Equals(val, defVal))
			{
				return resolveVal;
			}
			return val;
		}

		internal static object CachePropertyValue(UltraControlBase ultraCtrl, CachedProperty prop, object controlVal, object defVal, object resolveVal)
		{
			ComponentRole componentRole = GetComponentRole(ultraCtrl);
			if (componentRole != null)
			{
				string customPropertyName = GetCustomPropertyName(prop);
				object obj = null;
				if (componentRole.HasProperty(customPropertyName))
				{
					obj = componentRole.GetProperty(customPropertyName);
				}
				return componentRole.CachePropertyValue(obj, controlVal, defVal, resolveVal, (int)prop);
			}
			return ResolveValueHelper(controlVal, defVal, resolveVal);
		}

		private static string[] GetCustomPropertyNames()
		{
			if (cachedCustomPropertyNames != null)
			{
				return cachedCustomPropertyNames;
			}
			object[] customPropertiesInfo = GetCustomPropertiesInfo();
			string[] array = new string[customPropertiesInfo.Length / 7];
			for (int i = 0; i < customPropertiesInfo.Length; i += 7)
			{
				array[i / 7] = (string)customPropertiesInfo[i];
			}
			return cachedCustomPropertyNames = array;
		}

		internal static string GetCustomPropertyName(CachedProperty prop)
		{
			string[] customPropertyNames = GetCustomPropertyNames();
			return customPropertyNames[(int)prop];
		}

		internal static AppStyleProperty[] GetCustomProperties(ComponentType component)
		{
			//IL_0048: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004f: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0054: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0061: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0068: Expected O, but got Unknown
			object[] customPropertiesInfo = GetCustomPropertiesInfo();
			ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
			for (int i = 0; i < customPropertiesInfo.Length; i += 7)
			{
				ComponentType componentType = (ComponentType)customPropertiesInfo[5 + i];
				if ((component & componentType) != ComponentType.None)
				{
					string text = (string)customPropertiesInfo[i];
					Type type = (Type)customPropertiesInfo[1 + i];
					object defaultValue = customPropertiesInfo[2 + i];
					AppStylePropertyConverter val = (AppStylePropertyConverter)customPropertiesInfo[3 + i];
					ValueConstraint constraint = (ValueConstraint)customPropertiesInfo[4 + i];
					AppStyleProperty val2 = new AppStyleProperty(text, type, val);
					val2.DefaultValue = defaultValue;
					val2.Constraint = constraint;
					string text2 = (string)customPropertiesInfo[6 + i];
					if (text2 != null && text2.Length > 0)
					{
						val2.Description = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(text2);
					}
					arrayList.Add(val2);
				}
			}
			return (AppStyleProperty[])arrayList.ToArray(typeof(AppStyleProperty));
		}

		internal static void ResolveAppearance(UIRole role, RoleState state, ref AppearanceData appData, ref AppearancePropFlags flags)
		{
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (role != null)
			{
				role.ResolveAppearance(ref appData, ref flags, state);
			}
		}

		internal static void ResolveAppearance(UltraControlBase ultraCtrl, AppearanceBase controlAppearance, Role eRole, RoleState state, ref AppearanceData appData, ref AppearancePropFlags flags)
		{
			//IL_000c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			ResolutionOrderInfo resOrder;
			UIRole role = GetRole(ultraCtrl, eRole, out resOrder);
			ResolveAppearance(controlAppearance, role, state, resOrder, ref appData, ref flags);
		}

		internal static void ResolveAppearance(AppearanceBase controlAppearance, UIRole role, RoleState state, ResolutionOrderInfo order, ref AppearanceData appData, ref AppearancePropFlags flags)
		{
			//IL_0011: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (((ResolutionOrderInfo)(ref order)).UseStyleBefore && role != null)
			{
				role.ResolveAppearance(ref appData, ref flags, state);
			}
			if (((ResolutionOrderInfo)(ref order)).UseControlInfo && controlAppearance != null)
			{
				controlAppearance.MergeData(ref appData, ref flags);
			}
			if (((ResolutionOrderInfo)(ref order)).UseStyleAfter && role != null)
			{
				role.ResolveAppearance(ref appData, ref flags, state);
			}
		}

		internal static bool HasAppearance(UIRole role, RoleState state)
		{
			//IL_0004: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (role != null)
			{
				return role.HasAppearance(state);
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal static int GetIndex(ComponentType componentType)
		{
			int i;
			for (i = 0; i < 32; i++)
			{
				if ((1 & ((int)componentType >> i)) != 0)
				{
					return i;
				}
			}
			return i;
		}

		}

		internal static string GetRoleName(Role eRole)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < ROLES.Length; i += 6)
			{
				if (eRole == (Role)ROLES[i])
				{
					return (string)ROLES[i + 1];
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		internal static string[] Combine(string[] arr1, string[] arr2)
		{
			if (arr1 == null || arr1.Length == 0)
			{
				return arr2;
			}
			if (arr2 == null || arr2.Length == 0)
			{
				return arr1;
			}
			string[] array = new string[arr1.Length + arr2.Length];
			int num = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < arr1.Length; i++)
			{
				array[num++] = arr1[i];
			}
			for (int j = 0; j < arr2.Length; j++)
			{
				array[num++] = arr2[j];
			}
			return array;
		}

		internal static string[] GetRoleNames(Role[] roles)
		{
			string[] array = new string[roles.Length];
			for (int i = 0; i < roles.Length; i++)
			{
				array[i] = GetRoleName(roles[i]);
			}
			return array;
		}

		internal static string[] GetSharedObjects(ComponentType componentType)
		{
			DebugComponents(componentType);
			return (string[])COMPONENTS[GetIndex(componentType) * 9 + 8];
		}

		internal static string[] GetSortedRoleNamesForCaching()
		{
			string[] array = new string[ROLES.Length / 6];
			for (int i = 0; i < ROLES.Length; i += 6)
			{
				Role role = (Role)ROLES[i];
				string text = (string)ROLES[i + 1];
				int num = (int)role;
				if (num >= 0 && num < array.Length && array[num] == null)
				{
					array[num] = text;
				}
			}
			return array;
		}

		internal static void GetRolesHelper(out string[] roleNames, out UIRoleDefinition[] roleDefs, ComponentType matchComponent, bool getRoleNames, bool getRoleDefs)
		{
			//IL_008f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0094: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_009f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00a4: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00ad: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00af: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00b1: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00b8: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ArrayList arrayList = (getRoleDefs ? new ArrayList() : null);
			ArrayList arrayList2 = (getRoleNames ? new ArrayList() : null);
			for (int i = 0; i < ROLES.Length; i += 6)
			{
				ComponentType componentType = (ComponentType)ROLES[i + 3];
				if ((matchComponent & componentType) != ComponentType.None)
				{
					_ = (Role)ROLES[i];
					string text = (string)ROLES[i + 1];
					object obj = ROLES[i + 2];
					string text2 = ((obj is Role) ? GetRoleName((Role)obj) : ((string)ROLES[i + 2]));
					RoleState val = (RoleState)ROLES[i + 5];
					SupportedRoleProperties val2 = (SupportedRoleProperties)ROLES[i + 4];
					if (arrayList != null)
					{
						UIRoleDefinition value = new UIRoleDefinition(text, text2, val, val2);
						arrayList.Add(value);
					}
					arrayList2?.Add(text);
				}
			}
			roleDefs = ((arrayList != null) ? ((UIRoleDefinition[])arrayList.ToArray(typeof(UIRoleDefinition))) : null);
			roleNames = ((arrayList2 != null) ? ((string[])arrayList2.ToArray(typeof(string))) : null);
		}

		internal static bool IsHottracking(Control ctrl)
		{
			if (ctrl == null || !ctrl.Enabled)
			{
				return false;
			}
			Point pt = ctrl.PointToClient(Control.MousePosition);
			return ctrl.ClientRectangle.Contains(pt);
		}

		internal static bool HasFontData(UltraControlBase ultraCtrl, AppearanceBase controlAppearance, Role eRole, RoleState state)
		{
			//IL_0002: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0012: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			AppearanceData appData = default(AppearanceData);
			AppearancePropFlags flags = (AppearancePropFlags)65011720;
			ResolveAppearance(ultraCtrl, controlAppearance, eRole, state, ref appData, ref flags);
			if (((AppearanceData)(ref appData)).HasFontData)
			{
				return ((AppearanceData)(ref appData)).HasPropertyBeenSet((AppearancePropFlags)65011720);
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal static Role GetTabItemRole(UltraTabControlBase tabCtrl)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0007: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0031: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			TabOrientation tabOrientation = tabCtrl.TabOrientation;
			switch ((int)tabOrientation)
			{
			default:
				return Role.TabItemHorizontalTop;
			case 3:
			case 4:
				return Role.TabItemHorizontalBottom;
			case 5:
			case 6:
				return Role.TabItemVerticalLeft;
			case 7:
			case 8:
				return Role.TabItemVerticalRight;
			}
		}

		internal static Role GetItemAreaRole(UltraTabControlBase tabCtrl)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0007: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0031: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			TabOrientation tabOrientation = tabCtrl.TabOrientation;
			switch ((int)tabOrientation)
			{
			default:
				return Role.ItemAreaHorizontalTop;
			case 3:
			case 4:
				return Role.ItemAreaHorizontalBottom;
			case 5:
			case 6:
				return Role.ItemAreaVerticalLeft;
			case 7:
			case 8:
				return Role.ItemAreaVerticalRight;
			}
		}

		internal static ViewStyle ViewStyleToViewStyle(ViewStyle viewStyle)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0002: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0004: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0026: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			switch (viewStyle - 1)
			{
			case 3:
				return ViewStyle.Office2003;
			case 0:
				return ViewStyle.Standard;
			case 4:
				return ViewStyle.VisualStudio2005;
			case 5:
			case 6:
				return ViewStyle.Office2007;
			default:
				return ViewStyle.Default;
			}
		}
	}

namespace Infragistics.Win.UltraWinTabControl.Serialization
{
	public class Binder : Binder
	{
		public override Type BindToType(string assemblyName, string typeName)
		{
			if (assemblyName == null || !assemblyName.StartsWith("Infragistics.Win.UltraWinTabControl"))
			{
				return ((Binder)this).BindToType(assemblyName, typeName);
			}
			Type type = null;
			try
			{
				assemblyName = typeof(UltraTabControlBase).Assembly.FullName;
			}
			catch
			{
				assemblyName = "Infragistics4.Win.UltraWinTabControl.v15.1";
				if (this == null)
				{
					throw;
				}
			}
			type = Type.GetType($"{typeName}, {assemblyName}");
			if (type == null)
			{
				return ((Binder)this).BindToType(assemblyName, typeName);
			}
			return type;
		}
	}

		{
			this.tabControl = tabControl;
			this.designerHost = designerHost;
			InitializeComponent();
			lblTabControl.Text = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("ConvertTabControlDlgLabelTabControl");
			grpOptions.Text = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("ConvertTabControlDlgOptions");

			cmdOK.Text = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("ConvertTabControlDlgOk");
			Text = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("ConvertTabControlDlgTitle");
			try
			{
				Text = string.Format(Text, ((Control)(object)this.tabControl).Name);
			}
			catch (FormatException)
			{
			}
			Control control = ((Control)(object)this.tabControl).Parent;
			if (control != null)
			{
				while (control.Parent != null)
				{
					control = control.Parent;
				}
				PopulateTabControlCombo(control);
				if (cmbTabControl.Items.Count > 0)
				{
					cmbTabControl.SelectedIndex = 0;
					cmdOK.Enabled = true;
				}
				else
				{
					cmdOK.Enabled = false;
				}
			}
		}

		private void chkReparent_CheckedChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{

		}

		private void ConvertTabPage(TabControl inboxTabControl, TabPage tabPage, UltraTab[] existingTabs)
		{
			UltraTab ultraTab = null;
			for (int i = 0; i < existingTabs.Length; i++)
			{
				if (existingTabs[i].Key == tabPage.Text)
				{
					ultraTab = existingTabs[i];
					break;
				}
				if ((existingTabs[i].Key == null || existingTabs[i].Key.Length < 1) && existingTabs[i].Text == tabPage.Text)
				{
					ultraTab = existingTabs[i];
					break;
				}
			}
			if (ultraTab == null)
			{
				ultraTab = ((!chkSetKeyValues.Checked) ? tabControl.Tabs.Add() : tabControl.Tabs.Add(tabPage.Text));
				ultraTab.Text = tabPage.Text;
				_ = ((Control)(object)ultraTab.TabPage).Size;
			}
			if (tabPage.Text != null && tabPage.Text.Length > 0 && ultraTab.Text.Length < 1)
			{
				ultraTab.Text = tabPage.Text;
			}
			if (tabPage.ToolTipText != null && tabPage.ToolTipText.Length > 0 && ultraTab.ToolTipText.Length < 1)
			{
				ultraTab.ToolTipText = tabPage.ToolTipText;
			}
			if (tabPage.Tag != null && ultraTab.Tag == null)
			{
				ultraTab.Tag = tabPage.Tag;
			}
			if (inboxTabControl.ImageList != null && tabPage.ImageIndex >= 0 && tabPage.ImageIndex < inboxTabControl.ImageList.Images.Count && ultraTab.Appearance.Image == null)
			{
				ultraTab.Appearance.Image = tabPage.ImageIndex;
			}
			if (tabPage.BackgroundImage != null && ultraTab.Appearance.ImageBackground == null)
			{
				ultraTab.Appearance.ImageBackground = tabPage.BackgroundImage;
			}
			if (chkReparent.Checked)
			{
				while (tabPage.Controls.Count > 0)
				{
					tabPage.Controls[0].Parent = (Control)(object)ultraTab.TabPage;
				}
			}
		}

		private void DoConversion()
		{
			if (!(cmbTabControl.SelectedItem is TabControl tabControl))
			{
				return;
			}
			DesignerTransaction designerTransaction = this.tabControl.CreateTransaction(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("ConvertTabControlTransaction"));
			try
			{
				IComponentChangeService componentChangeService = null;
				ISite site = ((Component)(object)this.tabControl).Site;
				componentChangeService = site.GetService(typeof(IComponentChangeService)) as IComponentChangeService;
				if (componentChangeService != null)
				{
					componentChangeService.OnComponentChanging(this.tabControl, null);
					componentChangeService.OnComponentChanged(this.tabControl, null, null, null);
					componentChangeService.OnComponentChanging(tabControl, null);
					componentChangeService.OnComponentChanged(tabControl, null, null, null);
				}
				UltraTab[] array = new UltraTab[this.tabControl.Tabs.Count];
				if (this.tabControl.Tabs.Count > 0)
				{
					this.tabControl.Tabs.CopyTo(array, 0);
				}
				this.tabControl.ConvertingInboxTab = true;
				((Control)(object)this.tabControl).Size = tabControl.Size;
				this.tabControl.ImageList = tabControl.ImageList;
				((Control)(object)this.tabControl).Enabled = tabControl.Enabled;
				((Control)(object)this.tabControl).Visible = tabControl.Visible;
				this.tabControl.UseHotTracking = (DefaultableBoolean)(tabControl.HotTrack ? 1 : 2);
				if (chkDeleteTabControl.Checked)
				{
					((Control)(object)this.tabControl).Dock = tabControl.Dock;
					((Control)(object)this.tabControl).Anchor = tabControl.Anchor;
				}
				if (tabControl.Tag != null && ((Control)(object)this.tabControl).Tag == null)
				{
					((Control)(object)this.tabControl).Tag = tabControl.Tag;
				}
				switch (tabControl.Appearance)
				{
				case TabAppearance.Normal:
					this.tabControl.Style = UltraTabControlStyle.PropertyPage;
					break;
				case TabAppearance.FlatButtons:
					this.tabControl.Style = UltraTabControlStyle.StateButtons;
					this.tabControl.TabButtonStyle = (UIElementButtonStyle)3;
					break;
				case TabAppearance.Buttons:
					this.tabControl.Style = UltraTabControlStyle.StateButtons;
					break;
				}
				switch (tabControl.Alignment)
				{
				case TabAlignment.Top:
					this.tabControl.TabOrientation = (TabOrientation)1;
					break;
				case TabAlignment.Bottom:
					this.tabControl.TabOrientation = (TabOrientation)3;
					break;
				case TabAlignment.Left:
					this.tabControl.TabOrientation = (TabOrientation)5;
					break;
				case TabAlignment.Right:
					this.tabControl.TabOrientation = (TabOrientation)7;
					break;
				}
				switch (tabControl.SizeMode)
				{
				case TabSizeMode.FillToRight:
					if (tabControl.Multiline)
					{
						this.tabControl.TabLayoutStyle = (TabLayoutStyle)8;
					}
					else
					{
						this.tabControl.TabLayoutStyle = (TabLayoutStyle)2;
					}
					break;
				case TabSizeMode.Fixed:
					if (tabControl.Multiline)
					{
						this.tabControl.TabLayoutStyle = (TabLayoutStyle)7;
					}
					else
					{
						this.tabControl.TabLayoutStyle = (TabLayoutStyle)1;
					}
					break;
				case TabSizeMode.Normal:
					if (tabControl.Multiline)
					{
						this.tabControl.TabLayoutStyle = (TabLayoutStyle)6;
					}
					else
					{
						this.tabControl.TabLayoutStyle = (TabLayoutStyle)0;
					}
					break;
				}
				if (tabControl.TabPages.Count > 0)
				{
					this.tabControl.TabPageSize = tabControl.TabPages[0].Size;
					foreach (TabPage tabPage in tabControl.TabPages)
					{
						if (componentChangeService != null)
						{
							componentChangeService.OnComponentChanging(tabPage, null);
							componentChangeService.OnComponentChanged(tabPage, null, null, null);
						}
						ConvertTabPage(tabControl, tabPage, array);
						if (chkDeleteTabControl.Checked)
						{
							designerHost.DestroyComponent(tabPage);
						}
					}
				}
				if (chkDeleteTabControl.Checked)
				{
					_ = tabControl.Name;
					((Control)(object)this.tabControl).Parent = tabControl.Parent;
					((Control)(object)this.tabControl).Location = tabControl.Location;
					((Control)(object)this.tabControl).Dock = tabControl.Dock;
					((Control)(object)this.tabControl).Anchor = tabControl.Anchor;
					designerHost.DestroyComponent(tabControl);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				if (designerTransaction != null)
				{
					designerTransaction.Cancel();
					designerTransaction = null;
				}
				if (!this.tabControl.IsDesignMode)
				{
					throw;
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				this.tabControl.ConvertingInboxTab = false;
				if (this.tabControl.Tabs.Count > 0)
				{
					this.tabControl.SelectedTab = this.tabControl.Tabs[0];
				}
				else
				{
					this.tabControl.ShowSharedControlsPage();
				}
				designerTransaction?.Commit();
			}
		}

		private void PopulateTabControlCombo(Control container)
		{
			foreach (Control control in container.Controls)
			{
				if (control is TabControl)
				{
					cmbTabControl.Items.Add(control);
				}
				PopulateTabControlCombo(control);
			}
		}

		private void ConvertTabControlDialog_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
		}
	}
}

	public class UltraTabControlEventManager : EventManagerBase
	{
		private UltraTabControlBase tabControl;

		private static int[] beforeEventIndexes;

		private static int[] afterEventIndexes;

		internal UltraTabControlBase TabControl => tabControl;

		private int[] AfterEventIndexes
		{
			get
			{
				if (afterEventIndexes == null)
				{
					afterEventIndexes = new int[8] { 13, 3, 4, 6, 7, 9, 11, 15 };
				}
				return afterEventIndexes;
			}
		}

		private int[] BeforeEventIndexes
		{
			get
			{
				if (beforeEventIndexes == null)
				{
					beforeEventIndexes = new int[6] { 12, 2, 5, 8, 10, 14 };
				}
				return beforeEventIndexes;
			}
		}

		public UltraTabControlEventManager(UltraTabControlBase tabControl)
			: base(18)
		{
			this.tabControl = tabControl;
		}

		public bool InProgress(UltraTabControlEventId eventid)
		{
			return InProgress((int)eventid);
		}

		public bool IsEnabled(UltraTabControlEventId eventid)
		{
			return IsEnabled((int)eventid);
		}

		public void SetEnabled(UltraTabControlEventId eventid, bool enabled)
		{
			SetEnabled((int)eventid, enabled);
		}

		public bool IsEnabled(UltraTabControlEventGroup group)
		{
			return group switch
			{
				UltraTabControlEventGroup.AllEvents => base.AllEventsEnabled, 
				UltraTabControlEventGroup.BeforeEvents => IsEnabled(BeforeEventIndexes), 
				UltraTabControlEventGroup.AfterEvents => IsEnabled(AfterEventIndexes), 
				_ => false, 
			};
		}

		public void SetEnabled(UltraTabControlEventGroup group, bool enabled)
		{
			switch (group)
			{
			case UltraTabControlEventGroup.AllEvents:
				base.AllEventsEnabled = enabled;
				break;
			case UltraTabControlEventGroup.BeforeEvents:
				SetEnabled(BeforeEventIndexes, enabled);
				break;
			case UltraTabControlEventGroup.AfterEvents:
				SetEnabled(AfterEventIndexes, enabled);
				break;
			}
		}

		internal void IncrementInProgress(UltraTabControlEventId eventid)
		{
			IncrementInProgress((int)eventid);
		}

		internal void DecrementInProgress(UltraTabControlEventId eventid)
		{
			DecrementInProgress((int)eventid);
		}

		internal bool CanFireEvent(UltraTabControlEventId eventid)
		{
			return IsEnabled(eventid);
		}
	}

	internal class ExcludeSharedControlsDialog : Form
	{
		private UltraTab tabBeingEdited;

		private UltraTabControlBase tabControl;

		private CheckedListBox chkLstSharedControls;

		private ComboBox cmbTabs;

		private Label lblTab;

		private Label lblSharedControls;

		private Button cmdCancel;

		private Button cmdOK;

		private Button cmdApply;

		private Container components;

		public ExcludeSharedControlsDialog(UltraTabControlBase tabControl)
		{
			this.tabControl = tabControl;
			tabBeingEdited = null;
			InitializeComponent();
			lblTab.Text = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("ExcludeSharedControlsDlgLabelTab");
			lblSharedControls.Text = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("ExcludeSharedControlsDlgLabelControls");
			cmdApply.Text = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("ExcludeSharedControlsDlgApply");
			cmdCancel.Text = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("ExcludeSharedControlsDlgCancel");
			cmdOK.Text = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("ExcludeSharedControlsDlgOk");
			Text = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("ExcludeSharedControlsDlgTitle");
			try
			{
				Text = string.Format(Text, ((Control)(object)this.tabControl).Name);
			}
			catch (FormatException)
			{
			}
			Control[] array = new Control[this.tabControl.SharedControls.Count];
			this.tabControl.SharedControls.CopyTo(array, 0);
			chkLstSharedControls.Items.AddRange(array);
			UltraTab[] array2 = new UltraTab[this.tabControl.Tabs.Count];
			this.tabControl.Tabs.CopyTo(array2, 0);
			cmbTabs.Items.AddRange(array2);
			if (this.tabControl.SelectedTab != null)
			{
				cmbTabs.SelectedItem = this.tabControl.SelectedTab;
			}
			else
			{
				cmbTabs.SelectedItem = this.tabControl.VisibleTabs.FirstSelectableTab;
			}
		}

		protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing && components != null)
			{
				components.Dispose();
			}
			base.Dispose(disposing);
		}

		private void cmdApply_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			ApplySettings();
		}

		private void chkLstSharedControls_ItemCheck(object sender, ItemCheckEventArgs e)
		{
			cmdApply.Enabled = true;
		}

		private void ApplySettings()
		{
			if (tabBeingEdited != null)
			{
				DesignerTransaction designerTransaction = tabControl.CreateTransaction(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("ExcludeSharedControlsTransaction"));
				tabControl.OnPropertiesChanged();
				UltraTabPageControl tabPage = tabBeingEdited.TabPage;
				tabPage?.OnPropertiesChanged();
				if (tabBeingEdited.HasExcludedSharedControls)
				{
					tabBeingEdited.ExcludedSharedControls.Clear();
				}
				for (int i = 0; i < chkLstSharedControls.Items.Count; i++)
				{
					Control control = chkLstSharedControls.Items[i] as Control;
					if (chkLstSharedControls.GetItemChecked(i))
					{
						tabBeingEdited.ExcludedSharedControls.Add(control);
					}
				}
				tabControl.OnPropertiesChanged();
				tabPage?.OnPropertiesChanged();
				designerTransaction?.Commit();
			}
			chkLstSharedControls.ClearSelected();
			cmdApply.Enabled = false;
		}

		private void cmdCancel_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			base.DialogResult = DialogResult.Cancel;
			Close();
		}

		private void cmdOK_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			ApplySettings();
			base.DialogResult = DialogResult.OK;
			Close();
		}

		private void cmbTabs_SelectedIndexChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (tabBeingEdited != null)
			{
				ApplySettings();
			}
			tabBeingEdited = cmbTabs.SelectedItem as UltraTab;
			if (tabBeingEdited != null)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < chkLstSharedControls.Items.Count; i++)
				{
					Control control = chkLstSharedControls.Items[i] as Control;
					if (tabBeingEdited.HasExcludedSharedControls && tabBeingEdited.ExcludedSharedControls.Contains(control))
					{
						chkLstSharedControls.SetItemCheckState(i, CheckState.Checked);
					}
					else
					{
						chkLstSharedControls.SetItemCheckState(i, CheckState.Unchecked);
					}
				}
			}
			chkLstSharedControls.ClearSelected();
			cmdApply.Enabled = false;
		}
	}
}

		{
			internal int left;

			internal int top;

			internal int right;

			internal int bottom;

			internal Rectangle Rect => new Rectangle(left, top, right - left, bottom - top);

			internal RECT(int left, int top, int right, int bottom)
			{
				this.left = left;
				this.top = top;
				this.right = right;
				this.bottom = bottom;
			}

			internal static RECT FromXYWH(int x, int y, int width, int height)
			{
				return new RECT(x, y, x + width, y + height);
			}

			internal static RECT FromRectangle(Rectangle rect)
			{
				return new RECT(rect.Left, rect.Top, rect.Right, rect.Bottom);
			}

		internal static void CloseForm(Form form)
		{
			if (form != null && form.IsHandleCreated && !form.Disposing && !form.IsDisposed)
			{
				try
				{
					SecurityPermission securityPermission = new SecurityPermission(SecurityPermissionFlag.UnmanagedCode);
					securityPermission.Demand();
				}
				catch (SecurityException)
				{
					form.Close();
					return;
				}
				SendMessageApi(form.Handle, 274, new IntPtr(61536), IntPtr.Zero);
			}
		}

		internal static IntPtr CallNextHookExApi(IntPtr hook, int nCode, IntPtr wparam, IntPtr lparam)

			return CallNextHookEx(hook, nCode, wparam, lparam);
		}

	public class NotePageEarMarkUIElement : ImageUIElement
	{
		private UltraTabControlBase tabControl;

		private bool previous;

		public bool Previous => previous;

		public UltraTabControlBase TabControl => tabControl;

		public NotePageEarMarkUIElement(UIElement parent, UltraTabControlBase tabControl, bool previous)
			: base(parent, previous ? tabControl.NotePageEarMarkTopLeft : tabControl.NotePageEarMarkTopRight)
		{
			this.tabControl = tabControl;
			this.previous = previous;
		}

		protected override bool OnMouseDown(MouseEventArgs e, bool adjustableArea, ref UIElement captureMouseForElement)
		{
			if ((e.Button & MouseButtons.Left) == MouseButtons.Left)
			{
				if (((Control)(object)tabControl).IsDisposed || ((Control)(object)tabControl).Disposing)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (previous && tabControl.IsDesignMode && tabControl.SelectedTab == tabControl.VisibleTabs.FirstSelectableTab)
				{
					TabControl.ShowSharedControlsPage();
					return true;
				}
				tabControl.PerformAction(previous ? UltraTabControlAction.SelectPreviousTab : UltraTabControlAction.SelectNextTab);
				return true;
			}
			return ((UIElement)this).OnMouseDown(e, adjustableArea, ref captureMouseForElement);
		}

		protected override void InitAppearance(ref AppearanceData appearance, ref AppearancePropFlags requestedProps)
		{
			if (((ulong)requestedProps & 0x40uL) != 0 && !((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).HasPropertyBeenSet((AppearancePropFlags)64))
			{
				((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).ImageHAlign = (HAlign)(previous ? 1 : 3);
				requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)((ulong)requestedProps & 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFBFuL);
			}
			if (((ulong)requestedProps & 0x80uL) != 0 && !((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).HasPropertyBeenSet((AppearancePropFlags)128))
			{
				((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).ImageVAlign = (VAlign)1;
				requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)((ulong)requestedProps & 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFF7FuL);
			}
			((UIElement)this).InitAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
		}
	}

namespace Infragistics.Win.UltraWinTabControl.Design
{
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class SelectedTabUITypeEditor : UITypeEditor
	{
		private IWindowsFormsEditorService edSvc;

		private UltraTabControlBase tabControl;

		private ITypeDescriptorContext context;

		[PermissionSet(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Name = "linkcheck", Unrestricted = true)]
		public override object EditValue(ITypeDescriptorContext context, IServiceProvider provider, object value)
		{
			if (context != null && context.Instance != null && provider != null)
			{
				edSvc = (IWindowsFormsEditorService)provider.GetService(typeof(IWindowsFormsEditorService));
				if (edSvc != null)
				{
					this.context = context;
					tabControl = context.Instance as UltraTabControlBase;
					if (tabControl == null)
					{
						return value;
					}
					DropDownListBox dropDownListBox = CreateListBox();
					edSvc.DropDownControl(dropDownListBox);
					dropDownListBox.Dispose();
				}
			}
			return value;
		}

		[PermissionSet(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Name = "linkcheck", Unrestricted = true)]
		public override UITypeEditorEditStyle GetEditStyle(ITypeDescriptorContext context)
		{
			if (context != null && context.Instance is UltraTabControlBase)
			{
				UltraTabControlBase ultraTabControlBase = context.Instance as UltraTabControlBase;
				if (!ultraTabControlBase.IsBound)
				{
					return UITypeEditorEditStyle.DropDown;
				}
			}
			return UITypeEditorEditStyle.None;
		}

		private DropDownListBox CreateListBox()
		{
			DropDownListBox dropDownListBox = null;
			UltraTab[] array = new UltraTab[tabControl.Tabs.Count];
			tabControl.Tabs.CopyTo(array, 0);
			UltraTab defaultItem = context.PropertyDescriptor.GetValue(tabControl) as UltraTab;
			dropDownListBox = new DropDownListBox(array, defaultItem, sorted: false);
			dropDownListBox.Click += OnListClick;
			return dropDownListBox;
		}

		private void OnListClick(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is DropDownListBox { SelectedItem: not null } dropDownListBox && tabControl != null)
			{
				if (context != null && context.Instance is UltraTabControlBase && context.PropertyDescriptor.Name == "SelectedTab")
				{
					tabControl.SelectedTab = dropDownListBox.SelectedItem as UltraTab;
				}
				else
				{
					context.PropertyDescriptor.SetValue(tabControl, dropDownListBox.SelectedItem);
				}
			}
			if (edSvc != null)
			{
				edSvc.CloseDropDown();
			}
		}
	}
}
namespace Infragistics.Win.UltraWinTabControl

	[ListBindable(false)]
	public class SharedControlsCollection : DisposableObjectCollectionBase
	{
		public class SharedControlsEnumerator : DisposableObjectEnumeratorBase
		{
			public Control Current => (Control)((IEnumerator)this).Current;

			public SharedControlsEnumerator(SharedControlsCollection sharedControls)
				: base(sharedControls)
			{
			}
		}

		private class ZorderSortComparer : IComparer
		{
			private SharedControlsCollection sharedControls;

			internal ZorderSortComparer(SharedControlsCollection sharedControls)
			{
				this.sharedControls = sharedControls;
			}

			public int Compare(object x, object y)
			{
				Control control = x as Control;
				Control control2 = y as Control;
				if (control == control2)
				{
					return 0;
				}
				Control parent = control.Parent;
				Control parent2 = control2.Parent;
				if (parent == parent2)
				{
					int num = parent.Controls.IndexOf(control);
					int num2 = parent2.Controls.IndexOf(control2);
					if (num < num2)
					{
						return -1;
					}
					if (num > num2)
					{
						return 1;
					}
				}
				int num3 = sharedControls.IndexOf(control);
				int num4 = sharedControls.IndexOf(control2);
				if (num3 < num4)
				{
					return -1;
				}
				if (num3 > num4)
				{
					return 1;
				}
				return 0;
			}
		}

		private UltraTabControlBase tabControl;

		private UltraTab tab;

		private EventHandler controlDisposedHandler;

		private EventHandler controlParentChangedHandler;

		protected override int InitialCapacity => 10;

		public override bool IsReadOnly => false;

		public Control this[int index] => (Control)base.GetItem(index);

		private EventHandler ControlDisposedHandler
		{
			get
			{
				if (controlDisposedHandler == null)
				{
					controlDisposedHandler = OnControlDisposed;
				}
				return controlDisposedHandler;
			}
		}

		private EventHandler ControlParentChangedHandler
		{
			get
			{
				if (controlParentChangedHandler == null)
				{
					controlParentChangedHandler = OnControlParentChanged;
				}
				return controlParentChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		internal SharedControlsCollection(UltraTabControlBase tabControl, UltraTab tab)
		{
			this.tabControl = tabControl;
			this.tab = tab;
		}

		protected override void OnDispose()
		{
			Clear();
		}

		public int Add(Control control)
		{
			int num = IndexOf(control);
			if (num < 0)
			{
				num = List.Add(control);
				OnControlAdded(control);
				control.Disposed += ControlDisposedHandler;
				control.ParentChanged += ControlParentChangedHandler;
			}
			return num;
		}

		public void AddRange(Control[] controls)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < controls.Length; i++)
			{
				if (controls[i] != null)
				{
					OnControlAdded(controls[i]);
					controls[i].Disposed += ControlDisposedHandler;
					controls[i].ParentChanged += ControlParentChangedHandler;
				}
			}
			List.AddRange(controls);
		}

		public void Clear()
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < List.Count; i++)
			{
				this[i].Disposed -= ControlDisposedHandler;
				this[i].ParentChanged -= ControlParentChangedHandler;
				OnControlRemoved(this[i]);
			}
			List.Clear();
		}

		public bool Contains(Control control)
		{
			return IndexOf(control) >= 0;
		}

		public void CopyTo(Control[] array, int arrayIndex)
		{
			base.CopyTo(array, arrayIndex);
		}

		public SharedControlsEnumerator GetEnumerator()
		{
			return new SharedControlsEnumerator(this);
		}

		public void Remove(Control control)
		{
			int num = IndexOf(control);
			if (num >= 0)
			{
				RemoveAt(num);
			}
		}

		public void RemoveAt(int index)
		{
			RemoveAtHelper(index, reparentControl: true);
		}

		internal ArrayList CloneList()
		{
			return List.Clone() as ArrayList;
		}

		internal void InitTabControl(UltraTabControlBase tabControl)
		{
			this.tabControl = tabControl;
		}

		internal void RefreshZorder()
		{
			if (Count < 2)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				List.Sort(new ZorderSortComparer(this));
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}

		private void OnControlAdded(Control control)
		{
			UltraTabControlBase ultraTabControlBase = tabControl;
			if (ultraTabControlBase == null && tab != null)
			{
				ultraTabControlBase = tab.TabControl;
			}
			if (ultraTabControlBase != null && !ultraTabControlBase.Initializing && !ultraTabControlBase.IsDesignMode && !((Control)(object)ultraTabControlBase).Disposing && !((Control)(object)ultraTabControlBase).IsDisposed)
			{
				UltraTab selectedTab = ultraTabControlBase.SelectedTab;
				UltraTabPageControl ultraTabPageControl = null;
				if (selectedTab != null && (!selectedTab.HasExcludedSharedControls || !selectedTab.ExcludedSharedControls.Contains(control)))
				{
					ultraTabPageControl = selectedTab.TabPage;
				}
				if (ultraTabPageControl == null)
				{
					ultraTabPageControl = ultraTabControlBase.SharedControlsPage;
				}
				ReparentControl(control, (Control)(object)ultraTabPageControl);
			}
		}

		private void OnControlDisposed(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is Control control)
			{
				if (tabControl != null && (((Control)(object)tabControl).IsDisposed || ((Control)(object)tabControl).Disposing))
				{
					control.Disposed -= ControlDisposedHandler;
					control.ParentChanged -= ControlParentChangedHandler;
				}
				else
				{
					Remove(control);
				}
			}
		}

		private void OnControlParentChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (!(sender is Control control) || tabControl == null || ((Control)(object)tabControl).IsDisposed || ((Control)(object)tabControl).Disposing || tabControl.Initializing)
			{
				return;
			}
			ISite site = ((Component)(object)tabControl).Site;
			IDesignerHost designerHost = ((site != null) ? (site.GetService(typeof(IDesignerHost)) as IDesignerHost) : null);
			if ((designerHost == null || !designerHost.Loading) && control != tabControl.SharedControlBeingReparented)
			{
				int num = IndexOf(control);
				if (num >= 0)
				{
					RemoveAtHelper(num, reparentControl: false);
				}
			}
		}

		private void OnControlRemoved(Control control)
		{
			UltraTabControlBase ultraTabControlBase = tabControl;
			if (ultraTabControlBase == null && tab != null)
			{
				ultraTabControlBase = tab.TabControl;
			}
			if (ultraTabControlBase == null || ultraTabControlBase.Initializing || ultraTabControlBase.IsDesignMode || ((Control)(object)ultraTabControlBase).Disposing || ((Control)(object)ultraTabControlBase).IsDisposed || !((Control)(object)ultraTabControlBase).IsHandleCreated || control == null || control.IsDisposed || control.Disposing)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (tab != null)
			{
				if (tab == ultraTabControlBase.SelectedTab && ultraTabControlBase.SharedControls.Contains(control))
				{
					ReparentControl(control, (Control)(object)tab.TabPage);
				}
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				if (((Control)(object)ultraTabControlBase).Parent == null)
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
				return;
			}
			ReparentControl(control, ((Control)(object)ultraTabControlBase).Parent);
		}

		private void RemoveAtHelper(int index, bool reparentControl)
		{
			if (index >= 0 && index < List.Count)
			{
				Control control = this[index];
				control.Disposed -= ControlDisposedHandler;
				control.ParentChanged -= ControlParentChangedHandler;
				if (reparentControl)
				{
					OnControlRemoved(control);
				}
			}
			List.RemoveAt(index);
		}

		private void ReparentControl(Control control, Control newParent)
		{
			if (newParent.Controls.Contains(control))
			{
				return;
			}
			UltraTabControlBase ultraTabControlBase = tabControl;
			if (ultraTabControlBase == null && tab != null)
			{
				ultraTabControlBase = tab.TabControl;
			}
			if (ultraTabControlBase != null)
			{
				try
				{
					ultraTabControlBase.SharedControlBeingReparented = control;
					newParent.Controls.Add(control);
				}
				finally
				{
					ultraTabControlBase.SharedControlBeingReparented = null;
				}
			}
		}
	}

		protected override bool DrawTheme(ref UIElementDrawParams drawParams)
		{
			if (TabControl.StyleResolved != UltraTabControlStyle.PropertyPage && TabControl.StyleResolved != UltraTabControlStyle.PropertyPageSelected)
			{
				return false;
			}
			Rectangle rect = ((UIElement)this).Rect;
			Rectangle invalidRect = ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).InvalidRect;
			Border3DSide borderSides = TabControl.BorderSides;
			if ((borderSides & Border3DSide.Left) != Border3DSide.Left)
			{
				rect.X--;
				rect.Width++;
			}
			else if ((borderSides & Border3DSide.Top) != Border3DSide.Top)
			{
				rect.Y--;
				rect.Height++;
			}
			else if ((borderSides & Border3DSide.Right) != Border3DSide.Right)
			{
				rect.Width += 3;
			}
			else if ((borderSides & Border3DSide.Bottom) != Border3DSide.Bottom)
			{
				rect.Height += 3;
			}
			return XPThemes.Tab.DrawTabPane(ref drawParams, rect, invalidRect);
		}

		{

			{
				tabControl.ResolveClientAreaAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
			}
		}

		public override void Invalidate()
		{
			((UIElement)this).Invalidate();
			UltraTab selectedTab = TabControl.SelectedTab;
			if (selectedTab != null && selectedTab.TabPage != null)
			{
				((Control)(object)selectedTab.TabPage).Invalidate();
			}
		}

		public sealed class UltraTabTypeConverter : ExpandableObjectConverter
		{
			public override bool CanConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, Type destinationType)
			{
				if (destinationType == typeof(InstanceDescriptor))
				{
					return true;
				}
				return base.CanConvertTo(context, destinationType);
			}

			public override object ConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, CultureInfo culture, object value, Type destinationType)
			{
				if (destinationType == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_ArgumentNullException_0"));
				}
				if (destinationType == typeof(InstanceDescriptor) && value is UltraTab)
				{
					UltraTab ultraTab = (UltraTab)value;
					bool flag = ultraTab.TabControl != null && ultraTab == ultraTab.TabControl.InitialSelectedTab;
					ConstructorInfo constructorInfo = ((!flag) ? typeof(UltraTab).GetConstructor(new Type[0]) : typeof(UltraTab).GetConstructor(new Type[1] { typeof(bool) }));
					if (constructorInfo != null)
					{
						if (flag)
						{
							return new InstanceDescriptor(constructorInfo, new object[1] { true }, isComplete: false);
						}
						return new InstanceDescriptor(constructorInfo, new object[0], isComplete: false);
					}
				}
				return base.ConvertTo(context, culture, value, destinationType);
			}
		}

		bool ITabItem.AllowMoving
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_0018: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_001d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_001e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0020: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_002e: Expected I4, but got Unknown
				if (tabControl != null && tabControl.IsDesignMode)
				{
					return true;
				}
				DefaultableBoolean val = allowMoving;
				switch (val - 1)
				{
				case 0:
					return true;
				case 1:
					return false;
				default:
					if (tabControl != null)
					{
						return tabControl.AllowTabMoving;
					}
					return false;
				}
			}
		}

		bool ITabItem.Enabled => EnabledResolved;

		{
			get

				return false;
			}
		}

		Size ITabItem.ImageSize
		{
			get
			{
				if (tabControl == null)
				{
					return Size.Empty;
				}
				Size imageSize = tabControl.ImageSize;
				if (appearanceHolder != null && appearanceHolder.RootAppearance.HasPropertyBeenSet((AppearancePropFlags)256))
				{
					return imageSize;
				}
				if (hotTrackAppearanceHolder != null && hotTrackAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance.HasPropertyBeenSet((AppearancePropFlags)256))
				{
					return imageSize;
				}
				if (selectedAppearanceHolder != null && selectedAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance.HasPropertyBeenSet((AppearancePropFlags)256))
				{
					return imageSize;
				}
				if (activeAppearanceHolder != null && activeAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance.HasPropertyBeenSet((AppearancePropFlags)256))
				{
					return imageSize;
				}
				if (tabControl.HasAppearance && tabControl.Appearance.HasPropertyBeenSet((AppearancePropFlags)256))
				{
					return imageSize;
				}
				if (tabControl.HasHotTrackAppearance && tabControl.HotTrackAppearance.HasPropertyBeenSet((AppearancePropFlags)256))
				{
					return imageSize;
				}
				if (tabControl.HasSelectedTabAppearance && tabControl.SelectedTabAppearance.HasPropertyBeenSet((AppearancePropFlags)256))
				{
					return imageSize;
				}
				return Size.Empty;
			}
		}

		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_UltraTab_P_Enabled")]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedCategory("LC_Behavior")]
		public bool Enabled
		{
			get
			{
				if (tabPage != null && !(tabPage is UltraTabSharedControlsPage))
				{
					if (tabControl != null && tabControl.IsDesignMode && TabPage != null)
					{
						PropertyDescriptorCollection properties = TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(TabPage);
						if (properties != null)
						{
							PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor = properties["Enabled"];
							if (propertyDescriptor != null)
							{
								enabled = (bool)propertyDescriptor.GetValue(TabPage);
								return enabled;
							}
						}
						return enabled;
					}
					enabled = ((Control)(object)tabPage).Enabled;
				}
				return enabled;
			}
			set
			{
				VerifyCanBeModified();
				if (tabControl != null && tabControl.Initialized && TabPage != null && (Selected || !(tabPage is UltraTabSharedControlsPage)))
				{
					if (tabControl.IsDesignMode)
					{
						TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(TabPage)?["Enabled"]?.SetValue(TabPage, value);
					}
					else
					{
						((Control)(object)TabPage).Enabled = value;
					}
				}
				InternalSetEnabled(value);
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public int Index
		{
			get
			{
				if (tabControl == null)
				{
					return -1;
				}
				if (tabControl.IsBound && tabControl.EventManager.InProgress(UltraTabControlEventId.TabDataInitialized))
				{
					return index;
				}
				if (index >= 0 && index < tabControl.Tabs.Count && this == tabControl.Tabs[index])
				{
					return index;
				}
				index = tabControl.Tabs.IndexOf(this);
				return index;
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public bool IsHotTracked
		{
			get
			{
				if (tabControl == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (tabControl.IsBound && tabControl.EventManager.InProgress(UltraTabControlEventId.TabDataInitialized))
				{
					return false;
				}
				return tabControl.HotTrackedTab == this;
			}
		}

		{

				if (tabControl == null)

					return false;

				{
					return false;
				}
				return tabControl.TabManager.IsTabItemInView((ITabItem)(object)this);
			}
		}

		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedCategory("LC_Data")]
		[MergableProperty(false)]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_UltraTab_P_Key")]
		public override string Key
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.Key.Length > 0)
				{
					return base.Key;
				}
				if (TabStripControl != null && TabStripControl.TabKeyMemberDescriptor != null && TabStripControl.List != null && ListObject != null)
				{
					object obj = null;
					try
					{
						obj = TabStripControl.TabKeyMemberDescriptor.GetValue(ListObject);
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
						return string.Empty;
					}
					if (obj is string)
					{
						return obj as string;
					}
					if (obj != null)
					{
						return obj.ToString();
					}
				}
				return string.Empty;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!base.Key.Equals(value))
				{
					base.Key = value;
					NotifyPropChange(UltraTabControlPropertyId.Key);
				}
			}
		}

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		[Browsable(false)]
		public UltraTabControlBase TabControl => tabControl;

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Visible)]
		[Browsable(false)]
		public UltraTabPageControl TabPage
		{
			get
			{
				if (tabControl != null)
				{
					if (tabControl is UltraTabStripControl)
					{
						return tabControl.SharedControlsPage;
					}
					if (tabPage == null)
					{
						tabPage = tabControl.CreateTabPage(this);
						if (tabPage != null)
						{
							try
							{
								((Control)(object)tabControl).Controls.Add((Control)(object)tabPage);
							}
							catch (InvalidOperationException)
							{
							}
						}
					}
				}
				return tabPage;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value == tabPage)
				{
					return;
				}
				VerifyCanBeModified();
				if (tabPage != null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_1"));
				}
				tabPage = value;
				if (tabPage != null)
				{
					tabPage.InitializeTab(this);
					if (tabControl != null && tabControl.Initialized && tabControl.IsDesignMode)
					{
						Enabled = Enabled;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedCategory("LC_Appearance")]
		[Localizable(true)]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_UltraTab_P_Text")]
		public string Text
		{
			get
			{
				UltraTabStripControl tabStripControl = TabStripControl;
				if (tabStripControl != null && tabStripControl.IsDesignMode && tabStripControl.TabTextMemberDescriptor != null)
				{
					return tabStripControl.TabTextMemberDescriptor.Name;
				}
				if (text != null)
				{
					return text;
				}
				if (tabStripControl != null && tabStripControl.TabTextMemberDescriptor != null && tabStripControl.List != null && ListObject != null)
				{
					object obj = null;
					try
					{
						obj = TabStripControl.TabTextMemberDescriptor.GetValue(ListObject);
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
						return string.Empty;
					}
					if (obj is string)
					{
						return obj as string;
					}
					if (obj != null)
					{
						return obj.ToString();
					}
				}
				return string.Empty;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(value == text))
				{
					VerifyCanBeModified();
					text = value;
					NotifyPropChange(UltraTabControlPropertyId.Text);
				}
			}
		}

		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_UltraTab_P_ToolTipText")]

		public string ToolTipText

				if (TabStripControl != null && TabStripControl.ToolTipTextMemberDescriptor != null && TabStripControl.List != null && !TabStripControl.IsDesignMode && ListObject != null)

		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedCategory("LC_Appearance")]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_UltraTab_P_Visible")]
		public bool Visible
		{
			get
			{
				return visible;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value == visible)
				{
					return;
				}
				VerifyCanBeModified();
				if (!value && Selected && !tabControl.IsDesignMode)
				{
					UltraTab ultraTab = tabControl.TabManager.GetNextSelectableTabItem((ITabItem)(object)this, false) as UltraTab;
					if (ultraTab == null)
					{
						ultraTab = tabControl.TabManager.GetPreviousSelectableTabItem((ITabItem)(object)this, false) as UltraTab;
					}
					if (ultraTab == null)
					{
						ultraTab = tabControl.TabManager.GetNextVisibleTabItem((ITabItem)(object)this, false) as UltraTab;
					}
					if (ultraTab == null)
					{
						ultraTab = tabControl.TabManager.GetPreviousVisibleTabItem((ITabItem)(object)this, false) as UltraTab;
					}
					tabControl.SelectedTab = ultraTab;
				}
				visible = value;
				if (value && tabControl != null && !tabControl.IsDesignMode && tabControl.SelectedTab == null)
				{
					tabControl.SelectedTab = this;
				}
				NotifyPropChange(UltraTabControlPropertyId.Visible);
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public int VisibleIndex
		{
			get
			{
				if (tabControl == null)
				{
					return visibleIndex;
				}
				if (tabControl.IsBound && tabControl.EventManager.InProgress(UltraTabControlEventId.TabDataInitialized))
				{
					return visibleIndex;
				}
				VisibleTabsCollection visibleTabs = tabControl.VisibleTabs;
				if (visibleIndex >= 0 && visibleIndex < visibleTabs.Count && this == visibleTabs[visibleIndex])
				{
					return visibleIndex;
				}
				visibleIndex = visibleTabs.IndexOf(this);
				return visibleIndex;
			}
			set
			{
				//IL_008a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0086: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_008d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				if (value != VisibleIndex)
				{
					if (tabControl == null || !tabControl.Initialized || ((Control)(object)tabControl).Disposing || ((Control)(object)tabControl).IsDisposed)
					{
						throw new InvalidOperationException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_2"));
					}
					int count = tabControl.Tabs.Count;
					if (value < 0 || value >= count)
					{
						throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException();
					}
					UltraTab relativeTab = tabControl.VisibleTabs[value];
					RelativePosition relativePosition = ((value <= VisibleIndex) ? ((RelativePosition)0) : ((RelativePosition)1));
					Reposition(relativeTab, relativePosition);
					visibleIndex = value;
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnDispose()
		{
			if (tabControl != null)
			{
				tabControl.Tabs.Remove(this);
			}
			if (tabPage != null && !(tabPage is UltraTabSharedControlsPage))
			{
				((Component)(object)tabPage).Dispose();
			}
			base.OnDispose();
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append('[');
			if (Key != null && Key.Length > 0)
			{
				stringBuilder.Append(Key);
			}
			else
			{
				stringBuilder.Append(Index);
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(']');
			if (text != null && text.Length > 0)
			{
				stringBuilder.Append(" - ");
				stringBuilder.Append(text);
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		protected override void OnRemovedFromCollection(KeyedSubObjectsCollectionBase collection)
		{
			if (collection == base.PrimaryCollection)
			{
				InternalSetOwningControl(null);
			}
			base.OnRemovedFromCollection(collection);
			tabItemTag = null;
		}

		protected override void OnSubObjectPropChanged(PropChangeInfo propChangeInfo)
		{
			UltraTabControlPropertyId ultraTabControlPropertyId;
			if (propChangeInfo.Source == excludedSharedControls)
			{
				ultraTabControlPropertyId = UltraTabControlPropertyId.ExcludedSharedControls;
			}
			else if (HasAppearance && propChangeInfo.Source == appearanceHolder.RootAppearance)
			{
				ultraTabControlPropertyId = UltraTabControlPropertyId.Appearance;
			}
			else if (HasHotTrackAppearance && propChangeInfo.Source == hotTrackAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance)
			{
				ultraTabControlPropertyId = UltraTabControlPropertyId.HotTrackAppearance;
			}
			else if (HasSelectedAppearance && selectedAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance == propChangeInfo.Source)
			{
				ultraTabControlPropertyId = UltraTabControlPropertyId.SelectedAppearance;
			}
			else if (HasActiveAppearance && activeAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance == propChangeInfo.Source)
			{
				ultraTabControlPropertyId = UltraTabControlPropertyId.ActiveAppearance;
			}
			else
			{
				if (!HasClientAreaAppearance || clientAreaAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance != propChangeInfo.Source)
				{
					return;
				}
				ultraTabControlPropertyId = UltraTabControlPropertyId.ClientAreaAppearance;
			}
			NotifyPropChange(ultraTabControlPropertyId, propChangeInfo);
		}

		void ITabItem.ResolveAppearance(TabState state, ref AppearanceData appearance, ref AppearancePropFlags requestedProps)
		{
			//IL_0004: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			ResolveAppearance(ref appearance, requestedProps, state);
		}

		public void Close()
		{
			Close((TabCloseReason)1);
		}

		internal void Close(TabCloseReason reason)
		{
			//IL_0012: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0033: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			UltraTabControlBase ultraTabControlBase = TabControl;
			if (ultraTabControlBase != null)
			{
				ultraTabControlBase.VerifyCanCloseTabs();
				TabClosingEventArgs e = new TabClosingEventArgs(this, reason);
				ultraTabControlBase.RaiseEvent(UltraTabControlEventId.TabClosing, e);
				if (!e.Cancel)
				{
					Visible = false;
					TabClosedEventArgs e2 = new TabClosedEventArgs(this, reason);
					ultraTabControlBase.RaiseEvent(UltraTabControlEventId.TabClosed, e2);
				}
			}
		}

		public void EnsureTabInView()
		{
			if (tabControl != null && (!tabControl.IsBound || !tabControl.EventManager.InProgress(UltraTabControlEventId.TabDataInitialized)))
			{
				tabControl.TabManager.EnsureTabItemInView((ITabItem)(object)this);
			}
		}

		public void Reposition(UltraTab relativeTab, RelativePosition relativePosition)
		{
			//IL_006d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0059: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (tabControl == null || ((Control)(object)tabControl).Disposing || ((Control)(object)tabControl).IsDisposed || tabControl.Initializing)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_2"));
			}
			if (tabControl.IsDesignMode)
			{
				tabControl.Tabs.RepositionTab(this, relativeTab, relativePosition);
			}
			else
			{
				TabControl.TabManager.MoveTabItem((ITabItem)(object)this, (ITabItem)(object)relativeTab, relativePosition);
			}
		}

		public void ResolveAppearance(ref AppearanceData appearance, AppearancePropFlags requestedProps, bool excludeHotTrackSettings)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0033: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0034: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0030: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0023: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0024: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			TabState val = (TabState)0;
			if (Selected)
			{
				val = (TabState)(val | 1);
			}
			if (!excludeHotTrackSettings && IsHotTracked && ((ITabItem)this).HotTrack)
			{
				val = (TabState)(val | 2);
			}
			if (Active)
			{
				val = (TabState)(val | 4);
			}
			ResolveAppearance(ref appearance, requestedProps, val);
		}

		public void ResolveAppearance(ref AppearanceData appearance, AppearancePropFlags requestedProps, TabState state)
		{
			//IL_0004: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			ResolveAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, state, resolvingPageArea: false);
		}

		internal void ResolveAppearance(ref AppearanceData appearance, ref AppearancePropFlags requestedProps, TabState state, bool resolvingPageArea)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0002: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0004: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_004c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0050: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_002a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002e: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0063: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0065: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0067: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			if ((state & 2) == 2)
			{
				switch (tabControl.StyleResolved)
				{
				default:
					if ((state & 1) == 1)
					{
						ResolveAppearancePhase(AppearanceResolutionPhase.HotTrackSelected, ref appearance, ref requestedProps, resolvingPageArea);
					}
					ResolveAppearancePhase(AppearanceResolutionPhase.HotTrack, ref appearance, ref requestedProps, resolvingPageArea);
					break;
				case UltraTabControlStyle.Wizard:
				case UltraTabControlStyle.NotePage:
				case UltraTabControlStyle.NotePageFlat:
					break;
				}
			}
			if ((long)requestedProps != 0 && (state & 4) == 4)
			{
				ResolveAppearancePhase(AppearanceResolutionPhase.Active, ref appearance, ref requestedProps, resolvingPageArea);
			}
			if ((long)requestedProps != 0 && (state & 1) == 1)
			{
				ResolveAppearancePhase(AppearanceResolutionPhase.Selected, ref appearance, ref requestedProps, resolvingPageArea);
			}
			if ((long)requestedProps != 0)
			{
				ResolveAppearancePhase(AppearanceResolutionPhase.Tab, ref appearance, ref requestedProps, resolvingPageArea);
			}
			if ((long)requestedProps != 0)
			{
				ResolveAppearancePhase(AppearanceResolutionPhase.Final, ref appearance, ref requestedProps, resolvingPageArea);
			}
		}

		internal void CacheVisibleIndex()
		{
			cachedVisibleIndex = VisibleIndex;
		}

		internal void CleanTabPage()
		{
			CleanTabPage(disposeTabPage: false);
		}

		internal void CleanTabPage(bool disposeTabPage)
		{
			if (tabPage == null || tabControl != null || tabPage is UltraTabSharedControlsPage || ((Control)(object)tabPage).IsDisposed || ((Control)(object)tabPage).Disposing)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (tabPage.HasReparentedSharedControls)
			{
				tabPage.HasReparentedSharedControls = false;
				if (tabPage.TabControl != null)
				{
					tabPage.TabControl.ReparentSharedControls();
				}
			}
			if (tabPage.TabControl != null)
			{
				((Control)(object)tabPage.TabControl).Controls.Remove((Control)(object)tabPage);
				tabPage.InitializeTabControl(null);
				if (disposeTabPage)
				{
					((Component)(object)tabPage).Dispose();
				}
			}
		}

		internal UltraTabVisibleOrderSerializer CreateVisibleOrderSerializer()
		{
			return new UltraTabVisibleOrderSerializer(Index, VisibleIndex);
		}

		internal void DestroyTabPage()
		{
			if (tabPage != null && tabControl != null && !(tabPage is UltraTabSharedControlsPage) && !((Control)(object)tabPage).IsDisposed && !((Control)(object)tabPage).Disposing)
			{
				ISite site = ((Component)(object)tabControl).Site;
				IDesignerHost designerHost = null;
				if (site != null)
				{
					designerHost = site.GetService(typeof(IDesignerHost)) as IDesignerHost;
				}
				if (Selected)
				{
					tabControl.SelectedTab = null;
				}
				if (tabPage.HasReparentedSharedControls)
				{
					tabPage.HasReparentedSharedControls = false;
					tabControl.ReparentSharedControls();
				}
				if (designerHost != null)
				{
					designerHost.DestroyComponent((IComponent)tabPage);
				}
				else
				{
					((Component)(object)tabPage).Dispose();
				}
				tabPage = null;
			}
		}

		internal void InitControl(UltraTabControlBase tabControl)
		{
			if (this.tabControl == null)
			{
				InternalSetOwningControl(tabControl);
				if (this.tabControl != null)
				{
					if (excludedSharedControls != null)
					{
						excludedSharedControls.InitTabControl(this.tabControl);
					}
					if (appearanceHolder != null)
					{
						appearanceHolder.Collection = this.tabControl.Appearances;
					}
					if (hotTrackAppearanceHolder != null)
					{
						hotTrackAppearanceHolder.Collection = this.tabControl.Appearances;
					}
					if (selectedAppearanceHolder != null)
					{
						selectedAppearanceHolder.Collection = this.tabControl.Appearances;
					}
					if (activeAppearanceHolder != null)
					{
						activeAppearanceHolder.Collection = this.tabControl.Appearances;
					}
					if (clientAreaAppearanceHolder != null)
					{
						clientAreaAppearanceHolder.Collection = this.tabControl.Appearances;
					}
				}
			}
			if (tabPage != null)
			{
				tabPage.InitializeTab(this);
			}
			if (selectedWhenSerialized && this.tabControl != null)
			{
				TabControl.SelectedTab = this;
				selectedWhenSerialized = false;
				if (TabControl.IsDesignMode)
				{
					TabControl.InitialSelectedTab = this;
				}
			}
		}

		internal void InternalOnRemovedFromCollection(KeyedSubObjectsCollectionBase collection)
		{
			OnRemovedFromCollection(collection);
		}

		internal void InternalSetEnabled(bool value)
		{
			if (value != enabled)
			{
				enabled = value;
				NotifyPropChange(UltraTabControlPropertyId.Enabled);
			}
		}

		internal void InternalSetIsAddRow(bool val)
		{
			isAddRow = val;
			if (isAddRow && TabStripControl != null)
			{
				TabStripControl.InternalSetLastAddedTab(this);
			}
		}

		internal void InternalSetListObject(object listObject)

		}

		internal bool IsMnemonic(char charCode)
		{
			string text = Text;
			if (text == null || text.Length < 2)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return Control.IsMnemonic(charCode, text);
		}

		internal void ResetListIndex(int listIndex, object listObject)
		{
			this.listIndex = listIndex;
			this.listObject = listObject;
		}

		{

				base.PrimaryCollection = this.tabControl.Tabs;
			}
		}

			switch (phase)
			{

				{
					((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).ForeColor = SystemColors.HotTrack;
					requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)((ulong)requestedProps & 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFDuL);
				}
				if (viewStyleResolved == ViewStyle.Office2003 || viewStyleResolved == ViewStyle.VisualStudio2005)
				{
					bool selected = Selected;
					Color topColor = (selected ? TabControl.MSColors.HighlightMouseDownGradientDark : TabControl.MSColors.HighlightNotPressedGradientLight);
					Color bottomColor = (selected ? TabControl.MSColors.HighlightMouseDownGradientLight : TabControl.MSColors.HighlightNotPressedGradientDark);
					tabControl.AdjustAppearancePropertiesForTabOrientation(topColor, bottomColor, ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
					if (((ulong)requestedProps & 4uL) == 4 && !((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).HasPropertyBeenSet((AppearancePropFlags)4))
					{

					}

					{
						((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).ThemedElementAlpha = (Alpha)3;
						requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)((ulong)requestedProps & 0xFFFFFFFF7FFFFFFFuL);
					}
				}
				if (viewStyleResolved == ViewStyle.Office2007)
				{
					if (!Selected && !Active)
					{
						Color tabNonSelectedHotTrackedBackColor = Office2007ColorTable.Colors.TabNonSelectedHotTrackedBackColor;
						Color tabNonSelectedHotTrackedBackColor2 = Office2007ColorTable.Colors.TabNonSelectedHotTrackedBackColor2;

					}

						requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)((ulong)requestedProps & 0xFFFFFFEFFFFFFFFFuL);
					}

					{

						requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)((ulong)requestedProps ^ 2uL);
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			case AppearanceResolutionPhase.Active:
				if (HasActiveAppearance && ((ResolutionOrderInfo)(ref resolutionOrder)).UseControlInfo)
				{
					ActiveAppearance.MergeData(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
				}
				StyleUtils.ResolveAppearance(tabControl.HasActiveTabAppearance ? tabControl.ActiveTabAppearance : null, role, (RoleState)16, resolutionOrder, ref appearance, ref requestedProps);

				break;
			case AppearanceResolutionPhase.Tab:
				if (HasAppearance && ((ResolutionOrderInfo)(ref resolutionOrder)).UseControlInfo)
				{
					Appearance.MergeData(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
				}
				break;
			case AppearanceResolutionPhase.Final:
				tabControl.ResolveDefaultTabAppearance(this, ref appearance, ref requestedProps, resolvingPageArea);
				break;
			}
		}

	[Serializable]
	public class UltraTabVisibleOrderSerializer : ISerializable
	{
		private int index;

		private int visibleIndex;

		internal int Index => index;

		internal int VisibleIndex => visibleIndex;

		internal UltraTabVisibleOrderSerializer(int index, int visibleIndex)
		{
			this.index = index;
			this.visibleIndex = visibleIndex;
		}

		protected UltraTabVisibleOrderSerializer(SerializationInfo info, StreamingContext context)
		{
			SerializationInfoEnumerator enumerator = info.GetEnumerator();
			while (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				SerializationEntry current = enumerator.Current;
				switch (current.Name)
				{
				case "Index":
					index = (int)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(int), index);
					break;
				case "VisibleIndex":
					visibleIndex = (int)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(int), visibleIndex);
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		[SecurityPermission(SecurityAction.Demand, Flags = SecurityPermissionFlag.SerializationFormatter)]
		void ISerializable.GetObjectData(SerializationInfo info, StreamingContext context)
		{
			Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "Index", index);
			Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "VisibleIndex", visibleIndex);
		}
	}

			private StyleUtils.Role GetCloseButtonRole(bool isSelectedTabItem)

			}

						}
						else
						{
							glowOrientation = (GlowOrientation)2;
						}
						break;

			protected override bool ShouldSuppressTabHighlightGlow(ITabItem tab)
			{

				bool flag = (((TabManager)this).TabProvider.GetTabItemState(tab) & 4) != 0;

				if (flag && !flag2)
				{
					return true;
				}
				return ((TabManager)this).ShouldSuppressTabHighlightGlow(tab);
			}

			TabState ITabProvider.GetTabItemState(ITabItem tab)
			{
				//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0034: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

				//IL_0024: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0025: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_004a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0046: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0048: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0049: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				TabState val = (TabState)0;
				if (tabControl.HotTrackResolved && tab == tabControl.TabManager.HotTrackTabItem)
				{
					val = (TabState)(val | 2);
				}
				if ((object)tab == tabControl.SelectedTab)
				{
					val = (TabState)(val | 1);
				}
				if ((object)tab == tabControl.ActiveTab)
				{
					val = (TabState)(val | 4);
				}
				return val;
			}
