		internal class DesignTimeVerbsHolder
		{
			private UltraTabControlBase tabControl;

			private DesignerVerb addTabVerb;

			private DesignerVerb removeTabVerb;

			private DesignerVerb excludeSharedControlsVerb;

			private DesignerVerb showSharedControlsVerb;

			private DesignerVerb convertInboxTabControlVerb;

			private DesignerVerb AddTabVerb
			{
				get
				{
					if (addTabVerb == null)
					{
						addTabVerb = new DesignerVerb(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("DesigntimeVerbAddTab"), AddTab);
					}
					return addTabVerb;
				}
			}

			private bool ContainsInboxTabControl
			{
				get
				{
					Control parent = ((Control)(object)tabControl).Parent;
					if (parent == null)
					{
						return false;
					}
					while (parent.Parent != null)
					{
						parent = parent.Parent;
					}
					return ContainsInboxTabControlHelper(parent);
				}
			}

			private DesignerVerb ConvertInboxTabControlVerb
			{
				get
				{
					if (convertInboxTabControlVerb == null)
					{
						convertInboxTabControlVerb = new DesignerVerb(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("DesigntimeVerbConvertTabControl"), ConvertInboxTabControl);
					}
					return convertInboxTabControlVerb;
				}
			}

			private DesignerVerb ExcludeSharedControlsVerb
			{
				get
				{
					if (excludeSharedControlsVerb == null)
					{
						excludeSharedControlsVerb = new DesignerVerb(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("DesigntimeVerbExcludeSharedControls"), ExcludeSharedControls);
					}
					return excludeSharedControlsVerb;
				}
			}

			private DesignerVerb RemoveTabVerb
			{
				get
				{
					if (removeTabVerb == null)
					{
						removeTabVerb = new DesignerVerb(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("DesigntimeVerbRemoveTab"), RemoveTab);
					}
					return removeTabVerb;
				}
			}

			private DesignerVerb ShowSharedControlsVerb
			{
				get
				{
					if (showSharedControlsVerb == null)
					{
						showSharedControlsVerb = new DesignerVerb(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("DesigntimeVerbShowSharedControls"), ShowSharedControls);
					}
					return showSharedControlsVerb;
				}
			}

			internal DesignTimeVerbsHolder(UltraTabControlBase tabControl)
			{
				//IL_001a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0024: Expected O, but got Unknown
				this.tabControl = tabControl;
				((UltraControlBase)this.tabControl).PropertyChanged += new PropertyChangedEventHandler(OnTabControlPropertyChanged);
			}

			internal void AddVerbs(DesignerVerbCollection verbs)
			{
				if (verbs != null)
				{
					verbs.Add(AddTabVerb);
					verbs.Add(RemoveTabVerb);
					if (tabControl is UltraTabControl)
					{
						verbs.Add(ShowSharedControlsVerb);
						verbs.Add(ExcludeSharedControlsVerb);
						verbs.Add(ConvertInboxTabControlVerb);
					}
					SetVerbsEnabledStatus();
				}
			}

			internal void SetVerbsEnabledStatus()
			{
				if (excludeSharedControlsVerb != null)
				{
					excludeSharedControlsVerb.Enabled = tabControl.SharedControls.Count > 0;
				}
				AddTabVerb.Enabled = !tabControl.IsBound;
				RemoveTabVerb.Enabled = !tabControl.IsBound;
				RemoveTabVerb.Enabled = !tabControl.IsBound && tabControl.SelectedTab != null;
				if (convertInboxTabControlVerb != null)
				{
					convertInboxTabControlVerb.Enabled = ContainsInboxTabControl;
				}
			}

			private void AddTab(object sender, EventArgs e)
			{
				DesignerTransaction designerTransaction = tabControl.CreateTransaction(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("AddTabTransaction"));
				UltraTab ultraTab = tabControl.Tabs.Add();
				if (ultraTab != null)
				{
					ultraTab.Text = tabControl.GetNewTabText();
					_ = ultraTab.TabPage;
					ultraTab.EnsureTabInView();
				}
				designerTransaction?.Commit();
			}

			private bool ContainsInboxTabControlHelper(Control container)
			{
				foreach (Control control in container.Controls)
				{
					if (control is TabControl)
					{
						return true;
					}
					if (ContainsInboxTabControlHelper(control))
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				return false;
			}

			private void ConvertInboxTabControl(object sender, EventArgs e)
			{
				ISite site = ((Component)(object)tabControl).Site;
				ConvertTabControlDialog convertTabControlDialog = new ConvertTabControlDialog(tabControl as UltraTabControl, site.GetService(typeof(IDesignerHost)) as IDesignerHost);
				convertTabControlDialog.ShowDialog();
				convertTabControlDialog.Dispose();
			}

			private void ExcludeSharedControls(object sender, EventArgs e)
			{
				ExcludeSharedControlsDialog excludeSharedControlsDialog = new ExcludeSharedControlsDialog(tabControl);
				excludeSharedControlsDialog.ShowDialog();
				excludeSharedControlsDialog.Dispose();
			}

			private void OnTabControlPropertyChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				if (!(e.ChangeInfo.PropId is UltraTabControlPropertyId))
				{
					return;
				}
				UltraTabControlPropertyId ultraTabControlPropertyId = (UltraTabControlPropertyId)(object)e.ChangeInfo.PropId;
				if (ultraTabControlPropertyId != UltraTabControlPropertyId.SharedControls && ultraTabControlPropertyId != UltraTabControlPropertyId.Tabs)
				{
					return;
				}
				try
				{
					SetVerbsEnabledStatus();
				}
				catch
				{
					if (!tabControl.IsDesignMode)
					{
						throw;
					}
				}
			}

			private void RemoveTab(object sender, EventArgs e)
			{
				UltraTab selectedTab = tabControl.SelectedTab;
				if (selectedTab != null)
				{
					DesignerTransaction designerTransaction = tabControl.CreateTransaction(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("RemoveTabTransaction"));
					((UltraControlBase)tabControl).NotifyDesignEnvironmentOfChange();
					tabControl.Tabs.Remove(selectedTab, disposeTabPage: true);
					designerTransaction?.Commit();
				}
			}

			private void ShowSharedControls(object sender, EventArgs e)
			{
				tabControl.ShowSharedControlsPage();
			}
		}

		[ComVisible(false)]
		private class UltraTabControlCollection : Control.ControlCollection
		{
			private UltraTabControlBase owner;

			public UltraTabControlCollection(UltraTabControlBase owner)
				: base((Control)(object)owner)
			{
				this.owner = owner;
			}

			public override void Add(Control value)
			{
				if (!(value is UltraTabPageControl ultraTabPageControl))
				{
					throw new ArgumentException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_11"));
				}
				base.Add(value);
				ISite site = ((Component)(object)owner).Site;
				if (site != null)
				{
					ISite site2 = ((Component)(object)ultraTabPageControl).Site;
					if (site2 == null)
					{
						site.Container.Add(value);
					}
				}
				if (ultraTabPageControl.Tab == null && ultraTabPageControl != owner.SharedControlsPage && owner.Initialized)
				{
					UltraTab ultraTab = owner.Tabs.Add();
					if (ultraTab != null && !owner.ConvertingInboxTab)
					{
						ultraTab.TabPage = ultraTabPageControl;
						owner.SelectedTab = ultraTab;
					}
				}
			}

			public override void Remove(Control value)
			{
				if (!((Control)(object)owner).IsDisposed && !((Control)(object)owner).Disposing && !value.IsDisposed && !value.Disposing)
				{
					if ((object)value == owner.SharedControlsPage && !owner.IsDesignMode)
					{
						throw new ArgumentException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_10"));
					}
					if (!owner.IsDesignMode && value is UltraTabPageControl { Tab: not null } ultraTabPageControl && ultraTabPageControl.Tab.TabControl == owner)
					{
						owner.Tabs.Remove(ultraTabPageControl.Tab);
					}
				}
				base.Remove(value);
			}
		}

		private class VisibleOrderSortComparer : IComparer
		{
			int IComparer.Compare(object x, object y)
			{
				UltraTab ultraTab = x as UltraTab;
				UltraTab ultraTab2 = y as UltraTab;
				UltraTabVisibleOrderSerializer visibleOrderSerializer = ultraTab.VisibleOrderSerializer;
				UltraTabVisibleOrderSerializer visibleOrderSerializer2 = ultraTab2.VisibleOrderSerializer;
				if (visibleOrderSerializer != null && visibleOrderSerializer2 != null)
				{
					if (visibleOrderSerializer.VisibleIndex > visibleOrderSerializer2.VisibleIndex)
					{
						return 1;
					}
					if (visibleOrderSerializer.VisibleIndex < visibleOrderSerializer2.VisibleIndex)
					{
						return -1;
					}
					return 0;
				}
				if (visibleOrderSerializer != null)
				{
					return -1;
				}
				if (visibleOrderSerializer2 != null)
				{
					return 1;
				}
				int cachedVisibleIndex = ultraTab.CachedVisibleIndex;
				int cachedVisibleIndex2 = ultraTab2.CachedVisibleIndex;
				if (cachedVisibleIndex > cachedVisibleIndex2)
				{
					return 1;
				}
				if (cachedVisibleIndex < cachedVisibleIndex2)
				{
					return -1;
				}
				return 0;
			}
		}

		private class RestoreTabOrderSortComparer : IComparer
		{
			int IComparer.Compare(object x, object y)
			{
				UltraTab ultraTab = x as UltraTab;
				UltraTab ultraTab2 = y as UltraTab;
				int index = ultraTab.Index;
				int index2 = ultraTab2.Index;
				if (index > index2)
				{
					return 1;
				}
				if (index < index2)
				{
					return -1;
				}
				return 0;
			}
		}

		protected class TabControlBaseAccessibleObject : UltraControlAccessibleObject
		{
			private UltraTabControlBase tabControlBase;

			public override Rectangle Bounds => ((AccessibleObject)this).Bounds;

			public override AccessibleRole Role
			{
				get
				{
					if (((Control)(object)tabControlBase).AccessibleRole != AccessibleRole.Default)
					{
						return ((Control.ControlAccessibleObject)this).Role;
					}
					return AccessibleRole.PageTabList;
				}
			}

			public override AccessibleStates State
			{
				get
				{
					AccessibleStates accessibleStates = AccessibleStates.None;
					if (!((Control)(object)tabControlBase).Enabled)
					{
						accessibleStates |= AccessibleStates.Unavailable;
					}
					else if (((Control)(object)tabControlBase).Visible)
					{
						accessibleStates |= AccessibleStates.Focusable;
						if (((Control)(object)tabControlBase).Focused)
						{
							accessibleStates |= AccessibleStates.Focused;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						accessibleStates |= AccessibleStates.Invisible;
					}
					if (((AccessibleObject)(object)this).Bounds.IsEmpty)
					{
						accessibleStates |= AccessibleStates.Offscreen;
					}
					return accessibleStates;
				}
			}

			public UltraTabControlBase TabControlBase => tabControlBase;

			public TabControlBaseAccessibleObject(UltraTabControlBase tabControlBase)
				: base((Control)(object)tabControlBase)
			{
				this.tabControlBase = tabControlBase;
			}

			public override AccessibleObject GetChild(int index)
			{
				//IL_007a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_007f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0080: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_00bb: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_00c1: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
				//IL_00a0: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_00a6: Expected O, but got Unknown
				//IL_00e0: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_00e6: Expected O, but got Unknown
				if (index < 0 || index >= ((AccessibleObject)(object)this).GetChildCount())
				{
					return null;
				}
				if (index < TabControlBase.VisibleTabs.Count)
				{
					return TabControlBase.VisibleTabs[index]?.AccessibilityObject;
				}
				index -= TabControlBase.VisibleTabs.Count;
				if (index < ((UltraControlAccessibleObject)this).GetChildCount())
				{
					return ((UltraControlAccessibleObject)this).GetChild(index);
				}
				index -= ((UltraControlAccessibleObject)this).GetChildCount();
				ScrollButtonTypes scrollButtonTypesResolved = tabControlBase.TabManager.ScrollButtonTypesResolved;

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public UltraTab ActiveTab
		{
			get
			{
				if (activeTab == null)
				{
					return SelectedTab;
				}
				return activeTab;
			}
			set
			{
				UltraTab ultraTab = ActiveTab;
				if (ultraTab == value || (ultraTab != null && value != null && !InternalValidateContainer()))
				{
					return;
				}
				ActiveTabChangingEventArgs e = new ActiveTabChangingEventArgs(value);
				RaiseEvent(UltraTabControlEventId.ActiveTabChanging, e);
				bool flag = true;
				if (IsBound && this is UltraTabStripControl { InPositionChanged: not false })
				{
					flag = false;
				}
				if (e.Cancel && flag)
				{
					return;
				}
				activeTab = value;
				if (Initialized)
				{
					if (ultraTab != null)
					{
						TabManager.DirtyTabItem((ITabItem)(object)ultraTab, true, false, false);
					}
					if (activeTab != null)
					{
						if (activeTab.IsInView)
						{
							TabManager.DirtyTabItem((ITabItem)(object)activeTab, true, false, false);
						}
						else
						{
							activeTab.EnsureTabInView();
						}
					}
				}
				if (activeTab != null)
				{
					AccessibilityNotifyClientsInternal(AccessibleEvents.Focus, ActiveTab.VisibleIndex);
				}
				else
				{
					AccessibilityNotifyClientsInternal(AccessibleEvents.Focus, -1);
				}
				RaiseEvent(UltraTabControlEventId.ActiveTabChanged, new ActiveTabChangedEventArgs(value, ultraTab));
			}
		}

		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedCategory("LC_Behavior")]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_UltraTabControlBase_P_CloseButtonLocation")]
		public TabCloseButtonLocation CloseButtonLocation
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				return closeButtonLocation;
			}
			set
			{
				//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_000a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0021: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_000d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_000f: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
				//IL_0045: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0046: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0033: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0043: Expected I4, but got Unknown
				if (closeButtonLocation != value)
				{
					if ((int)value != 0 && (int)value != 1)
					{
						VerifyCanCloseTabs();
					}
					if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(TabCloseButtonLocation), value))
					{
						throw new InvalidEnumArgumentException("value", (int)value, typeof(TabCloseButtonLocation));
					}
					closeButtonLocation = value;
					((UltraControlBase)this).NotifyPropChange((Enum)UltraTabControlPropertyId.CloseButtonLocation);
				}
			}
		}

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		[Browsable(false)]
		public UltraTabControlStates CurrentState
		{
			get
			{
				int num = 0;
				UltraTab selectedTab = SelectedTab;
				if (selectedTab != null)
				{
					if (selectedTab == VisibleTabs.FirstSelectableTab)
					{
						num |= 1;
					}
					if (selectedTab == VisibleTabs.LastSelectableTab)
					{
						num |= 2;
					}
					if (selectedTab == ActiveTab)
					{
						num |= 0x200;
					}
				}
				if (this is UltraTabControl)
				{
					num |= 0x20;
				}
				else if (this is UltraTabStripControl)
				{
					num |= 0x40;
				}
				if (((TabControlTabManager)(object)TabManager).CanActiveTabBeClosed)
				{
					num |= 0x400;
				}
				if (AreTabsDisplayed)
				{
					num |= 0x100;
					if (IsVerticalTabOrientation)
					{
						num |= 0x80;
					}
					if (IsMultiRow)
					{
						num |= 0x10;
						if (selectedTab != null)
						{
							int tabItemRowNumber = TabManager.GetTabItemRowNumber((ITabItem)(object)selectedTab);
							if (tabItemRowNumber == 1)
							{
								num |= 4;
							}
							if (tabItemRowNumber == TabManager.TabRowCount)
							{
								num |= 8;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if (StyleResolved == UltraTabControlStyle.Wizard)
				{
					num |= 0x800;
				}
				return (UltraTabControlStates)num;
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public UltraTabControlEventManager EventManager
		{
			get
			{
				if (eventManager == null)

					eventManager = new UltraTabControlEventManager(this);
				}
				return eventManager;
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public bool IsMultiRow
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_000b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0010: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0011: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0013: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0029: Expected I4, but got Unknown
				if (!AreTabsDisplayed)
				{
					return false;
				}
				TabLayoutStyle val = tabLayoutStyle;
				switch (val - 6)
				{
				case 0:
				case 1:
				case 2:
				case 3:
					return true;
				default:
					return false;
				}
			}
		}

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Visible)]
		[Browsable(false)]
		public UltraTabSharedControlsPage SharedControlsPage
		{
			get
			{
				if (sharedControlsPage == null)
				{
					bool flag = true;
					if (((Component)(object)this).Site != null && ((Control)this).Parent == null && ((Component)(object)this).GetService(typeof(IDesignerHost)) is IDesignerHost designerHost && designerHost.RootComponent != this)
					{
						flag = false;
					}
					if (flag)
					{
						sharedControlsPage = CreateTabPage(null) as UltraTabSharedControlsPage;
						if (sharedControlsPage != null)
						{
							try
							{
								((Control)this).Controls.Add((Control)(object)sharedControlsPage);
							}
							catch (InvalidOperationException)
							{
							}
						}
					}
				}
				return sharedControlsPage;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != sharedControlsPage)
				{
					if (sharedControlsPage != null)
					{
						throw new InvalidOperationException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_6"));
					}
					sharedControlsPage = value;
					sharedControlsPage.InitializeTabControl(this);
					if (!Initializing)
					{
						ResizeTabPage(sharedControlsPage, TabPageRect);
					}
					if (sharedControlsPage != null)
					{
						sharedControlsPage.InitializeTabControl(this);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public UltraTabControlStyle StyleResolved
		{
			get
			{
				UltraTabControlStyle ultraTabControlStyle = Style;
				switch (ultraTabControlStyle)
				{
				case UltraTabControlStyle.Wizard:
				case UltraTabControlStyle.NotePage:
				case UltraTabControlStyle.NotePageFlat:
					return ultraTabControlStyle;
				default:
				{
					if (!StyleUtils.GetCachedPropertyVal((UltraControlBase)(object)this, StyleUtils.CachedProperty.Style, out var val))
					{
						val = StyleUtils.CachePropertyValue((UltraControlBase)(object)this, StyleUtils.CachedProperty.Style, Style, UltraTabControlStyle.Default, DefaultStyleForCurrentViewStyle);
					}
					return (UltraTabControlStyle)val;
				}
				}
			}
		}

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedCategory("LC_Layout")]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_UltraTabControlBase_P_TabPageSize")]
		public Size TabPageSize
		{
			get
			{
				return TabPageRect.Size;
			}
			set
			{
				Size size = TabPageRect.Size;
				if (size != value)
				{
					Size size2 = ((Control)this).Size;
					size2.Width += value.Width - size.Width;
					size2.Height += value.Height - size.Height;
					((Control)this).Size = size2;
				}
			}
		}

		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedCategory("LC_Appearance")]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_UltraTabControlBase_P_ViewStyle")]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Visible)]
		public ViewStyle ViewStyle
		{
			get
			{
				return viewStyle;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != viewStyle)
				{
					if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(ViewStyle), value))
					{
						throw new InvalidEnumArgumentException("value", (int)value, typeof(ViewStyle));
					}
					viewStyle = value;
					((UltraControlBase)this).NotifyPropChange((Enum)UltraTabControlPropertyId.ViewStyle);
					TabManager.Dirty(false, false, true);
					TabManager.DirtyFont();
					((UltraControlBase)this).Invalidate();
				}
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public ViewStyle ViewStyleResolved
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_002c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				if (!StyleUtils.GetCachedPropertyVal((UltraControlBase)(object)this, StyleUtils.CachedProperty.ViewStyle, out var val))
				{
					ComponentRole componentRole = StyleUtils.GetComponentRole((UltraControlBase)(object)this);
					ViewStyle viewStyle = (ViewStyle)StyleUtils.GetCustomPropertyValue((UltraControlBase)(object)this, StyleUtils.CachedProperty.ViewStyle, ViewStyle.Default);
					if (viewStyle == ViewStyle.Default && componentRole != null)
					{
						viewStyle = StyleUtils.ViewStyleToViewStyle(componentRole.ViewStyle);
					}
					val = StyleUtils.CachePropertyValue((UltraControlBase)(object)this, StyleUtils.CachedProperty.ViewStyle, viewStyle, ViewStyle, ViewStyle.Default, ViewStyle.Standard);
				}
				return (ViewStyle)val;
			}
		}

		internal bool AreTabsDisplayed
		{
			get
			{
				switch (StyleResolved)
				{
				case UltraTabControlStyle.Wizard:
				case UltraTabControlStyle.NotePage:
				case UltraTabControlStyle.NotePageFlat:
					return false;
				default:
					return true;
				}
			}
		}

		internal Image ArrowLeftImage => GetCachedImage(CachedImageIndex.ArrowLeft);

		internal Image ArrowRightImage => GetCachedImage(CachedImageIndex.ArrowRight);

		[DefaultValue(typeof(Color), "")]
		[Browsable(false)]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Visible)]
		public Color BackColorInternal
		{
			get
			{
				return backColorInternal;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != backColorInternal)
				{
					backColorInternal = value;
					SyncBackColor();
				}
			}
		}

		internal Border3DSide BorderSides => GetBorderSides(honorRightToLeft: true);

		internal int BorderWidth
		{
			get
			{
				int result = 0;
				switch (StyleResolved)
				{
				case UltraTabControlStyle.VisualStudio:
				case UltraTabControlStyle.Flat:
				case UltraTabControlStyle.Excel:
				case UltraTabControlStyle.VisualStudio2005:
				case UltraTabControlStyle.Office2007Ribbon:
					result = 1;
					break;
				default:
					result = 2;
					break;
				case UltraTabControlStyle.StateButtons:
				case UltraTabControlStyle.Wizard:
				case UltraTabControlStyle.NotePageFlat:
					break;
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		internal bool ConvertingInboxTab
		{
			get
			{
				return convertingInboxTab;
			}
			set
			{
				convertingInboxTab = value;
			}
		}

		internal UltraTabControlStyle DefaultStyleForCurrentViewStyle
		{
			get
			{
				switch (ViewStyleResolved)
				{
				case ViewStyle.Office2003:
				case ViewStyle.Standard:
					return UltraTabControlStyle.PropertyPage;
				case ViewStyle.VisualStudio2005:
					return UltraTabControlStyle.PropertyPage2003;
				case ViewStyle.Office2007:
					return UltraTabControlStyle.Office2007Ribbon;
				default:
					return UltraTabControlStyle.PropertyPage;
				}
			}
		}

		internal bool DelayedInitializePerformed => delayedInitializePerformed;

		internal bool DesignModeBeginInitCalled
		{
			get
			{
				return designModeBeginInitCalled;
			}
			set
			{
				if (designModeBeginInitCalled)
				{
					return;
				}
				designModeBeginInitCalled = true;
				if (tabs == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				foreach (UltraTab tab in Tabs)
				{
					tab.Inherited = true;
				}
			}
		}

		internal Font DesignTimeLiteralFont
		{
			get
			{
				if (designTimeLiteralFont == null)
				{
					designTimeLiteralFont = new Font(FontFamily.GenericSansSerif.Name, 10f);
				}
				return designTimeLiteralFont;
			}
		}

		internal int DesignTimeLiteralFontHeight
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_0034: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				if (designTimeLiteralFontHeight < 1)
				{
					Graphics graphics = null;
					try
					{
						graphics = DrawUtility.GetCachedGraphics((Control)null);
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
						if (IsDesignMode)
						{
							throw;
						}
					}
					if (graphics == null)
					{
						return 20;
					}
					Size size = Size.Ceiling(DrawUtility.MeasureString(graphics, "Wy", DesignTimeLiteralFont, DrawUtility.GetGdiDrawStringFlags((UltraControlBase)(object)this)));
					DrawUtility.ReleaseCachedGraphics(graphics);
					designTimeLiteralFontHeight = size.Height + 2;
				}
				return designTimeLiteralFontHeight;
			}
		}

					designTimeVerbs = new DesignTimeVerbsHolder(this);

				return designTimeVerbs;

		}

		internal bool DrawTabThemed
		{
			get
			{
				bool flag = StyleResolved == UltraTabControlStyle.PropertyPage || StyleResolved == UltraTabControlStyle.PropertyPageSelected;
				if (flag)
				{
					flag = ((UltraControlBase)this).UseOsThemesResolved && XPThemes.ThemesSupported && XPThemes.IsThemeActive && XPThemes.AppControlsThemeThemed;
				}
				return flag;
			}
		}

				return false;

		internal bool IsVerticalTabOrientation
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0007: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0009: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_001f: Expected I4, but got Unknown
				TabOrientation val = tabOrientation;
				switch (val - 5)
				{
				case 0:
				case 1:
				case 2:
				case 3:
					return true;
				default:
					return false;
				}
			}
		}

		internal NavigationStyle NavigationStyleResolved
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_001d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0023: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
				if (navigationStyle != NavigationStyle.Default)
				{
					return navigationStyle;
				}
				if (IsMultiRow && (int)TabManager.MultiRowSelectionStyleResolved == 1)
				{
					return NavigationStyle.Activate;
				}
				if (StyleResolved == UltraTabControlStyle.StateButtons)
				{
					return NavigationStyle.Activate;
				}
				return NavigationStyle.AutoSelect;
			}
		}

		internal string Page_X_of_X_Text
		{
			get
			{
				UltraTab selectedTab = SelectedTab;
				if (selectedTab == null)
				{
					if (((Component)this).DesignMode && this is UltraTabControl)
					{
						return Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("SharedPageText");
					}
					return string.Empty;
				}
				if (pageNumberFormatString == null)
				{
					pageNumberFormatString = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("PageNumberText");
				}
				return string.Format(pageNumberFormatString, selectedTab.Index + 1, Tabs.Count);
			}
		}

		internal int TabHeaderAreaExtent
		{
			get
			{
				int result = 0;
				switch (StyleResolved)
				{
				default:
				{
					if (IsDesignMode && Tabs.Count < 1)
					{
						return DesignTimeLiteralFontHeight;
					}
					Size tabAreaSize = TabManager.TabAreaSize;
					result = ((!IsVerticalTabOrientation) ? tabAreaSize.Height : tabAreaSize.Width);
					break;
				}
				case UltraTabControlStyle.Wizard:
				case UltraTabControlStyle.NotePage:
				case UltraTabControlStyle.NotePageFlat:
					break;
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		internal Rectangle TabHeaderAreaRect
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_0019: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_001e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_001f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0021: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0047: Expected I4, but got Unknown
				int tabHeaderAreaExtent = TabHeaderAreaExtent;
				if (tabHeaderAreaExtent < 1)
				{
					return Rectangle.Empty;
				}
				Size clientSize = ((Control)this).ClientSize;
				TabOrientation val = tabOrientation;
				Rectangle result;
				switch (val - 1)
				{
				default:
					result = new Rectangle(0, 0, clientSize.Width, tabHeaderAreaExtent);
					break;
				case 2:
				case 3:
					result = new Rectangle(0, Math.Max(0, clientSize.Height - tabHeaderAreaExtent), clientSize.Width, tabHeaderAreaExtent);
					break;
				case 4:
				case 5:
					result = new Rectangle(0, 0, tabHeaderAreaExtent, clientSize.Height);
					break;
				case 6:
				case 7:
					result = new Rectangle(Math.Max(0, clientSize.Width - tabHeaderAreaExtent), 0, tabHeaderAreaExtent, clientSize.Height);
					break;
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		internal TabManager TabManager
		{

				//IL_002b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

				//IL_0042: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

				//IL_0091: Expected O, but got Unknown

				//IL_00a8: Expected O, but got Unknown
				if (tabManager == null)

					_ = TabProvider;

					tabManager.SelectedTabItemChanged += new SelectedTabItemChangedEventHandler(OnManagerSelectedTabItemChanged);
					tabManager.SelectedTabItemChanging += new SelectedTabItemChangingEventHandler(OnManagerSelectedTabItemChanging);
					tabManager.TabItemMoved += new TabItemMovedEventHandler(OnManagerTabItemMoved);

					tabManager.TabScrolled += new TabScrolledEventHandler(OnManagerTabScrolled);

			}

		internal bool UseHotTrackingResolvedWithoutDesignModeCheck
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_0031: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0036: Invalid comparison between I4 and Unknown
				//IL_000e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

				if (!StyleUtils.GetCachedPropertyVal((UltraControlBase)(object)this, StyleUtils.CachedProperty.UseHotTracking, out var val))
				{
					val = StyleUtils.CachePropertyValue((UltraControlBase)(object)this, StyleUtils.CachedProperty.UseHotTracking, UseHotTracking, (object)(DefaultableBoolean)0, UseHotTrackingDefault);
				}
				return 1 == (int)(DefaultableBoolean)val;
			}
		}

		internal bool UseHotTrackingResolved
		{

				return false;
			}
		}

		private DefaultableBoolean UseHotTrackingDefault
		{
			get

				{
				case ViewStyle.Office2003:
				case ViewStyle.VisualStudio2005:
				case ViewStyle.Standard:
					return (DefaultableBoolean)2;
				case ViewStyle.Office2007:
					return (DefaultableBoolean)1;
				default:
					return (DefaultableBoolean)2;
				}
			}
		}

		internal UltraTabControlBase()
		{
			//IL_0058: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0066: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0073: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0078: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_007e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0083: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			mainElement = new UltraTabControlUIElement(this);
			((Control)this).SetStyle(ControlStyles.AllPaintingInWmPaint, value: true);
			((Control)this).SetStyle(ControlStyles.UserPaint, value: true);
			((Control)this).SetStyle(ControlStyles.ResizeRedraw, value: true);
			((Control)this).SetStyle(ControlStyles.OptimizedDoubleBuffer, value: false);
			((Control)this).SetStyle(ControlStyles.StandardClick, value: true);
			((Control)this).SetStyle(ControlStyles.StandardDoubleClick, value: true);
			((Control)this).SetStyle(ControlStyles.SupportsTransparentBackColor, value: true);
			((Control)this).SetStyle(ControlStyles.ContainerControl, value: true);
		}

		void ISupportInitialize.BeginInit()
		{
			initializing = true;
			delayedInitializePending = true;
			persistedSelectedTab = null;
			if (((Component)this).DesignMode)
			{
				DesignModeBeginInitCalled = true;
			}
			OnBeginInit();
		}

		void ISupportInitialize.EndInit()
		{
			initializing = false;
			UltraTabStripControl ultraTabStripControl = this as UltraTabStripControl;
			if (((Component)this).DesignMode)
			{
				HookComponentChangeService();
			}
			if (((Control)this).IsHandleCreated)
			{
				((Control)this).BeginInvoke((Delegate)new MethodInvoker(PerformDelayedInitialize));
			}
			if (((Component)this).DesignMode)
			{
				InheritanceAttribute inheritanceAttribute = Utilities.GetInheritanceAttribute((Component)(object)this);
				TabPageMargins.ForceSerialization = inheritanceAttribute != null && inheritanceAttribute.InheritanceLevel == InheritanceLevel.Inherited;
			}
			SyncBackColor();
			OnEndInit();
			if (ultraTabStripControl != null && !((Component)this).DesignMode && IsBound && ((Control)this).IsHandleCreated)
			{
				((Control)(object)ultraTabStripControl).BeginInvoke((Delegate)new MethodInvoker(ultraTabStripControl.VerifyBindingManagerInitialized));
			}
		}

		internal void RaiseEvent(UltraTabControlEventId id, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (!EventManager.CanFireEvent(id) || !Enum.IsDefined(typeof(UltraTabControlEventId), id))
			{
				return;
			}
			EventManager.IncrementInProgress(id);
			try
			{
				switch (id)
				{
				case UltraTabControlEventId.ActiveTabChanged:
					OnActiveTabChanged(e as ActiveTabChangedEventArgs);
					break;
				case UltraTabControlEventId.ActiveTabChanging:
					OnActiveTabChanging(e as ActiveTabChangingEventArgs);
					break;
				case UltraTabControlEventId.PerformingAction:
					OnPerformingAction(e as PerformingActionEventArgs);
					break;
				case UltraTabControlEventId.PerformedAction:
					OnPerformedAction(e as PerformedActionEventArgs);
					break;
				case UltraTabControlEventId.SelectedTabChanged:
					OnSelectedTabChanged(e as SelectedTabChangedEventArgs);
					break;
				case UltraTabControlEventId.SelectedTabChanging:
					OnSelectedTabChanging(e as SelectedTabChangingEventArgs);
					break;
				case UltraTabControlEventId.TabDataInitialized:
					if (this is UltraTabStripControl)
					{
						((UltraTabStripControl)this).RaiseTabDataInitialized(e as TabDataInitializedEventArgs);
					}
					break;
				case UltraTabControlEventId.TabClosed:
					OnTabClosed(e as TabClosedEventArgs);
					break;
				case UltraTabControlEventId.TabClosing:
					OnTabClosing(e as TabClosingEventArgs);
					break;
				case UltraTabControlEventId.TabMoved:
					OnTabMoved(e as TabMovedEventArgs);
					break;
				case UltraTabControlEventId.TabMoving:
					OnTabMoving(e as TabMovingEventArgs);
					break;
				case UltraTabControlEventId.TabPageResized:
					OnTabPageResized(e as TabPageResizedEventArgs);
					break;
				case UltraTabControlEventId.TabScrolled:
					OnTabScrolled((TabScrolledEventArgs)(object)((e is TabScrolledEventArgs) ? e : null));
					break;
				case UltraTabControlEventId.TabScrolling:
					OnTabScrolling((TabScrollingEventArgs)(object)((e is TabScrollingEventArgs) ? e : null));
					break;
				case UltraTabControlEventId.MouseEnterElement:
					((UltraControlBase)this).OnMouseEnterElement((UIElementEventArgs)(object)((e is UIElementEventArgs) ? e : null));
					break;
				case UltraTabControlEventId.MouseLeaveElement:
					((UltraControlBase)this).OnMouseLeaveElement((UIElementEventArgs)(object)((e is UIElementEventArgs) ? e : null));
					break;
				case UltraTabControlEventId.BeforeNewTabButtonClicked:
					OnBeforeNewTabButtonClicked(e as CancelEventArgs);
					break;
				case UltraTabControlEventId.AfterNewTabButtonClicked:
					OnAfterNewTabButtonClicked(e as TabEventArgs);
					break;
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				EventManager.DecrementInProgress(id);

		private void OnManagerSelectedTabItemChanging(object sender, SelectedTabItemChangingEventArgs e)
		{
			if (((CancelableTabItemEventArgs)e).TabItem != null)
			{
				ActiveTab = ((CancelableTabItemEventArgs)e).TabItem as UltraTab;
				if ((object)ActiveTab != ((CancelableTabItemEventArgs)e).TabItem)
				{
					((CancelEventArgs)(object)e).Cancel = true;
					return;
				}
			}
			bool flag = true;
			if (IsBound && this is UltraTabStripControl { InPositionChanged: not false })
			{
				flag = false;
			}
			if ((object)GetSelectedTab(allowSelectionReplacement: false) == ((CancelableTabItemEventArgs)e).TabItem)
			{
				((CancelEventArgs)(object)e).Cancel = flag;
				return;
			}
			SelectedTabChangingEventArgs e2 = new SelectedTabChangingEventArgs(((CancelableTabItemEventArgs)e).TabItem as UltraTab);
			RaiseEvent(UltraTabControlEventId.SelectedTabChanging, e2);
			((CancelEventArgs)(object)e).Cancel = e2.Cancel && flag;
		}

		private void OnManagerSelectedTabItemChanged(object sender, SelectedTabItemChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			UltraTab ultraTab = e.PreviousSelectedTabItem as UltraTab;
			UltraTabPageControl ultraTabPageControl = null;
			UltraTabPageControl ultraTabPageControl2 = null;
			SelectedTabChangedEventArgs e2 = null;
			Control activeControl = GetActiveControl();
			try
			{
				UltraTab ultraTab2 = (activeTab = ((TabItemEventArgs)e).TabItem as UltraTab);
				Rectangle tabPageRect = TabPageRect;
				ResizeTabPage(sharedControlsPage, tabPageRect);
				if (this is UltraTabStripControl)
				{
					if (IsBound && this is UltraTabStripControl ultraTabStripControl)
					{
						ultraTabStripControl.SyncPositionWithSelectedTab();
					}
				}
				else
				{
					SetRedraw(setRedrawOn: false, (Control)(object)this);
					if (ultraTab != null)
					{
						ultraTabPageControl = ultraTab.TabPage;
					}
					if (ultraTab2 != null)
					{
						ultraTabPageControl2 = ultraTab2.TabPage;
					}
					if (ultraTabPageControl != null)
					{
						SetRedraw(setRedrawOn: false, (Control)(object)ultraTabPageControl);
					}
					if (ultraTabPageControl2 != null)
					{
						SetRedraw(setRedrawOn: false, (Control)(object)ultraTabPageControl2);
					}
					if (ultraTab != null)
					{
						ResizeTabPage(ultraTab.TabPage, tabPageRect);
					}
					if (ultraTab2 != null)
					{
						ResizeTabPage(ultraTab2.TabPage, tabPageRect);
					}
					if (ultraTabPageControl != null)
					{
						ultraTabPageControl.HasReparentedSharedControls = false;
					}
					ReparentSharedControlsToTab(SelectedTab, ultraTab);
				}
				if (!((Component)this).DesignMode)
				{
					if (ultraTabPageControl2 != null)
					{
						ultraTabPageControl2.Visible = true;
					}
					if (this is UltraTabStripControl && ultraTab2 != null)
					{
						SharedControlsPage.Enabled = ultraTab2.Enabled;
					}
					if (ultraTabPageControl != null)
					{
						ultraTabPageControl.Visible = false;
					}
					e2 = new SelectedTabChangedEventArgs(((TabItemEventArgs)e).TabItem as UltraTab, e.PreviousSelectedTabItem as UltraTab);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				((UIElement)mainElement).DirtyChildElements(true);
				if (!(this is UltraTabStripControl))
				{
					if (((Component)this).DesignMode && ultraTabPageControl != null)
					{
						SetRedraw(setRedrawOn: true, (Control)(object)ultraTabPageControl);
					}
					SetRedraw(setRedrawOn: true, (Control)(object)this);
					if (sharedControlsPage != null && !((Component)this).DesignMode)
					{
						sharedControlsPage.Visible = ultraTabPageControl2 == null;
					}
					if (ultraTabPageControl2 != null)
					{
						SetRedraw(setRedrawOn: true, (Control)(object)ultraTabPageControl2);
					}
					else if (SharedControlsPage != null)
					{
						SetRedraw(setRedrawOn: true, (Control)(object)SharedControlsPage);
					}
				}
				if (e2 != null)
				{
					if (activeControl != null && ultraTabPageControl2 != null)
					{
						ReactivateControlIfVisible(activeControl);
					}
					RaiseEvent(UltraTabControlEventId.SelectedTabChanged, e2);
				}
				if (((Control)this).IsHandleCreated)
				{
					((Control)this).BeginInvoke((Delegate)new MethodInvoker(SetTabPageRegion));
				}
				AccessibilityNotifyClientsInternal(AccessibleEvents.Selection, (SelectedTab != null) ? SelectedTab.VisibleIndex : (-1));
			}
		}

		protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			//IL_01b5: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01bf: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_01cc: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01d6: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_01e3: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01ed: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_01fa: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0204: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0211: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_021b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0228: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0232: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (disposing)
			{
				UnhookComponentChangeService();
				if (sharedControls != null)
				{
					sharedControls = null;
				}
				if (designTimeLiteralFont != null)
				{
					designTimeLiteralFont.Dispose();
					designTimeLiteralFont = null;
				}
				if (mainElement != null)
				{
					((DisposableObject)(object)mainElement).Dispose();
					mainElement = null;
				}
				if (appearanceHolder != null)
				{
					appearanceHolder.SubObjectPropChanged -= SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
					appearanceHolder.Reset();
					appearanceHolder = null;
				}
				if (activeTabAppearanceHolder != null)
				{
					activeTabAppearanceHolder.SubObjectPropChanged -= SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
					activeTabAppearanceHolder.Reset();
					activeTabAppearanceHolder = null;
				}
				if (clientAreaAppearanceHolder != null)
				{
					clientAreaAppearanceHolder.SubObjectPropChanged -= SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
					clientAreaAppearanceHolder.Reset();
					clientAreaAppearanceHolder = null;
				}
				if (selectedTabAppearanceHolder != null)
				{
					selectedTabAppearanceHolder.SubObjectPropChanged -= SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
					selectedTabAppearanceHolder.Reset();
					selectedTabAppearanceHolder = null;
				}
				if (hotTrackAppearanceHolder != null)
				{
					hotTrackAppearanceHolder.SubObjectPropChanged -= SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
					hotTrackAppearanceHolder.Reset();
					hotTrackAppearanceHolder = null;
				}
				if (tabHeaderAreaAppearanceHolder != null)
				{
					tabHeaderAreaAppearanceHolder.SubObjectPropChanged -= SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
					tabHeaderAreaAppearanceHolder.Reset();
					tabHeaderAreaAppearanceHolder = null;
				}
				for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
				{
					if (cachedImages[i] != null)
					{
						cachedImages[i].Dispose();
						cachedImages[i] = null;
					}
					if (cachedImageStreams[i] != null)
					{
						cachedImageStreams[i].Close();
						cachedImageStreams[i] = null;
					}
				}
				if (tabManager != null)
				{
					tabManager.SelectedTabItemChanged -= new SelectedTabItemChangedEventHandler(OnManagerSelectedTabItemChanged);
					tabManager.SelectedTabItemChanging -= new SelectedTabItemChangingEventHandler(OnManagerSelectedTabItemChanging);
					tabManager.TabItemMoved -= new TabItemMovedEventHandler(OnManagerTabItemMoved);
					tabManager.TabItemMoving -= new TabItemMovingEventHandler(OnManagerTabItemMoving);
					tabManager.TabScrolled -= new TabScrolledEventHandler(OnManagerTabScrolled);
					tabManager.TabScrolling -= new TabScrollingEventHandler(OnManagerTabScrolling);
					((DisposableObject)(object)tabManager).Dispose();
					tabManager = null;
					tabProvider = null;
				}
			}
			((UltraControlBase)this).Dispose(disposing);
		}

		protected override bool IsInputKey(Keys keyData)
		{
			Keys keys = Keys.KeyCode & keyData;
			if (IsMultiRow || !IsVerticalTabOrientation)
			{
				switch (keys)
				{
				case Keys.Left:
					return true;
				case Keys.Right:
					return true;
				}
			}
			if (IsMultiRow || IsVerticalTabOrientation)
			{
				switch (keys)
				{
				case Keys.Down:
					return true;
				case Keys.Up:
					return true;
				}
			}
			if (((Control)this).IsInputKey(keyData))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return ((KeyActionMappingsBase)KeyActionMappings).IsKeyMapped(keyData, (long)CurrentState);
		}

		protected override bool IsPointDesignTimeActive(Point point)
		{
			if (IsBound)
			{
				return false;
			}
			UIElement val = ((UIElement)mainElement).ElementFromPoint(point);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (val.GetAncestor(typeof(TabCloseButtonUIElement)) != null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (val is ScrollButtonUIElement || val is NotePageEarMarkUIElement || val is WizardNavigationButtonUIElement || val.Parent is WizardNavigationButtonUIElement)
			{
				return val.Enabled;
			}
			object context = val.GetContext(typeof(ITabItem), true);
			ITabItem val2 = (ITabItem)((context is ITabItem) ? context : null);
			if (val2 != null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			UIElement ancestor = val.GetAncestor(typeof(TabScrollButtonUIElement));
			TabScrollButtonUIElement val3 = (TabScrollButtonUIElement)(object)((ancestor is TabScrollButtonUIElement) ? ancestor : null);
			if (val3 != null)
			{
				return val.Enabled;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected override void OnMouseEnterElement(UIElementEventArgs e)
		{
			if (EventManager.CanFireEvent(UltraTabControlEventId.MouseEnterElement))
			{
				EventManager.IncrementInProgress(UltraTabControlEventId.MouseEnterElement);
				try
				{
					((UltraControlBase)this).OnMouseEnterElement(e);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					EventManager.DecrementInProgress(UltraTabControlEventId.MouseEnterElement);
					throw;
				}
				EventManager.DecrementInProgress(UltraTabControlEventId.MouseEnterElement);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnPaint(PaintEventArgs e)
		{
			if (firstDraw && !((UltraControlBase)this).IsUpdating)
			{
				firstDraw = false;
				PerformDelayedInitialize();
				if (IsBound)
				{
					((UltraTabStripControl)this).VerifyBindingManagerInitialized();
				}
				PositionTabPages();
				Control activeControl = GetActiveControl();
				ReparentSharedControls();
				if (activeControl != null)
				{
					ReactivateControlIfVisible(activeControl);
				}
			}
			((UltraControlBase)this).OnPaint(e);
		}

		protected override void OnPropertyChanged(PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((UltraControlBase)this).OnPropertyChanged(e);
			StyleUtils.DirtyCachedPropertyVals((UltraControlBase)(object)this);
			if (!((Control)this).Created)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			if (tabManager != null && e.ChangeInfo.PropId is UltraTabControlPropertyId)
			{
				switch ((UltraTabControlPropertyId)(object)e.ChangeInfo.PropId)
				{
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.Appearance:
					SyncBackColor();
					break;
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.DataMember:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.DataSource:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.HotTrack:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.MultiRowSelectionStyle:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.TabKeyMember:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.TabTextMember:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.MaxTabWidth:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.TabsPerRow:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.InterTabSpacing:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.ScaleImages:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.ShowButtonSeparators:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.MinTabWidth:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.ShowPartialTabs:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.ShowTabListButton:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.SpaceAfterTabs:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.SpaceBeforeTabs:
					tabManager.Dirty(false, false, true);
					break;
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.ImageList:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.ImageSize:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.MaxVisibleTabRows:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.ScrollTrackExtent:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.ScrollButtonTypes:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.Style:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.TabLayoutStyle:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.TabOrientation:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.TabPadding:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.TabPageMargins:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.TabPageSize:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.TabSize:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.TextOrientation:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.FixedWidth:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.ScrollButtons:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.InterRowSpacing:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.AllowTabClosing:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.CloseButtonLocation:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.AllowClosing:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.CloseButtonVisibility:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.TabCloseButtonVisibility:
					flag = true;
					tabManager.Dirty(false, false, true);
					break;
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.SortDirection:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.SortMember:
					tabManager.Dirty(false, true, false);
					break;
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.TabCloseButtonAlignment:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.CloseButtonAlignment:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.CloseButtonAppearance:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.HotTrackCloseButtonAppearance:
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.PressedCloseButtonAppearance:
					tabManager.Dirty(true, false, false);
					break;
				}
			}
			((UIElement)mainElement).DirtyChildElements(true);
			if (flag)
			{
				PositionTabPages(sharedAndSelectedTabsOnly: false, Initialized);
			}
			else
			{
				PositionTabPages();
			}
			if (!firstDraw)
			{
				UltraTab selectedTab = SelectedTab;
				if (selectedTab != null && selectedTab.TabPage != null)
				{
					((Control)(object)selectedTab.TabPage).Invalidate();
				}
				else if (sharedControlsPage != null)
				{
					((Control)(object)sharedControlsPage).Invalidate();
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnResize(EventArgs e)
		{
			((UltraControlBase)this).OnResize(e);
			if (!isScaling && ((Control)this).Created)
			{
				PositionTabPages();
				SetTabPageRegion();
			}
		}

		protected void OnSubObjectPropChanged(PropChangeInfo propChangeInfo)
		{
			//IL_00a9: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00af: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_00df: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00e5: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_01a1: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01a7: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_01d7: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01dd: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			if (!((Control)this).Created)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			UltraTabControlPropertyId ultraTabControlPropertyId;
			if (propChangeInfo.Source == tabs)
			{
				if (activeTab != null && activeTab.TabControl != this)
				{
					activeTab = SelectedTab;
				}
				ultraTabControlPropertyId = UltraTabControlPropertyId.Tabs;
			}
			else if (propChangeInfo.Source == sharedControls)
			{
				ultraTabControlPropertyId = UltraTabControlPropertyId.SharedControls;
			}
			else if (propChangeInfo.Source == appearances)
			{
				ultraTabControlPropertyId = UltraTabControlPropertyId.Appearances;
			}
			else if (propChangeInfo.Source == tabPageMargins)
			{
				ultraTabControlPropertyId = UltraTabControlPropertyId.TabPageMargins;
			}
			else if (HasAppearance && propChangeInfo.Source == appearanceHolder.RootAppearance)
			{
				ultraTabControlPropertyId = UltraTabControlPropertyId.Appearance;
				if ((int)(AppearancePropIds)(object)propChangeInfo.PropId == 4)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			else if (HasHotTrackAppearance && propChangeInfo.Source == hotTrackAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance)
			{
				ultraTabControlPropertyId = UltraTabControlPropertyId.HotTrackAppearance;
				if ((int)(AppearancePropIds)(object)propChangeInfo.PropId == 4)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			else if (HasTabHeaderAreaAppearance && tabHeaderAreaAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance == propChangeInfo.Source)
			{
				ultraTabControlPropertyId = UltraTabControlPropertyId.TabHeaderAreaAppearance;
			}
			else if (HasScrollButtonAppearance && scrollButtonAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance == propChangeInfo.Source)
			{
				ultraTabControlPropertyId = UltraTabControlPropertyId.ScrollButtonAppearance;
			}
			else if (HasTabListButtonAppearance && tabListButtonAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance == propChangeInfo.Source)
			{
				ultraTabControlPropertyId = UltraTabControlPropertyId.TabListButtonAppearance;
			}
			else if (HasScrollTrackAppearance && scrollTrackAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance == propChangeInfo.Source)
			{
				ultraTabControlPropertyId = UltraTabControlPropertyId.ScrollTrackAppearance;
			}
			else if (HasSelectedTabAppearance && selectedTabAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance == propChangeInfo.Source)
			{
				ultraTabControlPropertyId = UltraTabControlPropertyId.SelectedTabAppearance;
				if ((int)(AppearancePropIds)(object)propChangeInfo.PropId == 4)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			else if (HasActiveTabAppearance && activeTabAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance == propChangeInfo.Source)
			{
				ultraTabControlPropertyId = UltraTabControlPropertyId.ActiveTabAppearance;
				if ((int)(AppearancePropIds)(object)propChangeInfo.PropId == 4)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			else if (HasClientAreaAppearance && clientAreaAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance == propChangeInfo.Source)
			{
				ultraTabControlPropertyId = UltraTabControlPropertyId.ClientAreaAppearance;
			}
			else if (HasCloseButtonAppearance && closeButtonAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance == propChangeInfo.Source)
			{
				ultraTabControlPropertyId = UltraTabControlPropertyId.CloseButtonAppearance;
			}
			else if (HasHotTrackCloseButtonAppearance && hotTrackCloseButtonAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance == propChangeInfo.Source)
			{
				ultraTabControlPropertyId = UltraTabControlPropertyId.HotTrackCloseButtonAppearance;
			}
			else if (HasPressedCloseButtonAppearance && pressedCloseButtonAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance == propChangeInfo.Source)
			{
				ultraTabControlPropertyId = UltraTabControlPropertyId.PressedCloseButtonAppearance;
			}
			else if (HasNewTabButtonAppearance && newTabButtonAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance == propChangeInfo.Source)
			{
				ultraTabControlPropertyId = UltraTabControlPropertyId.NewTabButtonAppearance;
			}
			else if (HasPressedNewTabButtonAppearance && pressedNewTabButtonAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance == propChangeInfo.Source)
			{
				ultraTabControlPropertyId = UltraTabControlPropertyId.PressedNewTabButtonAppearance;
			}
			else
			{
				if (!HasHotTrackNewTabButtonAppearance || hotTrackNewTabButtonAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance != propChangeInfo.Source)
				{
					return;
				}
				ultraTabControlPropertyId = UltraTabControlPropertyId.HotTrackNewTabButtonAppearance;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				InternalFontChanged();
			}
			((UltraControlBase)this).NotifyPropChange((Enum)ultraTabControlPropertyId, propChangeInfo);
		}

		protected override bool ProcessDialogKey(Keys keyData)
		{
			if (!((Control)(object)this).Focused)
			{
				switch (keyData)
				{
				case Keys.Tab | Keys.Control:
				{
					KeyEventArgs e2 = new KeyEventArgs(keyData);
					if (((UltraControlBase)this).ControlUIElement != null)
					{
						((UltraControlBase)this).ControlUIElement.ProcessKeyDown(e2);
						return e2.Handled;
					}
					break;
				}
				case Keys.Tab | Keys.Shift | Keys.Control:
				{
					KeyEventArgs e = new KeyEventArgs(keyData);
					if (((UltraControlBase)this).ControlUIElement != null)
					{
						((UltraControlBase)this).ControlUIElement.ProcessKeyDown(e);
						return e.Handled;
					}
					break;
				}
				}
			}
			return ((Control)this).ProcessDialogKey(keyData);
		}

		protected override bool ProcessMnemonic(char charCode)
		{
			if (!UseMnemonicsResolved || !((Control)this).Enabled || !((Control)this).Visible)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (tabs == null || tabs.Count < 1)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return ProcessMnemonicHelper(charCode, SelectedTab, null);
		}

		private bool ProcessMnemonicHelper(char charCode, UltraTab startAfterTab, UltraTab stopAtTab)
		{
			VisibleTabsCollection visibleTabs = VisibleTabs;
			int num = 0;
			int count = visibleTabs.Count;
			if (startAfterTab != null)
			{
				num = startAfterTab.VisibleIndex + 1;
				if (num >= count)
				{
					startAfterTab = null;
					num = 0;
				}
			}
			for (int i = num; i < count; i++)
			{
				UltraTab ultraTab = visibleTabs[i];
				if (ultraTab.EnabledResolved && ultraTab.VisibleResolved && ultraTab.IsMnemonic(charCode))
				{
					SelectedTab = ultraTab;
					if (SelectedTab == ultraTab && TabProvider.ShowFocus && !((Control)(object)this).Focused)
					{
						InternalSetFocus();
					}
					return true;
				}
				if (stopAtTab == ultraTab)
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			if (startAfterTab != null)
			{
				return ProcessMnemonicHelper(charCode, null, startAfterTab);
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected override void ScaleControl(SizeF factor, BoundsSpecified specified)
		{
			isScaling = true;
			((Control)this).ScaleControl(factor, specified);
			isScaling = false;
		}

				return tabHeaderAreaAppearanceHolder.ShouldSerialize();
			}
			return false;
		}

		public virtual bool PerformAction(UltraTabControlAction actionCode)
		{
			if (!KeyActionMappings.IsActionAllowed(actionCode, CurrentState))
			{
				return false;
			}
			PerformingActionEventArgs e = new PerformingActionEventArgs(actionCode);
			RaiseEvent(UltraTabControlEventId.PerformingAction, e);
			if (e.Cancel)
			{
				return false;
			}
			UltraTabControlAction ultraTabControlAction = actionCode;
			actionCode = TranslateNavigationAction(actionCode);
			if (actionCode != ultraTabControlAction)
			{
				e = new PerformingActionEventArgs(actionCode);
				RaiseEvent(UltraTabControlEventId.PerformingAction, e);
				if (e.Cancel)
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			bool flag = false;
			bool flag2 = false;
			UltraTab ultraTab = null;
			switch (actionCode)
			{
			case UltraTabControlAction.ActivateFirstTab:
				ultraTab = VisibleTabs.FirstSelectableTab;
				break;
			case UltraTabControlAction.ActivateLastTab:
				ultraTab = VisibleTabs.LastSelectableTab;
				break;
			case UltraTabControlAction.ActivateNextTab:
				ultraTab = VisibleTabs.GetNextSelectableTab(ActiveTab);
				break;
			case UltraTabControlAction.ActivatePreviousTab:
				ultraTab = VisibleTabs.GetPreviousSelectableTab(ActiveTab);
				break;
			case UltraTabControlAction.ActivateTabNextPage:
				ultraTab = TabManager.GetCorrespondingTabItemOnNextPage((ITabItem)(object)ActiveTab) as UltraTab;
				break;
			case UltraTabControlAction.ActivateTabNextRow:
				ultraTab = TabManager.GetCorrespondingTabItemOnNextRow((ITabItem)(object)ActiveTab) as UltraTab;
				break;
			case UltraTabControlAction.ActivateTabPreviousPage:
				ultraTab = TabManager.GetCorrespondingTabItemOnPreviousPage((ITabItem)(object)ActiveTab) as UltraTab;
				break;
			case UltraTabControlAction.ActivateTabPreviousRow:
				ultraTab = TabManager.GetCorrespondingTabItemOnPreviousRow((ITabItem)(object)ActiveTab) as UltraTab;
				break;
			case UltraTabControlAction.SelectActiveTab:
				ultraTab = ActiveTab;
				flag = true;
				break;
			case UltraTabControlAction.SelectFirstTab:
				ultraTab = VisibleTabs.FirstSelectableTab;
				flag = true;
				break;
			case UltraTabControlAction.SelectLastTab:
				ultraTab = VisibleTabs.LastSelectableTab;
				flag = true;
				break;
			case UltraTabControlAction.SelectNextTab:
				ultraTab = VisibleTabs.GetNextSelectableTab(ActiveTab, wrap: true);
				flag = true;
				break;
			case UltraTabControlAction.SelectPreviousTab:
				ultraTab = VisibleTabs.GetPreviousSelectableTab(ActiveTab, wrap: true);
				flag = true;
				break;
			case UltraTabControlAction.SelectTabNextPage:
				ultraTab = TabManager.GetCorrespondingTabItemOnNextPage((ITabItem)(object)ActiveTab) as UltraTab;
				flag = true;
				break;
			case UltraTabControlAction.SelectTabNextRow:
				ultraTab = TabManager.GetCorrespondingTabItemOnNextRow((ITabItem)(object)ActiveTab) as UltraTab;
				flag = true;
				break;
			case UltraTabControlAction.SelectTabPreviousPage:
				ultraTab = TabManager.GetCorrespondingTabItemOnPreviousPage((ITabItem)(object)ActiveTab) as UltraTab;
				flag = true;
				break;
			case UltraTabControlAction.SelectTabPreviousRow:
				ultraTab = TabManager.GetCorrespondingTabItemOnPreviousRow((ITabItem)(object)ActiveTab) as UltraTab;
				flag = true;
				break;
			case UltraTabControlAction.CloseActiveTab:
			{
				UltraTab ultraTab2 = ActiveTab;
				if (ultraTab2 != null)
				{
					ultraTab2.Close();
					flag2 = !ultraTab2.Visible;
				}
				break;
			}
			}
			if (ultraTab != null)
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					if (ultraTab != SelectedTab)
					{
						SelectedTab = ultraTab;
						flag2 = ultraTab == SelectedTab;
					}
				}
				else if (ultraTab != ActiveTab)
				{
					ActiveTab = ultraTab;
					flag2 = ultraTab == ActiveTab;
				}
			}
			if (flag2)
			{
				RaiseEvent(UltraTabControlEventId.PerformedAction, new PerformedActionEventArgs(actionCode));
				if (actionCode != ultraTabControlAction)
				{
					RaiseEvent(UltraTabControlEventId.PerformedAction, new PerformedActionEventArgs(ultraTabControlAction));
				}
			}
			return flag2;
		}

		}

		public void ResolveClientAreaAppearance(ref AppearanceData appearance, ref AppearancePropFlags requestedProps)
		{
			//IL_000e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0013: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			UltraTab selectedTab = SelectedTab;
			if (selectedTab != null)
			{
				ResolutionOrderInfo resolutionOrder = StyleUtils.GetResolutionOrder((UltraControlBase)(object)this);
				if (selectedTab.HasClientAreaAppearance && ((ResolutionOrderInfo)(ref resolutionOrder)).UseControlInfo)
				{
					selectedTab.ClientAreaAppearance.MergeData(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
				}
				StyleUtils.ResolveAppearance((UltraControlBase)(object)this, HasClientAreaAppearance ? ClientAreaAppearance : null, IsVerticalTabOrientation ? StyleUtils.Role.ClientAreaVertical : StyleUtils.Role.ClientAreaHorizontal, (RoleState)1, ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
				if (ViewStyleResolved == ViewStyle.Office2003)
				{
					AdjustAppearancePropertiesForTabOrientation(SystemColors.Control, SystemColors.ControlLightLight, ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
					if (((ulong)requestedProps & 4uL) == 4 && !((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).HasPropertyBeenSet((AppearancePropFlags)4))
					{
						((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).BorderColor = MSColors.ToolBorder;
						requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)((ulong)requestedProps & 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFBuL);
					}
				}
				selectedTab.ResolveAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, (TabState)1, resolvingPageArea: true);
			}
			else
			{
				StyleUtils.ResolveAppearance((UltraControlBase)(object)this, HasClientAreaAppearance ? ClientAreaAppearance : null, IsVerticalTabOrientation ? StyleUtils.Role.ClientAreaVertical : StyleUtils.Role.ClientAreaHorizontal, (RoleState)1, ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
				ResolveDefaultTabAppearance(null, ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
			}
		}

		public void Scroll(ScrollType scrollType)
		{
			//IL_0027: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (!Initializing && !((Control)this).Disposing && !((Control)this).IsDisposed && ((Control)this).IsHandleCreated)
			{
				TabManager.ScrollTabs(scrollType, 1);
			}
		}

		public UltraTab TabFromPoint(Point point)
		{
			if (Initializing || ((Control)this).Disposing || ((Control)this).IsDisposed || !((Control)this).IsHandleCreated)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (TabManager.FirstDisplayedTabItem == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			((UIElement)UIElement).VerifyChildElements(true);
			UIElement val = ((UIElement)UIElement).ElementFromPoint(point);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return val.GetContext(typeof(ITabItem), true) as UltraTab;
		}

		internal UltraTabPageControl CreateTabPage(UltraTab tab)
		{
			if (!Initialized)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (((Control)this).IsDisposed || ((Control)this).Disposing)
			{
				return null;
			}
			UltraTabPageControl ultraTabPageControl = null;
			ultraTabPageControl = ((!(((Component)(object)this).GetService(typeof(IDesignerHost)) is IDesignerHost designerHost)) ? ((tab != null) ? new UltraTabPageControl() : new UltraTabSharedControlsPage()) : ((tab != null) ? (designerHost.CreateComponent(typeof(UltraTabPageControl)) as UltraTabPageControl) : (designerHost.CreateComponent(typeof(UltraTabSharedControlsPage)) as UltraTabPageControl)));
			if (tab != null)
			{
				ultraTabPageControl.InitializeTab(tab);
			}
			ultraTabPageControl.InitializeTabControl(this);
			if (!delayedInitializePending && !((Component)this).DesignMode && tab != null && tab != SelectedTab)
			{
				ultraTabPageControl.Visible = false;
			}
			ResizeTabPage(ultraTabPageControl, TabPageRect);
			return ultraTabPageControl;
		}

		internal DesignerTransaction CreateTransaction()
		{

		}

		internal DesignerTransaction CreateTransaction(string description)
		{
			IDesignerHost designerHost = ((Component)(object)this).GetService(typeof(IDesignerHost)) as IDesignerHost;
			DesignerTransaction designerTransaction = null;
			if (designerHost != null)
			{
				designerTransaction = ((description == null || description.Length <= 0) ? designerHost.CreateTransaction() : designerHost.CreateTransaction(description));
				if (designerTransaction != null)
				{
					((UltraControlBase)this).NotifyDesignEnvironmentOfChange();
				}
			}
			return designerTransaction;
		}

			}
		}

		internal string GetNewTabText()
		{
			if (defaultTabText == null)
			{
				defaultTabText = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("DefaultTabText");
			}
			int num = 1;
			while (true)
			{
				string format = defaultTabText;
				try
				{
					format = string.Format(format, num.ToString());
				}
				catch (FormatException)
				{
					break;
				}
				bool flag = false;
				foreach (UltraTab tab in Tabs)
				{
					if (tab.Text == format)
					{
						flag = true;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					num++;
					continue;
				}
				return format;
			}
			return "";
		}

		internal Region GetTabPageRegion()
		{
			if (!((Control)this).Created || ((Control)this).IsDisposed || ((Control)this).Disposing || ((Control)this).ClientRectangle.IsEmpty)
			{
				return null;
			}
			switch (StyleResolved)
			{
			case UltraTabControlStyle.Wizard:
				if (!((Component)this).DesignMode)
				{
					return null;
				}
				break;
			default:
				return null;
			case UltraTabControlStyle.NotePage:
			case UltraTabControlStyle.NotePageFlat:
				break;
			}
			Region region = null;
			((UIElement)mainElement).VerifyChildElements(false);
			Rectangle tabPageRect = TabPageRect;
			if (tabPageRect.IsEmpty)
			{
				return null;
			}
			Point location = tabPageRect.Location;
			tabPageRect.Offset(-location.X, -location.Y);
			UIElementEnumerator enumerator = ((UIElement)mainElement).ChildElements.GetEnumerator();
			try
			{
				while (enumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					UIElement current = enumerator.Current;
					if (!(current is NotePageEarMarkUIElement) && !(current is TabPageNumberUIElement) && !(current is WizardNavigationUIElement))
					{
						continue;
					}
					Rectangle rect = current.Rect;
					rect.Offset(-location.X, -location.Y);
					Rectangle rect2 = Rectangle.Intersect(rect, tabPageRect);
					if (!rect2.IsEmpty)
					{
						if (region == null)
						{
							region = new Region(tabPageRect);
						}
						region.Exclude(rect2);
					}
				}
				return region;
			}
			finally
			{
				if (enumerator is IDisposable disposable)
				{
					disposable.Dispose();
				}
			}
		}

		internal bool InternalSetFocus()
		{
			if (!((Component)this).DesignMode)
			{
				try
				{
					if (DisposableObject.HasSamePublicKey(((object)this).GetType()))
					{
						return TabUtilities.FocusControl((Control)(object)this);
					}
					return ((Control)this).Focus();
				}
				catch (SecurityException)
				{
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		internal bool InternalValidateContainer()
		{
			if (Initialized && ((Control)this).IsHandleCreated && !((Control)this).Disposing && !((Control)this).IsDisposed && !((Component)this).DesignMode && ((Control)this).CausesValidation)
			{
				try
				{
					if (((Control)this).GetContainerControl() is ContainerControl containerControl && !containerControl.Validate())
					{
						ResetValidationCancelled();
						return false;
					}
				}
				catch (SecurityException)
				{
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		internal void InvalidateActiveTab()
		{
			UltraTab ultraTab = ActiveTab;
			if (ultraTab != null)
			{
				TabManager.InvalidateTabItem((ITabItem)(object)ultraTab);
			}
		}

		internal void OnAppStyleChanged()
		{
			if (((Control)this).Disposing || ((Control)this).IsDisposed)
			{
				return;
			}
			SyncBackColor();
			if (TabManager != null)
			{
				TabManager.Dirty(true, false, true);
				TabManager.DirtyFont();
			}
			if (mainElement != null)
			{
				((UIElement)mainElement).DirtyChildElements();
			}
			PositionTabPages(sharedAndSelectedTabsOnly: false, Initialized);
			if (!firstDraw)
			{
				UltraTab selectedTab = SelectedTab;
				if (selectedTab != null && selectedTab.TabPage != null)
				{
					((Control)(object)selectedTab.TabPage).Invalidate();
				}
				else if (sharedControlsPage != null)
				{
					((Control)(object)sharedControlsPage).Invalidate();
				}
			}
		}

		internal void OnFirstCreated()
		{
			UltraTabPageControl ultraTabPageControl = SharedControlsPage;
			if (ultraTabPageControl != null)
			{
				initializing = false;
			}
		}

		internal void OnPropertiesChanged()
		{
			((UltraControlBase)this).NotifyDesignEnvironmentOfChange();
		}

		internal void PositionTabPages()
		{
			PositionTabPages(sharedAndSelectedTabsOnly: true, clearOldRegions: false);
		}

		internal void PositionTabPages(bool sharedAndSelectedTabsOnly)
		{
			PositionTabPages(sharedAndSelectedTabsOnly, clearOldRegions: false);
		}

		internal void PositionTabPages(bool sharedAndSelectedTabsOnly, bool clearOldRegions)
		{
			if (((UltraControlBase)this).IsUpdating || initializing || delayedInitializePending)
			{
				return;
			}
			Rectangle tabPageRect = TabPageRect;
			Region tabPageRegion = GetTabPageRegion();
			if (sharedControlsPage != null)
			{
				ResizeTabPage(sharedControlsPage, tabPageRect);
				if (tabPageRegion != null || clearOldRegions)
				{
					sharedControlsPage.SetRegion(tabPageRegion);
				}
			}
			if (!(this is UltraTabControl))
			{
				return;
			}
			UltraTab selectedTab = SelectedTab;
			if (sharedAndSelectedTabsOnly)
			{
				if (selectedTab != null && selectedTab.TabPage != null && selectedTab.TabPage != sharedControlsPage)
				{
					ResizeTabPage(selectedTab.TabPage, tabPageRect);
					if (tabPageRegion != null || clearOldRegions)
					{
						selectedTab.TabPage.SetRegion(tabPageRegion);
					}
				}
				return;
			}
			foreach (UltraTab tab in Tabs)
			{
				ResizeTabPage(tab.TabPage, tabPageRect);
				if (selectedTab == tab && selectedTab.TabPage != null && selectedTab.TabPage != sharedControlsPage)
				{
					tab.TabPage.SetRegion(tabPageRegion);
				}
				else if (clearOldRegions && tabPageRegion == null)
				{
					tab.TabPage.SetRegion(null);
				}
			}
		}

		internal void ReparentSharedControls()
		{
			ReparentSharedControlsToTab(SelectedTab);
		}

		internal void ReparentSharedControlsToTab(UltraTab tab)
		{
			ReparentSharedControlsToTab(tab, null);
		}

		internal void ReparentSharedControlsToTab(UltraTab tab, UltraTab prevTab)
		{
			if (sharedControls == null || sharedControls.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				sharedControls.RefreshZorder();
				UltraTabPageControl ultraTabPageControl = null;
				if (tab != null)
				{
					ultraTabPageControl = tab.TabPage;
				}
				IButtonControl buttonControl = null;
				IButtonControl buttonControl2 = null;
				Form form = null;
				try
				{
					form = ((Control)this).FindForm();
					if (form != null)
					{
						buttonControl = form.AcceptButton;
						buttonControl2 = form.CancelButton;
					}
				}
				catch (SecurityException)
				{
				}
				Control.ControlCollection controls = ((Control)(object)SharedControlsPage).Controls;
				Point point = ((prevTab != null && prevTab.HasTabPage) ? ((ScrollableControl)(object)prevTab.TabPage).AutoScrollPosition : Point.Empty);
				foreach (Control sharedControl in sharedControls)
				{
					Control control = (sharedControlBeingReparented = sharedControl);
					if (!controls.Contains(control))
					{
						if (!point.IsEmpty)
						{
							Point location = control.Location;
							location.Offset(-point.X, -point.Y);
							control.Location = location;
						}
						controls.Add(control);
					}
					sharedControlBeingReparented = null;
				}
				ArrayList arrayList = sharedControls.CloneList();
				if (arrayList != null)
				{
					int num = 0;
					for (int i = 0; i < arrayList.Count; i++)
					{
						Control control2 = arrayList[i] as Control;
						int num2 = controls.IndexOf(control2);
						if (num2 < 0)
						{
							num++;
						}
						else if (num2 != i)
						{
							controls.SetChildIndex(control2, Math.Max(0, i - num));
						}
					}
				}
				if (ultraTabPageControl != null && tab != null)
				{
					Point autoScrollPosition = ((ScrollableControl)(object)ultraTabPageControl).AutoScrollPosition;
					foreach (Control sharedControl2 in sharedControls)
					{
						Control control3 = (sharedControlBeingReparented = sharedControl2);
						if ((!tab.HasExcludedSharedControls || !tab.ExcludedSharedControls.Contains(control3)) && !((Control)(object)ultraTabPageControl).Controls.Contains(control3))
						{
							Point location2 = control3.Location;
							location2.Offset(autoScrollPosition);
							control3.Location = location2;
							ultraTabPageControl.HasReparentedSharedControls = true;
							((Control)(object)ultraTabPageControl).Controls.Add(control3);
						}
						sharedControlBeingReparented = null;
					}
				}
				if (form == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				try
				{
					if (buttonControl != null && form.AcceptButton == null)
					{
						form.AcceptButton = buttonControl;
					}
					if (buttonControl2 != null && form.CancelButton == null)
					{
						form.CancelButton = buttonControl2;
					}
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				sharedControlBeingReparented = null;
			}
		}

			}

		}

		internal void ResizeTabPage(UltraTabPageControl tabPage, Rectangle tabPageRect)
		{
			if (((UltraControlBase)this).IsUpdating || tabPage == null || tabPage.TabControl != this)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!(this is UltraTabStripControl) && ((Component)this).DesignMode && tabPage.Tab != SelectedTab)
			{
				tabPageRect.X = Math.Min(-10000, -tabPageRect.Width);
				tabPageRect.Y = Math.Min(-10000, -tabPageRect.Height);
			}
			if (((Control)(object)tabPage).Bounds != tabPageRect)
			{
				((Control)(object)tabPage).Bounds = tabPageRect;
				if (!((Component)this).DesignMode)
				{
					RaiseEvent(UltraTabControlEventId.TabPageResized, new TabPageResizedEventArgs(tabPage));
				}
			}
		}

		internal void SetSelectedTab(UltraTab tab)
		{
			if (ConvertingInboxTab)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (tab != null && !tab.VisibleResolved)
			{
				tab = null;
			}
			if ((tab == null || tab.TabControl == this) && SelectedTab != tab)
			{
				if (initializing || delayedInitializePending)
				{
					persistedSelectedTab = tab;
					activeTab = tab;
				}
				else
				{
					TabManager.SelectedTabItem = (ITabItem)(object)tab;
				}
			}
		}

		private void SetTabPageRegion()
		{
			Region tabPageRegion = GetTabPageRegion();
			if (tabPageRegion != null)
			{
				UltraTab selectedTab = SelectedTab;
				if (selectedTab != null && selectedTab.TabPage != null)
				{
					selectedTab.TabPage.SetRegion(tabPageRegion);
				}
				else if (SharedControlsPage != null)
				{
					SharedControlsPage.SetRegion(tabPageRegion);
				}
			}
		}

		internal void ShowSharedControlsPage()
		{
			SelectedTab = null;
		}

		}

		internal void AdjustAppearancePropertiesForTabOrientation(Color topColor, Color bottomColor, ref AppearanceData appearance, ref AppearancePropFlags requestedProps)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0011: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0012: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

			//IL_003f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0047: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

			//IL_00f1: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			GradientStyle backGradientStyle = (GradientStyle)1;

			Color backColor2 = bottomColor;
			TabOrientation tabOrientationRTLResolved = TabManager.TabOrientationRTLResolved;
			switch ((int)tabOrientationRTLResolved)
			{
			case 0:
			case 1:
			case 2:
				backGradientStyle = (GradientStyle)2;
				backColor = topColor;
				backColor2 = bottomColor;
				break;
			case 3:
			case 4:
				backGradientStyle = (GradientStyle)2;
				backColor = bottomColor;
				backColor2 = topColor;
				break;
			case 5:
			case 6:
				backGradientStyle = (GradientStyle)3;
				backColor = topColor;
				backColor2 = bottomColor;
				break;
			case 7:
			case 8:
				backGradientStyle = (GradientStyle)3;
				backColor = bottomColor;
				backColor2 = topColor;
				break;
			}
			if (((ulong)requestedProps & 1uL) == 1 && !((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).HasPropertyBeenSet((AppearancePropFlags)1) && (StyleResolved != UltraTabControlStyle.VisualStudio2005 || ViewStyleResolved != ViewStyle.VisualStudio2005))
