		private Control GetActiveControl()
		{
			try
			{
				return GetActiveControl(((Control)this).GetContainerControl());
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
			}
			return null;
		}

		private Control GetActiveControl(IContainerControl container)
		{
			try
			{
				if (container != null)
				{
					Control activeControl = container.ActiveControl;
					if (activeControl != null)
					{
						if (activeControl.Focused)
						{
							return activeControl;
						}
						if (activeControl is IContainerControl)
						{
							Control activeControl2 = GetActiveControl((IContainerControl)activeControl);
							if (activeControl2 != null)
							{
								return activeControl2;
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
			}
			return null;
		}

		private void ReactivateControlIfVisible(Control controlToActivate)
		{
			if (controlToActivate == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				IContainerControl containerControl = ((Control)this).GetContainerControl();
				if (containerControl != null && controlToActivate != containerControl.ActiveControl && controlToActivate.Visible)
				{
					containerControl.ActiveControl = controlToActivate;
				}
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
			}
		}

		private Image GetCachedImage(CachedImageIndex index)
		{
			if (cachedImages[(int)index] == null)
			{
				string name;
				switch (index)
				{
				case CachedImageIndex.NotePageEarMarkTopLeft:
					name = "NotePageEarMark_TopLeft.ico";
					break;
				case CachedImageIndex.NotePageEarMarkTopRight:
					name = "NotePageEarMark_TopRight.ico";
					break;
				case CachedImageIndex.ArrowLeft:
					name = "ArrowLeft.ico";
					break;
				case CachedImageIndex.ArrowRight:
					name = "ArrowRight.ico";
					break;
				default:
					return null;
				}
				cachedImageStreams[(int)index] = typeof(UltraTabControlBase).Module.Assembly.GetManifestResourceStream(typeof(UltraTabControlBase), name);
				Stream stream = cachedImageStreams[(int)index];
				if (stream != null)
				{
					cachedImages[(int)index] = Image.FromStream(stream);
				}
			}
			return cachedImages[(int)index];
		}

		private void Save(Stream stream, bool xml)
		{
			if (stream == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_7"), Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_8"));
			}
			IFormatter formatter = CreateFormatter(xml);
			ObjectStreamer graph = CreateObjectStreamer();
			formatter.Serialize(stream, graph);
		}

		private IFormatter CreateFormatter(bool xml)
		{
			if (xml)
			{
				SoapFormatter soapFormatter = new SoapFormatter();
				soapFormatter.AssemblyFormat = FormatterAssemblyStyle.Simple;
				return soapFormatter;
			}
			BinaryFormatter binaryFormatter = new BinaryFormatter();
			binaryFormatter.AssemblyFormat = FormatterAssemblyStyle.Simple;
			return binaryFormatter;
		}

		private void Load(Stream stream, bool xml)
		{
			if (stream == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_7"), Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_8"));
			}
			IFormatter formatter = CreateFormatter(xml);
			formatter.Binder = (SerializationBinder)(object)new Infragistics.Win.UltraWinTabControl.Serialization.Binder();
			ObjectStreamer objectStreamer = formatter.Deserialize(stream) as ObjectStreamer;
			if (objectStreamer.AssemblyName == null || objectStreamer.AssemblyName.IndexOf("Infragistics.Win.UltraWinTabControl") < 0)
			{
				throw new InvalidCastException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_9"));
			}
			Tabs.ClearVisibleOrderSerializers();
			int count = Tabs.Count;
			foreach (DictionaryEntry item in objectStreamer.Dictionary)
			{
				if (item.Value is SerializationEntry)
				{
					SerializationEntry entry = (SerializationEntry)item.Value;
					if (Utils.DeserializeProperty(entry, typeof(UltraTabVisibleOrderSerializer), null) is UltraTabVisibleOrderSerializer { Index: >=0 } ultraTabVisibleOrderSerializer && ultraTabVisibleOrderSerializer.Index < count)
					{
						Tabs[ultraTabVisibleOrderSerializer.Index].VisibleOrderSerializer = ultraTabVisibleOrderSerializer;
					}
				}
			}
			Tabs.CacheVisibleIndices();
			VisibleTabs.Sort(new VisibleOrderSortComparer());
			Tabs.ClearVisibleOrderSerializers();
		}

		private ObjectStreamer CreateObjectStreamer()
		{
			ObjectStreamer objectStreamer = new ObjectStreamer("Infragistics.Win.UltraWinTabControl", 25);
			IDictionary dictionary = objectStreamer.Dictionary;
			foreach (UltraTab tab in Tabs)
			{
				dictionary.Add(tab.Index.ToString(), tab.CreateVisibleOrderSerializer());
			}
			return objectStreamer;
		}

		private void HookComponentChangeService()
		{
			if (!hookedIntoComponentChangeService && ((Component)(object)this).GetService(typeof(IComponentChangeService)) is IComponentChangeService componentChangeService)
			{
				hookedIntoComponentChangeService = true;
				componentChangeService.ComponentRemoving += OnComponentRemoving;
				componentChangeService.ComponentRemoved += OnComponentRemoved;
			}
		}

		private void InternalFontChanged()
		{
			if (tabManager != null)
			{
				tabManager.DirtyFont();
			}
			PositionTabPages(sharedAndSelectedTabsOnly: false);
		}

		private void OnComponentRemoved(object sender, ComponentEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!((Control)this).Disposing && !((Control)this).IsDisposed && !Initializing && e.Component is UltraTabPageControl ultraTabPageControl && ultraTabPageControl.TabControl == this && ultraTabPageControl.Tab != null && !((Control)(object)ultraTabPageControl).Disposing && !((Control)(object)ultraTabPageControl).IsDisposed)
			{
				Tabs.Remove(ultraTabPageControl.Tab);
				((Control)this).BeginInvoke((Delegate)new MethodInvoker(SelectTabPageAtDesignTime));
			}
		}

		private void OnComponentRemoving(object sender, ComponentEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Component == this)
			{
				UnhookComponentChangeService();
			}
			else if (!((Control)this).Disposing && !((Control)this).IsDisposed && !Initializing && e.Component is UltraTabPageControl ultraTabPageControl && ultraTabPageControl.TabControl == this && !((Control)(object)ultraTabPageControl).Disposing && !((Control)(object)ultraTabPageControl).IsDisposed)
			{
				if (e.Component is UltraTabSharedControlsPage && !IsUndoInProgress())
				{
					throw new ArgumentException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_10"));
				}
				if (ultraTabPageControl.Tab != null)
				{
					((UltraControlBase)this).NotifyDesignEnvironmentOfChange();
				}
			}
		}

		private void OnTabControlAddedAfterLoad()
		{
			if (((Component)this).DesignMode)
			{
				HookComponentChangeService();
			}
		}

		private void PerformDelayedInitialize()
		{
			if (!delayedInitializePending)
			{
				return;
			}
			delayedInitializePending = false;
			if (!(this is UltraTabStripControl))
			{
				if (((Component)this).DesignMode)
				{
					PositionTabPages(sharedAndSelectedTabsOnly: false);
				}
				else
				{
					SharedControlsPage.Visible = false;
					UltraTab ultraTab = persistedSelectedTab;
					foreach (UltraTab tab in Tabs)
					{
						tab.TabPage.Visible = ultraTab == tab;
					}
				}
			}
			if (persistedSelectedTab != null)
			{
				TabManager.SelectedTabItem = (ITabItem)(object)persistedSelectedTab;
			}
			if (SelectedTab == null)
			{
				PerformAction(UltraTabControlAction.SelectFirstTab);
			}
			persistedSelectedTab = null;
			delayedInitializePerformed = true;
		}

		internal void ResetValidationCancelled()
		{
			if (Environment.Version.Major < 2 || !canAccessValidationCancelled)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				BindingFlags bindingFlags = BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic;
				PropertyInfo property = typeof(Control).GetProperty("ValidationCancelled", bindingFlags);
				property.SetValue(this, false, bindingFlags, null, null, null);
			}
			catch
			{
				canAccessValidationCancelled = false;
			}
		}

		private void SelectTabPageAtDesignTime()
		{
			if (!((Component)this).DesignMode || this is UltraTabStripControl || !(((Component)(object)this).GetService(typeof(ISelectionService)) is ISelectionService selectionService))
			{
				return;
			}
			ICollection selectedComponents = selectionService.GetSelectedComponents();
			UltraTabPageControl ultraTabPageControl = null;
			UltraTab selectedTab = SelectedTab;
			ultraTabPageControl = ((selectedTab == null) ? SharedControlsPage : selectedTab.TabPage);
			if (ultraTabPageControl == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (selectedComponents.Count == 1)
			{
				foreach (Component item in selectedComponents)
				{
					if ((object)item == ultraTabPageControl)
					{
						return;
					}
				}
			}
			ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList(1);
			arrayList.Add(ultraTabPageControl);
			selectionService.SetSelectedComponents(arrayList, SelectionTypes.Replace);
		}

		private void SetRedraw(bool setRedrawOn, Control control)
		{
			try
			{
				if (!control.Disposing && !control.IsDisposed && control.IsHandleCreated && !((Control)this).Disposing && !((Control)this).IsDisposed && Initialized && ((Control)this).IsHandleCreated && (((Control)this).Visible || ((Component)this).DesignMode))
				{
					SecurityPermission securityPermission = new SecurityPermission(SecurityPermissionFlag.UnmanagedCode);
					if (Utilities.CanAssert)
					{
						securityPermission.Assert();
					}
					NativeWindowMethods.SendMessageApi(control.Handle, 11, setRedrawOn ? NativeWindowMethods.TRUE : NativeWindowMethods.FALSE, IntPtr.Zero);
					if (setRedrawOn && control.Visible)
					{
						control.Invalidate(invalidateChildren: true);
					}
				}
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
			}
		}

		private UltraTabControlAction TranslateNavigationAction(UltraTabControlAction actionCode)
		{
			//IL_0008: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00c9: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00ca: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00cc: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00cf: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00f5: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			//IL_007d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_007e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_007f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0081: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00a7: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			//IL_014c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_014d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_014f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0152: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0178: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			//IL_0116: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0117: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0119: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_011c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0142: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			bool flag = false;
			TabOrientation tabOrientationRTLResolved = TabManager.TabOrientationRTLResolved;
			switch (actionCode)
			{
			default:
				return actionCode;
			case UltraTabControlAction.NavigateNextTab:
				actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.ActivateNextTab;
				break;
			case UltraTabControlAction.NavigatePreviousTab:
				actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.ActivatePreviousTab;
				break;
			case UltraTabControlAction.NavigateNextRow:
				actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.ActivateTabNextRow;
				break;
			case UltraTabControlAction.NavigatePreviousRow:
				actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.ActivateTabPreviousRow;
				break;
			case UltraTabControlAction.NavigateToFirstTab:
				actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.ActivateFirstTab;
				break;
			case UltraTabControlAction.NavigateToLastTab:
				actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.ActivateLastTab;
				break;
			case UltraTabControlAction.NavigateUp:
				flag = true;
				goto case UltraTabControlAction.NavigateDown;
			case UltraTabControlAction.NavigateDown:
			{
				TabOrientation val4 = tabOrientationRTLResolved;
				switch (val4 - 1)
				{
				default:
					actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.ActivateTabPreviousRow;
					break;
				case 2:
				case 3:
					actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.ActivateTabNextRow;
					break;
				case 4:
				case 6:
					actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.ActivateNextTab;
					break;
				case 5:
				case 7:
					actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.ActivatePreviousTab;
					break;
				}
				break;
			}
			case UltraTabControlAction.NavigateRight:
				flag = true;
				goto case UltraTabControlAction.NavigateLeft;
			case UltraTabControlAction.NavigateLeft:
			{
				TabOrientation val3 = tabOrientationRTLResolved;
				switch (val3 - 1)
				{
				default:
					actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.ActivatePreviousTab;
					break;
				case 1:
				case 3:
					actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.ActivateNextTab;
					break;
				case 4:
				case 5:
					actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.ActivateTabNextRow;
					break;
				case 6:
				case 7:
					actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.ActivateTabPreviousRow;
					break;
				}
				break;
			}
			case UltraTabControlAction.NavigatePageUp:
				flag = true;
				goto case UltraTabControlAction.NavigatePageDown;
			case UltraTabControlAction.NavigatePageDown:
				if (IsMultiRow)
				{
					TabOrientation val = tabOrientationRTLResolved;
					switch (val - 1)
					{
					default:
						actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.ActivateTabPreviousPage;
						break;
					case 2:
					case 3:
					case 6:
					case 7:
						actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.ActivateTabNextPage;
						break;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					TabOrientation val2 = tabOrientationRTLResolved;
					switch (val2 - 1)
					{
					default:
						actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.ActivateTabNextPage;
						break;
					case 1:
					case 3:
					case 5:
					case 7:
						actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.ActivateTabPreviousPage;
						break;
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				switch (actionCode)
				{
				case UltraTabControlAction.ActivateNextTab:
					actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.ActivatePreviousTab;
					break;
				case UltraTabControlAction.ActivatePreviousTab:
					actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.ActivateNextTab;
					break;
				case UltraTabControlAction.ActivateTabPreviousPage:
					actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.ActivateTabNextPage;
					break;
				case UltraTabControlAction.ActivateTabNextPage:
					actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.ActivateTabPreviousPage;
					break;
				case UltraTabControlAction.ActivateTabNextRow:
					actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.ActivateTabPreviousRow;
					break;
				case UltraTabControlAction.ActivateTabPreviousRow:
					actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.ActivateTabNextRow;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (NavigationStyleResolved == NavigationStyle.AutoSelect)
			{
				switch (actionCode)
				{
				case UltraTabControlAction.ActivateFirstTab:
					actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.SelectFirstTab;
					break;
				case UltraTabControlAction.ActivateLastTab:
					actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.SelectLastTab;
					break;
				case UltraTabControlAction.ActivateNextTab:
					actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.SelectNextTab;
					break;
				case UltraTabControlAction.ActivatePreviousTab:
					actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.SelectPreviousTab;
					break;
				case UltraTabControlAction.ActivateTabNextPage:
					actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.SelectTabNextPage;
					break;
				case UltraTabControlAction.ActivateTabNextRow:
					actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.SelectTabNextRow;
					break;
				case UltraTabControlAction.ActivateTabPreviousPage:
					actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.SelectTabPreviousPage;
					break;
				case UltraTabControlAction.ActivateTabPreviousRow:
					actionCode = UltraTabControlAction.SelectTabPreviousRow;
					break;
				}
			}
			return actionCode;
		}

		private void UnhookComponentChangeService()
		{
			if (hookedIntoComponentChangeService && ((Component)(object)this).GetService(typeof(IComponentChangeService)) is IComponentChangeService componentChangeService)
			{
				hookedIntoComponentChangeService = false;
				componentChangeService.ComponentRemoving -= OnComponentRemoving;
				componentChangeService.ComponentRemoved -= OnComponentRemoved;
			}
		}

		private UltraTab GetSelectedTab(bool allowSelectionReplacement)
		{
			if (!Initialized || delayedInitializePending)
			{
				return persistedSelectedTab;
			}
			if (allowSelectionReplacement && ((Component)this).DesignMode && IsBound)
			{
				if (TabManager.SelectedTabItem != null)
				{
					TabManager.SelectedTabItem = null;
				}
				return null;
			}
			return TabManager.SelectedTabItem as UltraTab;
		}

		private bool IsUndoInProgress()
		{
			bool result = false;
			Type type = Type.GetType("System.ComponentModel.Design.UndoEngine, System.Design, Version=2.0.0.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b03f5f7f11d50a3a", throwOnError: false);
			if (type != null)
			{
				object service = ((Component)(object)this).GetService(type);
				if (service != null)
				{
					PropertyInfo property = type.GetProperty("UndoInProgress");
					if (property != null)
					{
						result = (bool)property.GetValue(service, null);
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		}

		}

		{

			{
				((Control)this).AccessibilityNotifyClients(accEvent, childID);
			}
		}

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		public Rectangle CalculateTabPageRect()
		{
			//IL_00be: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00c3: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00c5: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00c8: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00ee: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			//IL_027c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0288: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0296: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_029c: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			Rectangle clientRectangle = ((Control)this).ClientRectangle;
			if (clientRectangle.IsEmpty)
			{
				return Rectangle.Empty;
			}
			int tabHeaderAreaExtent = TabHeaderAreaExtent;
			int borderWidth = BorderWidth;
			if (borderWidth > 0)
			{
				Border3DSide borderSides = GetBorderSides(honorRightToLeft: false);
				if (borderSides == Border3DSide.All)
				{
					clientRectangle.Inflate(-borderWidth, -borderWidth);
				}
				else
				{
					if ((borderSides & Border3DSide.Top) != 0)
					{
						clientRectangle.Y += borderWidth;
						clientRectangle.Height -= borderWidth;
					}
					if ((borderSides & Border3DSide.Bottom) != 0)
					{
						clientRectangle.Height -= borderWidth;
					}
					if ((borderSides & Border3DSide.Left) != 0)
					{
						clientRectangle.X += borderWidth;
						clientRectangle.Width -= borderWidth;
					}
					if ((borderSides & Border3DSide.Right) != 0)
					{
						clientRectangle.Width -= borderWidth;
					}
				}
			}
			if (tabHeaderAreaExtent > 0)
			{
				TabOrientation val = tabOrientation;
				switch (val - 1)
				{
				default:
					clientRectangle.Y += tabHeaderAreaExtent;
					clientRectangle.Height -= tabHeaderAreaExtent;
					break;
				case 4:
				case 5:
					clientRectangle.X += tabHeaderAreaExtent;
					clientRectangle.Width -= tabHeaderAreaExtent;
					break;
				case 6:
				case 7:
					clientRectangle.Width -= tabHeaderAreaExtent;
					break;
				case 2:
				case 3:
					clientRectangle.Height -= tabHeaderAreaExtent;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (tabPageMargins != null)
			{
				if (tabPageMargins.Top > 0)
				{
					clientRectangle.Y += tabPageMargins.Top;
					clientRectangle.Height -= tabPageMargins.Top;
				}
				if (tabPageMargins.Bottom > 0)
				{
					clientRectangle.Height -= tabPageMargins.Bottom;
				}
				if (tabPageMargins.Left > 0)
				{
					clientRectangle.X += tabPageMargins.Left;
					clientRectangle.Width -= tabPageMargins.Left;
				}
				if (tabPageMargins.Right > 0)
				{
					clientRectangle.Width -= tabPageMargins.Right;
				}
			}
			switch (StyleResolved)
			{
			case UltraTabControlStyle.PropertyPage:
			case UltraTabControlStyle.PropertyPageSelected:
				if (((UltraControlBase)this).UseOsThemesResolved && XPThemes.ThemesSupported && XPThemes.IsThemeActive && XPThemes.AppControlsThemeThemed)
				{
					AppearanceData appearance = default(AppearanceData);
					AppearancePropFlags requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)2147483648u;
					ResolveClientAreaAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
					if ((int)((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).ThemedElementAlpha != 3)
					{
						clientRectangle.Offset(-1, -1);
					}
				}
				break;
			case UltraTabControlStyle.NotePage:
			case UltraTabControlStyle.NotePageFlat:
				if (tabPageMargins == null || tabPageMargins.Top < 0)
				{
					clientRectangle.Y += NotePageEarMarkTopLeft.Height;
					clientRectangle.Height -= NotePageEarMarkTopLeft.Height;
				}
				break;
			}
			return clientRectangle;
		}

		internal int AdjustForTouchMode(int extent)
		{
			return AdjustForTouchMode(extent, 0);
		}

		internal int AdjustForTouchMode(int extent, int padding)
		{
			return AdjustForTouchMode(extent, adjustByWidth: true, padding);
		}

		internal int AdjustForTouchMode(int extent, bool adjustByWidth = true, int padding = 0)
		{
			UltraTabControlUIElement uIElement = UIElement;
			if (uIElement != null)
			{
				return ((ControlUIElementBase)uIElement).AdjustForTouchMode(extent, adjustByWidth, padding);
			}
			return extent + padding;
		}

		internal Size AdjustForTouchMode(Size size)
		{
			UltraTabControlUIElement uIElement = UIElement;
			if (uIElement != null)
			{
				return ((ControlUIElementBase)uIElement).AdjustForTouchMode(size);
			}
			return size;
		}

		protected override void OnTouchProviderRegistrationChanged(ITouchProvider touchProvider, int touchProviderListVersion)
		{
			if (tabManager != null)
			{
				tabManager.Dirty(true, false, true);
			}
			PositionTabPages(sharedAndSelectedTabsOnly: false, Initialized);
			((UltraControlBase)this).OnTouchProviderRegistrationChanged(touchProvider, touchProviderListVersion);
		}

		protected override void OnTouchProviderStateChange(bool invalidate)
		{
			if (tabManager != null)
			{
				tabManager.Dirty(true, false, true);
			}
			PositionTabPages(sharedAndSelectedTabsOnly: false, Initialized);
			((UltraControlBase)this).OnTouchProviderStateChange(invalidate);
		}

		private void DirtyChildElementRecursive(UIElement element)
		{
			element.DirtyChildElements();
			UIElementEnumerator enumerator = element.ChildElements.GetEnumerator();
			try
			{
				while (enumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					UIElement current = enumerator.Current;
					DirtyChildElementRecursive(current);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				if (enumerator is IDisposable disposable)
				{
					disposable.Dispose();
				}
			}
		}

		internal Border3DSide GetBorderSides(bool honorRightToLeft)
		{
			//IL_0016: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001e: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_004d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0040: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0052: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0053: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0054: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0055: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0057: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_007d: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			if (StyleResolved == UltraTabControlStyle.NotePage)
			{
				return Border3DSide.All;
			}
			UIElementBorderStyle tabPageBorderStyle = TabManager.TabPageBorderStyle;
			if ((int)tabPageBorderStyle == 1)
			{
				return (Border3DSide)0;
			}
			if (Tabs.VisibleTabsCount < 1)
			{
				return Border3DSide.All;
			}
			Border3DSide border3DSide = Border3DSide.All;
			TabOrientation val = (honorRightToLeft ? tabManager.TabOrientationRTLResolved : TabOrientation);
			TabOrientation val2 = val;
			switch (val2 - 1)
			{
			default:
				return border3DSide & ~Border3DSide.Top;
			case 2:
			case 3:
				return border3DSide & ~Border3DSide.Bottom;
			case 4:
			case 5:
				return border3DSide & ~Border3DSide.Left;
			case 6:
			case 7:
				return border3DSide & ~Border3DSide.Right;
			}
		}

		protected override IRawElementProviderFragment CreateUIAutomationProvider(object context)
		{
			if (context == this)
			{
				return ((FragmentUIAProviderStub)new UltraTabControlBaseUIAutomationProviderStub(this)).Provider;
			}
			return ((UltraControlBase)this).CreateUIAutomationProvider(context);
		}
	}
	[ToolboxBitmap(typeof(UltraTabControl), "ToolboxBitmaps.CLR2.UltraTabControl.png")]
	[LicenseProvider(typeof(UltraLicenseProvider))]
	[UltraLicense("UltraTabControl")]
	[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_UltraTabControl")]
	public class UltraTabControl : UltraTabControlBase, IUltraLicensedComponent, ICodeDomSerializableEX, ICodeDomSerializable
	{
		private UltraLicense license;

		private bool serializing;

		[ParenthesizePropertyName(true)]
		[DesignOnly(true)]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_UltraTabControl_P_About")]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedCategory("LC_Design")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		[Editor(typeof(AboutDialogEditor), typeof(UITypeEditor))]
		public object About => null;

		UltraLicense IUltraLicensedComponent.License => license;

		internal bool Serializing => serializing;

		public UltraTabControl()
		{
			try
			{
				license = LicenseManager.Validate(typeof(UltraTabControl), this) as UltraLicense;
			}
			catch (FileNotFoundException)
			{
			}
		}

		void ICodeDomSerializable.BeforeSerialize(CodeDomSerializationInfo serializationInfo)
		{
			serializing = true;
			ReparentSharedControlsToTab(null);
		}

		void ICodeDomSerializable.AfterSerialize(CodeDomSerializationInfo serializationInfo)
		{
			serializing = false;
		}

		void ICodeDomSerializable.AfterDeserialize(IDesignerSerializationManager manager)
		{
		}

		void ICodeDomSerializableEX.AfterSerializationCompleted(IDesignerSerializationManager manager)
		{
			ReparentSharedControlsToTab(base.SelectedTab);
		}

		void ICodeDomSerializableEX.AfterDeserializationCompleted(IDesignerSerializationManager manager)
		{
		}

		protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing && license != null)
			{
				license.Dispose();
				license = null;
			}
			base.Dispose(disposing);
		}

		protected override ComponentRole CreateComponentRole()
		{
			return (ComponentRole)(object)new WinTabRole((UltraControlBase)(object)this, StyleUtils.ComponentType.TabControl);
		}

		protected override void OnOffice2007ColorSchemeChanged()
		{
			if (base.ViewStyleResolved == ViewStyle.Office2007)
			{
				((UltraControlBase)this).Invalidate(true);
			}
		}

		internal override UltraTab AddNewTab()
		{
			return base.Tabs.Add();
		}
	}

	public class UltraTabControlUIElement : ControlUIElementBase
	{
		private UltraTabControlBase tabControl;

		private WizardNavigationUIElement wizardUIElement;

		private TabPageNumberUIElement pageNumberElement;

		private UIElement tabAreaUIElement;

		private TabPageAreaUIElement tabPageAreaUIElement;

		public override UIElementBorderStyle BorderStyle
		{
			get
			{
				if (tabControl.StyleResolved != UltraTabControlStyle.NotePage)
				{
					if (tabControl.StyleResolved != UltraTabControlStyle.VisualStudio2005)
					{
						return (UIElementBorderStyle)1;
					}
					return (UIElementBorderStyle)4;
				}
				return (UIElementBorderStyle)6;
			}
		}

		protected override long CurrentState => (long)tabControl.CurrentState;

		protected override KeyActionMappingsBase KeyActionMappings => (KeyActionMappingsBase)(object)tabControl.KeyActionMappings;

		public override UIRole UIRole => StyleUtils.GetMainRole((UltraControlBase)(object)TabControl);

		public UltraTabControlBase TabControl => tabControl;

		private NotePageEarMarkUIElement LeftEarMarkUIElement
		{

					leftEarMarkUIElement = new NotePageEarMarkUIElement((UIElement)(object)this, tabControl, previous: true);
				}
				return leftEarMarkUIElement;
			}
		}

			}
		}

		}

		{

				((UIElement)this).DrawImageBackground(ref drawParams);
			}
		}

		protected override void InitAppearance(ref AppearanceData appearance, ref AppearancePropFlags requestedProps)
		{
			if (!((Control)(object)tabControl).Disposing && !((Control)(object)tabControl).IsDisposed)
			{
				requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)((ulong)requestedProps & 0xFFFFFFFFFC1FFFF7uL);
				requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)((ulong)requestedProps & 0xFFFFFFFF7FFFFFFFuL);
				tabControl.ResolveClientAreaAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
			}
			((ControlUIElementBase)this).InitAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
		}

		protected override bool OnPreMouseDown(ref MouseMessageInfo msgInfo)
		{
			UIElement val = ((UIElement)this).ElementFromPoint(new Point(((MouseMessageInfo)(ref msgInfo)).X, ((MouseMessageInfo)(ref msgInfo)).Y));
			if (val != null && ((MouseMessageInfo)(ref msgInfo)).Buttons != MouseButtons.Right && val.GetContext(typeof(UltraTab), true) is UltraTab && ((Control)(object)tabControl).Enabled && !((Control)(object)tabControl).Focused)
			{
				if (!tabControl.InternalSetFocus())
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (!((Control)(object)tabControl).ContainsFocus && !tabControl.IsDesignMode)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected override bool PerformKeyAction(Enum actionCode, bool shiftKeyDown, bool ctlKeyDown)
		{
			return tabControl.PerformAction((UltraTabControlAction)(object)actionCode);
		}

		protected override void PositionChildElements()
		{
			//IL_02f8: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_02fe: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0303: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0314: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0319: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_031b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_031e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0344: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			if (((Control)(object)tabControl).IsDisposed || ((Control)(object)tabControl).Disposing)
			{
				return;
			}
			((ArrayList)(object)((UIElement)this).ChildElements).Clear();
			_ = tabControl.SelectedTab;
			Rectangle rectInsideBorders = ((UIElement)this).RectInsideBorders;
			Rectangle rect = rectInsideBorders;
			switch (tabControl.StyleResolved)
			{
			case UltraTabControlStyle.Wizard:
				if (tabControl.IsDesignMode)
				{
					rect.Width = 84 + 2 * TabControl.NotePageEarMarkTopRight.Size.Width;
					rect.Height = 15;
					rect.Y = rectInsideBorders.Y;
					rect.X = rectInsideBorders.Right - rect.Width;
					((UIElement)WizardUIElement).Rect = rect;
					((UIElement)this).ChildElements.Add((UIElement)(object)WizardUIElement);
				}
				return;
			case UltraTabControlStyle.NotePage:
			case UltraTabControlStyle.NotePageFlat:
				if (tabControl.IsDesignMode)
				{
					rect.Width = 84;
					rect.Height = 15;
					rect.Y = rectInsideBorders.Y;
					rect.X = rectInsideBorders.Right - rect.Width;
					((UIElement)PageNumberElement).Rect = rect;
					((UIElement)this).ChildElements.Add((UIElement)(object)PageNumberElement);
				}
				if (tabControl.Tabs.Count <= 0)
				{
					return;
				}
				if ((tabControl.SelectedTab != null || tabControl.IsDesignMode) && tabControl.SelectedTab != null && ((tabControl.IsDesignMode && tabControl is UltraTabControl) || tabControl.SelectedTab != tabControl.VisibleTabs.FirstSelectableTab))
				{
					rect = rectInsideBorders;
					rect.Size = TabControl.NotePageEarMarkTopLeft.Size;
					rect.Size = ((ControlUIElementBase)this).AdjustForTouchMode(rect.Size);
					((UIElement)LeftEarMarkUIElement).Rect = rect;
					((UIElement)this).ChildElements.Add((UIElement)(object)LeftEarMarkUIElement);
				}
				if (tabControl.SelectedTab == null || tabControl.SelectedTab != tabControl.VisibleTabs.LastSelectableTab)
				{
					rect.Size = TabControl.NotePageEarMarkTopRight.Size;
					rect.Size = ((ControlUIElementBase)this).AdjustForTouchMode(rect.Size);
					rect.Y = rectInsideBorders.Y;
					rect.X = rectInsideBorders.Right - rect.Width;
					if (tabControl.IsDesignMode)
					{
						rect.Offset(-86, 0);
					}
					((UIElement)RightEarMarkUIElement).Rect = rect;
					((UIElement)this).ChildElements.Add((UIElement)(object)RightEarMarkUIElement);
				}
				return;
			}
			Rectangle tabHeaderAreaRect = tabControl.TabHeaderAreaRect;
			Rectangle rect2 = rectInsideBorders;
			_ = ((UIElement)TabPageAreaUIElement).BorderSides;
			if (tabControl.Tabs.VisibleTabsCount > 0)
			{
				UIElementBorderWidths borderWidths = ((UIElement)TabPageAreaUIElement).GetBorderWidths(((UIElement)TabPageAreaUIElement).BorderStyle, Border3DSide.Top);
				int top = ((UIElementBorderWidths)(ref borderWidths)).Top;
				TabOrientation tabOrientation = tabControl.TabOrientation;
				switch (tabOrientation - 1)
				{
				default:
					rect2.Height -= tabHeaderAreaRect.Height - top;
					rect2.Y += tabHeaderAreaRect.Height - top;
					break;
				case 2:
				case 3:
					rect2.Height -= tabHeaderAreaRect.Height - top;
					break;
				case 4:
				case 5:
					rect2.Width -= tabHeaderAreaRect.Width - top;
					rect2.X += tabHeaderAreaRect.Width - top;
					break;
				case 6:
				case 7:
					rect2.Width -= tabHeaderAreaRect.Width - top;
					break;
				}
			}
			((UIElement)TabPageAreaUIElement).Rect = rect2;
			((UIElement)this).ChildElements.Add((UIElement)(object)TabPageAreaUIElement);
			TabAreaUIElement.Rect = tabHeaderAreaRect;
			((UIElement)this).ChildElements.Add(TabAreaUIElement);
		}
	}

		[ComVisible(false)]
		private class UltraTabPageControlCollection : Control.ControlCollection
		{
			private UltraTabPageControl owner;

			internal UltraTabPageControlCollection(UltraTabPageControl owner)
				: base((Control)(object)owner)
			{
				this.owner = owner;
			}

			public override void Add(Control value)
			{
				if (value is TabPage || value is UltraTabPageControl)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_13"));
				}
				base.Add(value);
				if (owner.TabControl != null)
				{
					if (owner.tabControl.IsDesignMode)
					{
						DoTextBoxReparentWorkaround(value);
					}
					if (owner.TabControl.SharedControlsPage == owner)
					{
						owner.TabControl.SharedControls.Add(value);
					}
				}
			}

			public override void Remove(Control value)
			{
				base.Remove(value);
				if (owner.TabControl != null && owner.tabControl.IsDesignMode)
				{
					DoTextBoxReparentWorkaround(value);
				}
			}

			private void DoTextBoxReparentWorkaround(Control control)
			{
				if (control is TextBox { ScrollBars: var scrollBars } textBox)
				{
					if (scrollBars == ScrollBars.None)
					{
						textBox.ScrollBars = ScrollBars.Both;
					}
					else
					{
						textBox.ScrollBars = ScrollBars.None;
					}
					textBox.ScrollBars = scrollBars;
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnEnabledChanged(EventArgs e)

		}

		protected override void OnLocationChanged(EventArgs e)
		{
			((Control)this).OnLocationChanged(e);
			if (!((Control)this).IsHandleCreated || ((Control)this).Size.IsEmpty || tabControl == null || !tabControl.Initialized || !tabControl.IsDesignMode || !((Control)(object)tabControl).IsHandleCreated || tabControl.ConvertingInboxTab)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (this is UltraTabSharedControlsPage)
			{
				if (!(tabControl is UltraTabStripControl) && tabControl.SelectedTab != null)
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			else if (tabControl.SelectedTab == null || !tabControl.SelectedTab.HasTabPage || tabControl.SelectedTab.TabPage != this || TabControl.SelectedTab != tab)
			{
				return;
			}
			Point location = tabControl.TabPageRect.Location;
			if (((Control)this).Location != location)
			{
				((Control)this).Location = location;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnPaintBackground(PaintEventArgs e)

			{
				((ScrollableControl)this).OnPaintBackground(e);
			}
			else
			{
				PaintBackground(e);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnPaint(PaintEventArgs e)
		{
			((Control)this).RaisePaintEvent((object)this, e);
		}

		private void PaintBackground(PaintEventArgs e)
		{
			//IL_0050: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0185: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0282: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0322: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01d3: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (!((IGPanel)this).CanSubclass)
			{
				if (!lastDisplayRectPainted.IsEmpty && lastDisplayRectPainted != ((Control)(object)this).DisplayRectangle && ((Control)this).ClientRectangle != ((Control)(object)this).DisplayRectangle)
				{
					((Control)this).Invalidate(invalidateChildren: false);
				}
				lastDisplayRectPainted = ((Control)(object)this).DisplayRectangle;
			}
			AppearanceData appearance = default(AppearanceData);
			AppearancePropFlags requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)137438953471L;
			TabControl.ResolveClientAreaAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
			bool drawTabThemed = tabControl.DrawTabThemed;
			((UIElement)TabControl.UIElement).VerifyChildElements(true);
			TabPageAreaUIElement tabPageAreaUIElement = ((UIElement)TabControl.UIElement).GetDescendant(typeof(TabPageAreaUIElement)) as TabPageAreaUIElement;
			if (drawTabThemed || ((Control)(object)this).BackColor.A < byte.MaxValue)
			{
				((IGPanel)this).PaintTransparentBackground(e.Graphics, e.ClipRectangle);
			}
			else
			{
				Rectangle rectangle = ((tabPageAreaUIElement == null) ? ((Control)(object)TabControl).ClientRectangle : ((UIElement)tabPageAreaUIElement).Rect);
				Point renderingOrigin = e.Graphics.RenderingOrigin;
				e.Graphics.TranslateTransform(-((Control)this).Left, -((Control)this).Top);
				e.Graphics.RenderingOrigin = new Point(-((Control)this).Left, -((Control)this).Top);
				Rectangle clipRectangle = e.ClipRectangle;
				clipRectangle.Offset(((Control)this).Left, ((Control)this).Top);
				DrawUtility.DrawBackColor((Control)(object)tabControl, e.Graphics, ref appearance, rectangle, clipRectangle, ((Control)(object)tabControl).Enabled && ((Control)this).Enabled, ((UltraControlBase)TabControl).AlphaBlendMode);
				if (((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).ImageBackground != null)
				{
					Rectangle rectangle2;
					if (tabPageAreaUIElement != null)
					{
						rectangle2 = ((UIElement)tabPageAreaUIElement).RectInsideBorders;
						AnimatedImageManager.AnimateImageBackground(((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).GetImageBackground(((Control)this).Enabled), (UIElement)(object)tabPageAreaUIElement);
					}
					else
					{
						rectangle2 = ((Control)(object)TabControl).ClientRectangle;
					}
					DrawUtility.DrawImageBackground(e.Graphics, ref appearance, ((UltraControlBase)tabControl).AlphaBlendMode, clipRectangle, (Control)(object)tabControl, rectangle2, (ImageAttributes)null, ((Control)this).Enabled);
				}
				e.Graphics.RenderingOrigin = renderingOrigin;
				e.Graphics.TranslateTransform(((Control)this).Left, ((Control)this).Top);
			}
			if (tabControl == null || !tabControl.IsDesignMode || !(this is UltraTabSharedControlsPage))
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("SharedControlsPageDesignTimeLiteral");
			if (text != null && text.Length > 0)
			{
				SolidBrush solidBrush = Utilities.CreateSolidBrush(((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).GetForeColor(false));
				Rectangle clientRectangle = ((Control)this).ClientRectangle;
				Size size = Size.Ceiling(DrawUtility.MeasureString(e.Graphics, text, tabControl.DesignTimeLiteralFont, DrawUtility.GetGdiDrawStringFlags((UltraControlBase)(object)tabControl)));
				Rectangle rectangle3 = new Rectangle(Point.Empty, size);
				if (rectangle3.Width < clientRectangle.Width)
				{
					rectangle3.X = (clientRectangle.Width - rectangle3.Width) / 2;
				}
				if (rectangle3.Height < clientRectangle.Height)
				{
					rectangle3.Y = (clientRectangle.Height - rectangle3.Height) / 2;
				}
				rectangle3.X += 2;
				DrawUtility.DrawString(e.Graphics, text, tabControl.DesignTimeLiteralFont, solidBrush, (RectangleF)rectangle3, DrawUtility.GetGdiDrawStringFlags((UltraControlBase)(object)tabControl));
				solidBrush.Dispose();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnResize(EventArgs e)
		{
			((Panel)this).OnResize(e);
			if (((Control)this).IsHandleCreated && !((Control)this).Size.IsEmpty && tabControl != null && tabControl.Initialized && tabControl.IsDesignMode && ((Control)(object)tabControl).IsHandleCreated && !tabControl.ConvertingInboxTab)
			{
				Size tabPageSize = tabControl.TabPageSize;
				if (!tabPageSize.IsEmpty && ((Control)this).Size != tabPageSize)
				{
					((Control)this).BeginInvoke((Delegate)new MethodInvoker(SynchronizeSize));
				}
			}
		}

		internal void InitializeTab(UltraTab tab)
		{
			this.tab = tab;
			tabControl = tab.TabControl;
		}

		internal void InitializeTabControl(UltraTabControlBase tabControl)
		{
			this.tabControl = tabControl;
		}

		internal void OnPropertiesChanged()
		{
			if (!((Component)this).DesignMode)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				if (((Component)(object)this).GetService(typeof(IComponentChangeService)) is IComponentChangeService componentChangeService)
				{
					componentChangeService.OnComponentChanging(this, null);
					componentChangeService.OnComponentChanged(this, null, null, null);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		internal void SetRegion(Region region)
		{
			try
			{
				new UIPermission(UIPermissionWindow.AllWindows);
				((Control)this).Region = region;
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
			}
		}

		private void SynchronizeSize()
		{
			Size tabPageSize = tabControl.TabPageSize;
			if (!tabPageSize.IsEmpty && ((Control)this).Size != tabPageSize)
			{
				((Control)(object)this).AutoSize = false;
				((Control)this).Size = tabPageSize;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnHandleCreated(EventArgs e)
		{
			if (!((Control)this).RecreatingHandle)
			{
				CreateSubclasser();
			}
			((Control)this).OnHandleCreated(e);
		}

		private void CreateSubclasser()
		{
			try
			{
				SecurityPermission securityPermission = new SecurityPermission(SecurityPermissionFlag.UnmanagedCode);
				if (Utilities.CanAssert)
				{
					securityPermission.Assert();
				}
				CreateSubclasserUnsafe();
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
			}
		}

		private void CreateSubclasserUnsafe()
		{
			nativeWindow = new UiaProviderControlNativeWindow((IProvideUIAutomation)(object)this);
		}
	}

		private class ListObjectHolder
		{
			internal int listIndex = -1;

				this.listObject = listObject;
				this.comparisionListObject = comparisionListObject;
			}

			public override int GetHashCode()
			{
				return comparisionListObject.GetHashCode();
			}

			public override bool Equals(object o)
			{
				if (!(o is ListObjectHolder listObjectHolder))
				{
					return false;
				}
				return listObjectHolder.comparisionListObject == comparisionListObject;
			}
		}

		private UltraTabControlBase owningControl;

		private int initialCapacity = 20;

		private int visibleTabsCount;

		private UltraTab removingTab;

		object IList.this[int index]
		{

			}

			{
				if (IsReadOnly)
				{
					throw new NotSupportedException();

				UltraTab tabFromObject = GetTabFromObject(value);
				RemoveAt(index);
				InsertTabHelper(index, tabFromObject);
			}
		}

		{

			}
		}

				}

			}
		}

					EnsureInternalListCapacity(Count);
					for (int i = 0; i < Count; i++)
					{
						InternalGetTab(i);
					}
				}
				if (owningControl.IsDesignMode)
				{
					return Count;
				}
				return visibleTabsCount;
			}
		}

			EnsureInternalListCapacity(index + 1);
			return InternalGetTab(index);

		protected override void OnSubObjectPropChanged(PropChangeInfo propChangeInfo)
		{
			//IL_0086: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_008d: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			if (propChangeInfo.Source is UltraTab ultraTab)
			{
				switch ((UltraTabControlPropertyId)(object)propChangeInfo.PropId)
				{
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.Appearance:
					if (propChangeInfo.Trigger != null && propChangeInfo.Trigger.Source is AppearanceBase && propChangeInfo.Trigger.PropId is AppearancePropIds && (int)(AppearancePropIds)(object)propChangeInfo.Trigger.PropId == 9)
					{
						TabControl.TabManager.DirtyTabItem((ITabItem)(object)ultraTab, true, false, true);
					}
					else
					{
						TabControl.TabManager.DirtyTabItem((ITabItem)(object)ultraTab, true, false, false);
					}
					break;
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.Enabled:
					TabControl.TabManager.DirtyTabItem((ITabItem)(object)ultraTab, true, false, false);
					break;
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.FixedWidth:
					TabControl.TabManager.DirtyTabItem((ITabItem)(object)ultraTab, true, true, true);
					break;
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.Text:
					TabControl.TabManager.DirtyTabItem((ITabItem)(object)ultraTab, true, true, false);
					break;
				case UltraTabControlPropertyId.Visible:
					if (ultraTab.VisibleResolved)
					{
						visibleTabsCount++;
					}
					else if (visibleTabsCount > 0)
					{
						visibleTabsCount--;
					}
					TabControl.TabManager.Dirty(true, true, false);
					break;
				}
			}
			NotifyPropChange(UltraTabControlPropertyId.Tabs, propChangeInfo);
		}

		public int Add(object value)
		{
			if (TabControl != null && TabControl.IsDesignMode && !((UltraControlBase)TabControl).IsUpdating)
			{
				ISite site = ((Component)(object)TabControl).Site;
				if (site != null && site.GetService(typeof(IComponentChangeService)) is IComponentChangeService componentChangeService)
				{
					try
					{
						componentChangeService.OnComponentChanging(TabControl, null);
						componentChangeService.OnComponentChanged(TabControl, null, null, null);
					}
					catch (InvalidOperationException)
					{
					}
					catch (NullReferenceException)
					{
					}
				}
			}
			UltraTab ultraTab = value as UltraTab;
			bool flag = false;
			if (ultraTab != null && ultraTab.TabControl != null)
			{
				flag = true;
			}
			ultraTab = GetTabFromObject(value);
			if (ultraTab == null)
			{
				return -1;
			}
			int result = AddTabHelper(ultraTab);
			if (!flag && owningControl != null && owningControl.IsDesignMode && owningControl.Initialized)
			{
				if (!ultraTab.ShouldSerializeText())
				{
					ultraTab.Text = owningControl.GetNewTabText();
				}
				_ = ((Control)(object)ultraTab.TabPage).Size;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public UltraTab Add()
		{
			return Add("", "");
		}

		public UltraTab Add(string key)
		{
			return Add(key, "");
		}

		public UltraTab Add(string key, string text)
		{
			if (IsReadOnly)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException();
			}
			UltraTab ultraTab;
			if (key != null && key.Length > 0)
			{
				ValidateKeyDoesNotExist(key);
				ultraTab = new UltraTab(owningControl, key);
			}
			else
			{
				ultraTab = new UltraTab(owningControl);
			}
			if (text != null && text.Length > 0)
			{
				ultraTab.Text = text;
			}
			AddTabHelper(ultraTab);
			if (1 == Count && owningControl.SelectedTab == null && !owningControl.ConvertingInboxTab)
			{
				ultraTab.Selected = true;
			}
			return ultraTab;
		}

		public void AddRange(UltraTab[] tabs)
		{
			if (IsReadOnly)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException();
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < tabs.Length; i++)
			{
				Add(tabs[i]);
			}
		}

				ultraTab = new UltraTab(owningControl, key);

			}

			{
				ultraTab.Text = text;
			}
			InsertTabHelper(index, ultraTab);

			}
			return ultraTab;
		}

		public void Remove(UltraTab value)
		{
			Remove(value, disposeTabPage: false);
		}

		internal void Remove(UltraTab value, bool disposeTabPage)
		{
			if (removingTab != null && removingTab == value)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (IsReadOnly)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException();
			}
			if (value == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException();
			}
			removingTab = value;
			try
			{
				if (value == TabControl.InitialSelectedTab)
				{
					TabControl.InitialSelectedTab = null;
				}
				ITabItem val = null;
				bool selected = value.Selected;
				if (selected && TabControl != null && !((Control)(object)TabControl).Disposing && !((Control)(object)TabControl).IsDisposed)
				{
					val = TabControl.TabManager.GetPreviousSelectableTabItem((ITabItem)(object)value, false);
					if (val == null)
					{
						val = TabControl.TabManager.GetNextSelectableTabItem((ITabItem)(object)value, false);
					}
					if (val == null)
					{
						val = TabControl.TabManager.GetPreviousVisibleTabItem((ITabItem)(object)value, false);
					}
					if (val == null)
					{
						val = TabControl.TabManager.GetNextVisibleTabItem((ITabItem)(object)value, false);
					}
				}
				if (value.VisibleResolved)
				{
					if (visibleTabsCount > 0)
					{
						visibleTabsCount--;
					}
					TabControl.TabManager.Dirty(true, true, false);
				}
				value.SubObjectPropChanged -= base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				if (selected)
				{
					owningControl.SetSelectedTab(null);
				}
				int visibleIndex = value.VisibleIndex;
				InternalRemove(value);
				if (visibleIndex >= 0)
				{
					((IList)owningControl.VisibleTabsWrapper).RemoveAt(visibleIndex);
				}
				if (val != null)
				{
					owningControl.TabManager.Dirty(false, true, false);
					owningControl.SetSelectedTab(val as UltraTab);
				}
				value.CleanTabPage(disposeTabPage);
				NotifyPropChange(UltraTabControlPropertyId.Tabs, null);
			}
			finally
			{
				removingTab = null;
			}
		}

		public void RemoveAt(int index)
		{
			Remove(this[index]);
		}

		internal void AddDummyTabs()
		{
			UltraTab[] array = new UltraTab[120];
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				array[i] = new UltraTab(owningControl);
				((IList)owningControl.VisibleTabsWrapper).Add((object)array[i]);
			}
			List.AddRange(array);
			visibleTabsCount += array.Length;
			TabControl.TabManager.Dirty(true, true, true);
		}

		internal void BoundTabDeleted(int index)
		{
			if (!(List[index] is UltraTab ultraTab))
			{
				return;
			}
			ultraTab.SubObjectPropChanged -= base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
			List.RemoveAt(index);
			owningControl.VisibleTabs.RemoveInternal(ultraTab);
			int count = List.Count;
			for (int i = index; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (List[i] is UltraTab ultraTab2)
				{
					ultraTab2.InternalSetListIndex(i);
				}
			}
		}

		internal void CacheVisibleIndices()
		{
			int count = Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (GetItem(i) is UltraTab { VisibleOrderSerializer: null } ultraTab)
				{
					ultraTab.CacheVisibleIndex();
				}
			}
		}

		internal void ClearData()
		{
			int num = List.Count;
			if (num < 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				if (List[i] is UltraTab ultraTab && (ultraTab.CanBeModified || (ultraTab.TabControl != null && ultraTab.TabControl.Initializing)))
				{
					ultraTab.SubObjectPropChanged -= base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
					ultraTab.InternalOnRemovedFromCollection(this);
					if (!owningControl.IsDesignMode)
					{
						ultraTab.CleanTabPage();
					}
					InternalRemove(i);
					((IList)owningControl.VisibleTabsWrapper).RemoveAt(i);
					i--;
					num--;
				}
			}
			if (!owningControl.IsDesignMode)
			{
				TabControl.SetSelectedTab(null);
			}
			visibleTabsCount = num;
			TabControl.TabManager.Dirty(true, true, false);
			NotifyPropChange(UltraTabControlPropertyId.Tabs);
		}

		internal void ClearVisibleOrderSerializers()
		{
			int count = Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (List[i] != null)
				{
					((UltraTab)List[i]).VisibleOrderSerializer = null;
				}
			}
		}

		internal void EnsureInternalListCapacity(int capacity)
		{
			if (IsBound)
			{
				IList dataList = DataList;
				if (dataList == null)
				{
					ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
					_ = arrayList[capacity - 1];
				}
				if (capacity > List.Count && capacity <= dataList.Count)
				{
					object[] array = new object[capacity - List.Count];
					List.AddRange(array);
					TabControl.VisibleTabs.AddRangeInternal(array);
				}
			}
		}

		internal void RepositionTab(UltraTab tabToMove, UltraTab relativeTab, RelativePosition relativePosition)
		{
			//IL_002e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0030: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			int index = tabToMove.Index;
			if (index >= 0)
			{
				List.RemoveAt(index);
				int num = ((relativeTab == null) ? (-1) : List.IndexOf(relativeTab));
				int num2 = -1;
				num2 = (((int)relativePosition == 1) ? ((num >= 0) ? (num + 1) : 0) : ((num >= 0) ? num : List.Count));
				List.Insert(num2, tabToMove);
			}
		}

		internal void ResetTabListInfo(UltraTab tab, int listIndex, object listObject)
		{
			tab.ResetListIndex(listIndex, listObject);
			int num = IndexOf(tab);
			if (num >= 0 && listIndex >= 0 && num != listIndex)
			{
				List.RemoveAt(num);
				List.Insert(listIndex, tab);
			}
		}

		internal void SyncTabOrder(IList boundList)
		{
			if (boundList == null || boundList.Count < 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			Hashtable hashtable = new Hashtable(boundList.Count, 0.9f);
			bool flag = true;
			for (int i = 0; i < boundList.Count; i++)
			{
				object listObject = boundList[i];
				object objectForTabComparison = GetObjectForTabComparison(listObject);
				if (objectForTabComparison == null)
				{
					flag = false;
					break;
				}
				ListObjectHolder listObjectHolder = new ListObjectHolder(listObject, objectForTabComparison, i);
				hashtable[listObjectHolder] = listObjectHolder;
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				return;
			}
			UltraTab[] array = new UltraTab[boundList.Count];
			int num = 0;
			visibleTabsCount = 0;
			ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
			for (int i = 0; i < List.Count; i++)
			{
				UltraTab ultraTab = (UltraTab)List[i];
				ListObjectHolder listObjectHolder2 = null;
				if (ultraTab != null && ultraTab.ListObject != null)
				{
					object listObject2 = ultraTab.ListObject;
					object objectForTabComparison2 = GetObjectForTabComparison(listObject2);
					if (objectForTabComparison2 != null)
					{
						listObjectHolder2 = (ListObjectHolder)hashtable[new ListObjectHolder(listObject2, objectForTabComparison2, ultraTab.ListIndex)];
					}
				}
				if (listObjectHolder2 == null)
				{
					if (ultraTab != null)
					{
						arrayList.Add(ultraTab);
					}
					List[i] = null;
					continue;
				}
				int listIndex = listObjectHolder2.listIndex;
				if (array[listIndex] != null && array[listIndex] != ultraTab)
				{
					arrayList.Add(array[listIndex]);
				}
				num++;
				array[listIndex] = ultraTab;
				ultraTab.InternalSetListIndex(listIndex);
				ultraTab.InternalSetListObject(listObjectHolder2.listObject);
				List[i] = null;
			}
			bool flag2 = arrayList.Count != 0 || num != boundList.Count || num != List.Count;
			if (flag2)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < List.Count; i++)
				{
					UltraTab ultraTab2 = (UltraTab)List[i];
					if (ultraTab2 != null)
					{
						arrayList.Add(ultraTab2);
					}
				}
			}
			List.Clear();
			List.Capacity = array.Length;
			ArrayList arrayList2 = new ArrayList();
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				if (array[i] == null)
				{
					array[i] = new UltraTab(TabStripControl, i);
					array[i].InternalSetListIndex(i);
					array[i].InternalSetListObject(boundList[i]);
					arrayList2.Add(array[i]);
					flag2 = true;
				}
				if (array[i].VisibleResolved)
				{
					visibleTabsCount++;
				}
				List.Add(array[i]);
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < arrayList.Count; i++)
			{
				UltraTab ultraTab3 = (UltraTab)arrayList[i];
				ultraTab3.SubObjectPropChanged -= base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
			}
			if (flag2)
			{
				TabControl.VisibleTabs.ClearInternal();
				TabControl.VisibleTabs.AddRangeInternal(array);
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < arrayList2.Count; i++)
			{
				TabControl.RaiseEvent(UltraTabControlEventId.TabDataInitialized, new TabDataInitializedEventArgs((UltraTab)arrayList2[i], reinitialized: false));
			}
			if (flag2)
			{
				if (!List.Contains(TabControl.FirstDisplayedTab))
				{
					TabControl.TabManager.FirstDisplayedTabItem = (ITabItem)(object)(TabControl.TabManager.FirstVisibleTabItem as UltraTab);
				}
				if (!List.Contains(TabControl.SelectedTab))
				{
					TabControl.SelectedTab = TabControl.VisibleTabs.FirstSelectableTab;
				}
			}
		}

		private int AddTabHelper(UltraTab tab)
		{
			if (IsReadOnly)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException();
			}
			tab.SubObjectPropChanged += base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
			int result = InternalAdd(tab);
			if (tab.VisibleResolved)
			{
				visibleTabsCount++;
				TabControl.TabManager.Dirty(true, true, false);
			}
			((IList)owningControl.VisibleTabsWrapper).Add((object)tab);
			if (owningControl != null)
			{
				tab.InitControl(owningControl);
			}
			NotifyPropChange(UltraTabControlPropertyId.Tabs);
			return result;
		}

		internal object GetObjectForTabComparison(object listObject)
		{
			if (listObject is DataRowView dataRowView)
			{
				return dataRowView.Row;
			}
			return listObject;
		}

		private UltraTab GetTabFromObject(object value)
		{
			if (!(value is UltraTab ultraTab))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_ArgumentException_1"));
			}
			if (ultraTab.TabControl == null)
			{
				ultraTab.InitControl(owningControl);
			}
			else if (ultraTab.TabControl != owningControl)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_ArgumentException_2"));
			}
			return ultraTab;
		}

		private void InsertTabHelper(int index, UltraTab tab)
		{
			if (IsReadOnly)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException();
			}
			int index2 = 0;
			if (List.Count > 0)
			{
				index2 = ((index <= 0) ? InternalGetTab(0).VisibleIndex : (InternalGetTab(Math.Min(index - 1, List.Count - 1)).VisibleIndex + 1));
			}
			tab.SubObjectPropChanged += base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
			InternalInsert(index, tab);
			if (tab.VisibleResolved)
			{
				visibleTabsCount++;
				TabControl.TabManager.Dirty(true, true, false);
			}
			((IList)owningControl.VisibleTabsWrapper).Insert(index2, (object)tab);
			NotifyPropChange(UltraTabControlPropertyId.Tabs);
		}

		private UltraTab InternalGetTab(int index)
		{
			UltraTab ultraTab = List[index] as UltraTab;
			if (ultraTab == null && TabStripControl != null)
			{
				ultraTab = new UltraTab(owningControl as UltraTabStripControl, index);
				ultraTab.InternalSetListObject(TabStripControl.List[index]);
				TabControl.RaiseEvent(UltraTabControlEventId.TabDataInitialized, new TabDataInitializedEventArgs(ultraTab, reinitialized: false));
				ultraTab.SubObjectPropChanged += base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				if (ultraTab.VisibleResolved)
				{
					visibleTabsCount++;
				}
				List[index] = ultraTab;
			}
			return ultraTab;
		}

		bool IList.Contains(object P_0)
		{
			return Contains(P_0);
		}
	}

			protected override Control GetMarshallingControl()
			{
				return Utilities.GetMarshallingControl(((FragmentUIAProviderStub)this).GetParent());
			}

			public override IRawElementProviderFragment GetParent()
			{
				return ((UltraControlBase)VisibleTabs.TabControl).UIAutomationProvider;
			}

			public override IRawElementProviderFragment GetFirstChild()
			{
				VisibleTabsCollection visibleTabs = VisibleTabs;
				if (visibleTabs != null && visibleTabs.Count > 0)
				{
					return ((FragmentUIAProviderStub)visibleTabs[0].AutomationProviderStub).Provider;
				}
				return null;
			}

			public override IRawElementProviderFragment GetLastChild()
			{
				VisibleTabsCollection visibleTabs = VisibleTabs;
				if (visibleTabs != null && visibleTabs.Count > 0)
				{
					return ((FragmentUIAProviderStub)visibleTabs[visibleTabs.Count - 1].AutomationProviderStub).Provider;
				}
				return null;
			}

			public override IRawElementProviderFragment NavigateChildren(FragmentUIAProviderStub childStub, bool forward)
			{
				UltraTab.UltraTabUiaProviderStub ultraTabUiaProviderStub = childStub as UltraTab.UltraTabUiaProviderStub;
				VisibleTabsCollection visibleTabs = VisibleTabs;
				if (ultraTabUiaProviderStub != null && visibleTabs != null)
				{
					int num = visibleTabs.IndexOf(ultraTabUiaProviderStub.UltraTab);
					if (num != -1)
					{
						num += (forward ? 1 : (-1));
						if (num >= 0 && num < visibleTabs.Count)
						{
							return ((FragmentUIAProviderStub)visibleTabs[num].AutomationProviderStub).Provider;
						}
					}
				}
				return ((FragmentUIAProviderStub)this).NavigateChildren(childStub, forward);
			}
		}

		public class VisibleTabEnumerator : IEnumerator
		{
			private int position = -1;

			private VisibleTabsCollection tabs;

			public UltraTab Current => ((IEnumerator)this).Current as UltraTab;

			object IEnumerator.Current => tabs[position];

			internal VisibleTabEnumerator(VisibleTabsCollection tabs)
			{
				this.tabs = tabs;
			}

			public bool MoveNext()
			{
				if (position < tabs.Count - 1)
				{
					position++;
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}

			public void Reset()
			{
				position = -1;
			}
		}

		private class TabSortComparer : IComparer
		{
			int IComparer.Compare(object x, object y)
			{
				UltraTab ultraTab = x as UltraTab;
				UltraTab ultraTab2 = y as UltraTab;
				object sortValue = ultraTab.SortValue;
				object sortValue2 = ultraTab2.SortValue;
				if (object.ReferenceEquals(sortValue, sortValue2))
				{
					return 0;
				}
				int num = 0;
				if (sortValue == null)
				{
					num = -1;
				}
				else if (sortValue2 == null)
				{
					num = 1;
				}
				else if (sortValue is DBNull)
				{
					num = -1;
				}
				else if (sortValue2 is DBNull)
				{
					num = 1;
				}
				else if (sortValue is IComparable && sortValue2 is IComparable)
				{
					num = Comparer.Default.Compare(sortValue, sortValue2);
				}
				else
				{
					string strA = sortValue.ToString();
					string strB = sortValue2.ToString();
					num = string.Compare(strA, strB, ignoreCase: false, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
				}
				if (ultraTab.TabStripControl.SortDirection == SortDirection.Descending)
				{
					num = -num;
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

		private class TabTextSortComparer : IComparer
		{
			private SortDirection direction;

			internal TabTextSortComparer(SortDirection direction)
			{
				this.direction = direction;
			}

			int IComparer.Compare(object x, object y)
			{
				UltraTab ultraTab = x as UltraTab;
				UltraTab ultraTab2 = y as UltraTab;
				string text = ultraTab.Text;
				string text2 = ultraTab2.Text;
				int num = ((text == null) ? (-1) : ((text2 == null) ? 1 : string.Compare(text, text2, ignoreCase: false, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture)));
				if (direction == SortDirection.Descending)
				{
					num *= -1;
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

		private TabsCollectionUiaProviderStub uiaProviderStub;

		private UltraTabControlBase owningControl;

		private int sortVersion;

		private ArrayList list;

		internal TabsCollectionUiaProviderStub AutomationProviderStub
		{
			get
			{
				if (uiaProviderStub == null)
				{
					uiaProviderStub = new TabsCollectionUiaProviderStub(this);
				}
				return uiaProviderStub;
			}
		}

		IRawElementProviderFragment IProvideUIAutomation.UIAutomationProvider => ((FragmentUIAProviderStub)AutomationProviderStub).Provider;

		public int Count => List.Count;

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		[Browsable(false)]
		public UltraTab FirstSelectableTab
		{
			get
			{
				VerifySortOrder();
				return owningControl.TabManager.FirstSelectableTabItem as UltraTab;
			}
		}

		public UltraTab this[int index]
		{
			get
			{
				VerifySortOrder();
				if (List[index] is UltraTab result)
				{
					return result;
				}
				List[index] = owningControl.Tabs[index];
				return owningControl.Tabs[index];
			}
		}

		public UltraTab this[string key] => owningControl.Tabs[key];

		public bool IsSynchronized => List.IsSynchronized;

		public UltraTab LastSelectableTab
		{
			get
			{
				VerifySortOrder();
				return owningControl.TabManager.LastSelectableTabItem as UltraTab;
			}
		}

		public object SyncRoot => List.SyncRoot;

		public UltraTabControlBase TabControl => owningControl;

		internal ArrayList List
		{
			get
			{
				if (ListPrivate != null)
				{
					VerifySortOrder();
				}
				return list;
			}
		}

		private ArrayList ListPrivate
		{
			get
			{
				if (list == null)
				{
					if (IsBound)
					{
						list = new ArrayList(Math.Max(100, (TabStripControl.List != null) ? TabStripControl.List.Count : 100));
					}
					else if (owningControl.Tabs.Count > 0)
					{
						list = new ArrayList(Math.Max(20, owningControl.Tabs.Count));
					}
					else
					{
						list = new ArrayList(20);
					}
				}
				return list;
			}
		}

		private IList DataList
		{
			get
			{
				if (owningControl is UltraTabStripControl { List: not null } ultraTabStripControl)
				{
					return ultraTabStripControl.List;
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		private bool IsBound
		{
			get
			{
				if (owningControl is UltraTabStripControl { DataSource: not null })
				{
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		{

		}

		public void CopyTo(Array array, int index)
		{
			VerifySortOrder();
			List.CopyTo(array, index);
		}

			List.CopyTo(array, index);
		}

			return GetEnumerator();

			return new VisibleTabEnumerator(this);
		}

		public UltraTab GetNextSelectableTab(UltraTab tab)
		{
			VerifySortOrder();
			return owningControl.TabManager.GetNextSelectableTabItem((ITabItem)(object)tab, false) as UltraTab;
		}

		public UltraTab GetNextSelectableTab(UltraTab tab, bool wrap)
		{
			VerifySortOrder();
			return owningControl.TabManager.GetNextSelectableTabItem((ITabItem)(object)tab, wrap) as UltraTab;
		}

		public UltraTab GetPreviousSelectableTab(UltraTab tab)
		{
			VerifySortOrder();
			return owningControl.TabManager.GetPreviousSelectableTabItem((ITabItem)(object)tab, false) as UltraTab;
		}

		}

		public int IndexOf(UltraTab value)
		{
			return List.IndexOf(value);
		}

		public void Sort()
		{
			Sort(null);
		}

		public void Sort(SortDirection direction)
		{
			Sort(new TabTextSortComparer(direction));
		}

		public void Sort(IComparer comparer)
		{
			UltraTabsCollection tabs = TabControl.Tabs;
			IList dataList = DataList;
			if (dataList != null)
			{
				if (dataList.Count < 2)
				{
					return;
				}
				sortVersion = TabStripControl.SortVersion;
				tabs.EnsureInternalListCapacity(dataList.Count);
				for (int i = 0; i < tabs.Count; i++)
				{
					if (List[i] == null)
					{
						List[i] = tabs[i];
					}
				}
			}
			if (tabs.Count >= 2)
			{
				if (comparer == null)
				{
					comparer = new TabTextSortComparer(SortDirection.Ascending);
				}
				List.Sort(comparer);
				TabControl.TabManager.Dirty(true, true, false);
				if (TabControl.Initialized && TabControl.SelectedTab != null)
				{
					((Control)(object)TabControl).BeginInvoke((Delegate)new MethodInvoker(TabControl.EnsureSelectedTabIsInView));
				}
			}
		}

		internal void AddRangeInternal(object[] tabs)
		{
			ListPrivate.AddRange(tabs);
		}

		internal void ClearInternal()
		{
			if (list != null && list.Count >= 1)
			{
				list.Clear();
				sortVersion = 0;
			}
		}

		internal void RemoveInternal(UltraTab tab)
		{
			int num = ListPrivate.IndexOf(tab);
			if (num >= 0)
			{
				ListPrivate.RemoveAt(num);
			}
		}

		private void VerifySortOrder()
		{
			if (TabStripControl != null && TabStripControl.SortVersion != sortVersion && TabStripControl.SortMemberDescriptor != null)
			{
				IList dataList = DataList;
				if (dataList != null)
				{
					Sort(new TabSortComparer());
				}
			}
		}
	}

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		[TypeConverter("System.Windows.Forms.Design.DataSourceConverter, System.Design")]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_UltraTabStripControl_P_DataSource")]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedCategory("LC_Data")]
		[RefreshProperties(RefreshProperties.All)]
		public object DataSource
		{
			get
			{
				return dataSource;
			}
			set
			{
				if (dataSource == null || !dataSource.Equals(value))
				{
					if (dataSource != null && dataSource is IComponent)
					{
						((IComponent)dataSource).Disposed -= OnDataSourceDisposed;
					}
					dataSource = value;
					if (dataSource is IComponent)
					{
						((IComponent)dataSource).Disposed += OnDataSourceDisposed;
					}
					if (Utilities.IsDataMemberChangeRequired((Control)(object)this, dataSource, dataMember))
					{
						dataMember = string.Empty;
					}
					SetDataBinding(dataSource, dataMember);
					((UltraControlBase)this).NotifyPropChange((Enum)UltraTabControlPropertyId.DataSource);
				}
			}
		}

		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedCategory("LC_Data")]

		[DefaultValue(SortDirection.Ascending)]

		{
			get
			{
				return sortDirection;
			}
			set
			{
				if (sortDirection != value)
				{
					if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(SortDirection), value))
					{
						throw new InvalidEnumArgumentException("value", (int)value, typeof(SortDirection));
					}
					sortDirection = value;
					sortVersion++;
					((UltraControlBase)this).NotifyPropChange((Enum)UltraTabControlPropertyId.SortDirection);
				}
			}
		}
