		public UltraTabStripControl()
		{
			try
			{
				license = LicenseManager.Validate(typeof(UltraTabStripControl), this) as UltraLicense;
			}
			catch (FileNotFoundException)
			{
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnTabDataInitialized(TabDataInitializedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_3"));
			}
			((TabDataInitializedEventHandler)((Component)(object)this).Events[EVENT_TABDATAINITIALIZED])?.Invoke(this, e);
		}

		internal void RaiseTabDataInitialized(TabDataInitializedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnTabDataInitialized(e);
		}

		protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing)
			{
				UnWireDataSource();
				if (license != null)
				{
					license.Dispose();
					license = null;
				}
			}
			base.Dispose(disposing);
		}

		protected override ComponentRole CreateComponentRole()
		{
			return (ComponentRole)(object)new WinTabRole((UltraControlBase)(object)this, StyleUtils.ComponentType.TabStripControl);
		}

		internal override UltraTab AddNewTab()
		{
			IList list = List;
			if (!AllowAdd(list))
			{
				Form form = ((Control)(object)this).FindForm();
				MessageBox.Show(form, Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_UnableToAddTab"), form.Text);
				return null;
			}
			UltraTab result = null;
			if (list is IBindingList)
			{
				((IBindingList)list).AddNew();
				result = GetTabFromListIndex(base.Tabs.Count - 1);
			}
			else
			{
				BindingManagerBase bindingManagerBase = bindingManager;
				PropertyDescriptorCollection itemProperties = bindingManagerBase.GetItemProperties();
				if (itemProperties.Count == 1)
				{
					if (!DataBindingUtils.IsKnownType(itemProperties[0].PropertyType))
					{
						Form form2 = ((Control)(object)this).FindForm();
						MessageBox.Show(form2, Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_UnableToAddTab"), form2.Text);
						return null;
					}
					object value = DataBindingUtils.CreateObjectOfKnownType(list[0].GetType());
					list.Add(value);
					base.TabManager.Dirty(true, true, true);
					result = GetTabFromListIndex(base.Tabs.Count - 1);
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public int GetListIndexFromTab(UltraTab tab)
		{
			if (tab == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_14"));
			}
			return tab.ListIndex;
		}

		public object GetListObjectFromTab(UltraTab tab)
		{
			if (tab == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_14"));
			}
			return tab.ListObject;
		}

		public UltraTab GetTabFromListIndex(int listIndex)
		{
			if (listIndex < 0 || List == null || listIndex >= List.Count)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_15"));
			}
			SyncListObjects();
			if (base.Tabs[listIndex].ListIndex == listIndex)
			{
				return base.Tabs[listIndex];
			}
			foreach (UltraTab tab in base.Tabs)
			{
				if (tab.ListIndex == listIndex)
				{
					return tab;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public UltraTab GetTabFromListObject(object listObject)
		{
			SyncListObjects();
			UltraTabsCollection ultraTabsCollection = base.Tabs;
			object objectForTabComparison = ultraTabsCollection.GetObjectForTabComparison(listObject);
			foreach (UltraTab item in ultraTabsCollection)
			{
				if (item.ListObject == listObject)
				{
					return item;
				}
				if (objectForTabComparison == ultraTabsCollection.GetObjectForTabComparison(item.ListObject))
				{
					return item;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void SetDataBinding(object dataSource, string dataMember)
		{
			this.dataSource = dataSource;
			this.dataMember = dataMember;
			if (base.Initializing || inSetDataBinding)
			{
				return;
			}
			inSetDataBinding = true;
			try
			{
				UnWireDataSource();
				if (DataSource != null && ((Control)(object)this).BindingContext != null)
				{
					bindingManager = ((Control)(object)this).BindingContext[DataSource, DataMember];
				}
				else
				{
					bindingManager = null;
				}
				OnListManagerSet();
				if (bindingManager != null && List != null && List.Count > 0)
				{
					base.Tabs.EnsureInternalListCapacity(List.Count);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				inSetDataBinding = false;
			}
		}

		internal void InternalSetLastAddedTab(UltraTab tab)
		{
			if (lastAddedTab != tab)
			{
				if (lastAddedTab != null)
				{
					lastAddedTab.InternalSetIsAddRow(val: false);
				}
				lastAddedTab = tab;
			}
		}

		internal void SyncPositionWithSelectedTab()
		{
			if (!inPositionChanged && bindingManager != null && !((Component)(object)this).DesignMode)
			{
				SyncListObjects();
				UltraTab selectedTab = base.SelectedTab;
				if (selectedTab != null && bindingManager != null && selectedTab.ListIndex >= 0)
				{
					bindingManager.Position = selectedTab.ListIndex;
				}
			}
		}

		internal void SyncSelectedTabWithPosition()
		{
			if (bindingManager == null || ((Component)(object)this).DesignMode)
			{
				return;
			}
			SyncListObjects();
			inPositionChanged = true;
			try
			{
				int position = bindingManager.Position;
				UltraTab ultraTab = null;
				if (position >= 0 && position < base.Tabs.Count)
				{
					ultraTab = base.Tabs[position];
					ultraTab.InternalSetListObject(List[position]);
				}
				base.TabManager.SelectedTabItem = (ITabItem)(object)ultraTab;
			}
			finally
			{
				inPositionChanged = false;
			}
		}

		internal void SyncListObjects()
		{
			if (inSyncListObjects || bindingManager == null || ((Component)(object)this).DesignMode || listObjectVersionSynched == listObjectVersion)
			{
				return;
			}
			IList list = List;
			if (list == null || list.Count < 1)
			{
				base.Tabs.ClearData();
				return;
			}
			UltraTabsCollection ultraTabsCollection = base.Tabs;
			if (ultraTabsCollection.CountInternal == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			ultraTabsCollection.EnsureInternalListCapacity(list.Count);
			inSyncListObjects = true;
			listObjectVersionSynched = listObjectVersion;
			try
			{
				bool flag = ultraTabsCollection.CountInternal != list.Count;
				if (!flag)
				{
					for (int i = 0; i < list.Count; i++)
					{
						UltraTab ultraTab = ultraTabsCollection[i];
						if (ultraTab != null)
						{
							if (ultraTabsCollection.GetObjectForTabComparison(ultraTab.ListObject) != ultraTabsCollection.GetObjectForTabComparison(list[i]))
							{
								flag = true;
								break;
							}
							ultraTab.InternalSetListObject(list[i]);
						}
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					ultraTabsCollection.SyncTabOrder(list);
					base.TabManager.Dirty(true, true, false);
					((UIElement)base.UIElement).DirtyChildElements(true);
					if (sortMemberDescriptor != null)
					{
						sortVersion++;
					}
					else
					{
						RestoreTabOrder();
					}
					PositionTabPages(sharedAndSelectedTabsOnly: false, clearOldRegions: true);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				inSyncListObjects = false;
			}
		}

		internal void VerifyBindingManagerInitialized()
		{
			if (bindingManager == null && dataSource != null)
			{
				SetDataBinding(dataSource, dataMember);
			}
		}

		internal bool AllowAdd(IList dataList)
		{
			if (dataList is IBindingList bindingList)
			{
				return bindingList.AllowNew;
			}
			if (dataList != null)
			{
				return !dataList.IsFixedSize;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void InitializePropertyDescriptors()
		{
			//IL_0042: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			sortMemberDescriptor = null;
			tabKeyMemberDescriptor = null;
			tabTextMemberDescriptor = null;
			toolTipTextMemberDescriptor = null;
			if (dataSource == null || bindingManager == null || !base.Initialized)
			{
				return;
			}
			PropertyDescriptorCollection itemProperties = DataBindingUtils.GetItemProperties(bindingManager, new ValuePropertyDescriptorCreator(GetItemPropertiesCreator));
			PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor = null;
			foreach (PropertyDescriptor item in itemProperties)
			{
				if (!typeof(IList).IsAssignableFrom(item.PropertyType) || typeof(Array).IsAssignableFrom(item.PropertyType))
				{
					if (propertyDescriptor == null)
					{
						propertyDescriptor = item;
					}
					if (sortMember != null && sortMemberDescriptor == null && string.Compare(sortMember, item.Name, ignoreCase: false, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) == 0)
					{
						sortMemberDescriptor = item;
					}
					if (tabKeyMember != null && tabKeyMemberDescriptor == null && string.Compare(tabKeyMember, item.Name, ignoreCase: false, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) == 0)
					{
						tabKeyMemberDescriptor = item;
					}
					if (tabTextMember != null && tabTextMemberDescriptor == null && string.Compare(tabTextMember, item.Name, ignoreCase: false, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) == 0)
					{
						tabTextMemberDescriptor = item;
					}
					if (toolTipTextMember != null && toolTipTextMemberDescriptor == null && string.Compare(toolTipTextMember, item.Name, ignoreCase: false, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) == 0)
					{
						toolTipTextMemberDescriptor = item;
					}
				}
			}
			if (tabTextMemberDescriptor == null)
			{
				if (tabKeyMemberDescriptor != null)
				{
					tabTextMemberDescriptor = tabKeyMemberDescriptor;
				}
				else
				{
					tabTextMemberDescriptor = propertyDescriptor;
				}
			}
			if (((Component)(object)this).DesignMode)
			{
				sortMemberDescriptor = null;
				tabKeyMemberDescriptor = null;
				toolTipTextMemberDescriptor = null;
			}
		}

		private PropertyDescriptor GetItemPropertiesCreator(Type type, string name)
		{
			return (PropertyDescriptor)(object)new ValuePropertyDescriptor(type, this, name);
		}

		private void OnBindingListChanged(object sender, ListChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			try
			{
				switch (e.ListChangedType)
				{
				case ListChangedType.ItemMoved:
					listObjectVersion++;
					if (e.NewIndex >= 0 && e.OldIndex >= 0 && e.NewIndex != e.OldIndex)
					{
						UltraTab tabFromListObject = GetTabFromListObject(List[e.NewIndex]);
						if (tabFromListObject != null)
						{
							base.Tabs.ResetTabListInfo(tabFromListObject, e.NewIndex, List[e.NewIndex]);
						}
					}
					break;
				case ListChangedType.ItemAdded:
					listObjectVersion++;
					break;
				case ListChangedType.ItemDeleted:
					if (e.NewIndex >= 0)
					{
						base.Tabs.BoundTabDeleted(e.NewIndex);
					}
					else if (e.OldIndex >= 0)
					{
						base.Tabs.BoundTabDeleted(e.OldIndex);
					}
					listObjectVersion++;
					break;
				case ListChangedType.PropertyDescriptorAdded:
				case ListChangedType.PropertyDescriptorDeleted:
				case ListChangedType.PropertyDescriptorChanged:
					InitializePropertyDescriptors();
					base.TabManager.Dirty(true, false, true);
					break;
				case ListChangedType.Reset:
					listObjectVersion++;
					SyncListObjects();
					break;
				case ListChangedType.ItemChanged:
					break;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				if (base.IsDesignMode)
				{
					throw;
				}
			}
		}

		private void OnBindingsChanged(object sender, CollectionChangeEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		private void OnCurrentChanged(object sender, EventArgs ea)
		{
		}

		private void OnDataSourceDisposed(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			DataMember = null;
			DataSource = null;
		}

		private void OnItemChanged(object sender, ItemChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Index >= 0)
			{
				UltraTab tabFromListIndex = GetTabFromListIndex(e.Index);
				if (tabFromListIndex != null)
				{
					base.TabManager.DirtyTabItem((ITabItem)(object)tabFromListIndex, true, true, false);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				listObjectVersion++;
				base.TabManager.Dirty(true, true, true);
			}
		}

		private void OnListManagerSet()
		{
			if (!base.Initialized)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				base.Tabs.ClearData();
				if (bindingManager == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (bindingManager != null)
				{
					if (((Component)(object)this).DesignMode)
					{
						base.InitialSelectedTab = null;
					}
					PropertyDescriptorCollection itemProperties = bindingManager.GetItemProperties();
					bool flag = false;
					string text = null;
					foreach (PropertyDescriptor item in itemProperties)
					{
						if (typeof(IList).IsAssignableFrom(item.PropertyType) && !typeof(Array).IsAssignableFrom(item.PropertyType))
						{
							if (text == null)
							{
								text = item.Name;
							}
							continue;
						}
						flag = true;
						break;
					}
					if (!flag && text != null)
					{
						string text2 = dataMember;
						text2 = ((text2 == null || text2.Length == 0) ? text : (text2 + "." + text));
						bindingManager = ((Control)(object)this).BindingContext[DataSource, text2];
					}
				}
				WireDataSource();
				if (bindingManager != null && ((Component)(object)this).DesignMode)
				{
					base.Tabs.AddDummyTabs();
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				if (base.UIElement != null)
				{
					sortVersion++;
					InitializePropertyDescriptors();
					SyncSelectedTabWithPosition();
					((UIElement)base.UIElement).DirtyChildElements(true);
				}
			}
		}

		private void OnPositionChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			SyncSelectedTabWithPosition();
		}

		private string StripMember(string text)
		{
			if (text == null || text.Length < 1)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			string[] array = text.Split('.');
			int num = array.Length - 1;
			return array[num];
		}

		internal void UnWireDataSource()
		{
			if (bindingManager == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (bindingManager is CurrencyManager currencyManager)
			{
				if (currencyManager.List is IBindingList)
				{
					IBindingList bindingList = (IBindingList)currencyManager.List;
					if (bindingList != null)
					{
						bindingList.ListChanged -= OnBindingListChanged;
					}
				}
				currencyManager.CurrentChanged -= OnCurrentChanged;
				currencyManager.PositionChanged -= OnPositionChanged;
				currencyManager.ItemChanged -= OnItemChanged;
			}
			bindingManager.Bindings.CollectionChanged -= OnBindingsChanged;
		}

		private void WireDataSource()
		{
			if (bindingManager == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (bindingManager is CurrencyManager currencyManager)
			{
				if (currencyManager.List is IBindingList)
				{
					IBindingList bindingList = (IBindingList)currencyManager.List;
					if (bindingList != null)
					{
						bindingList.ListChanged += OnBindingListChanged;
					}
				}
				currencyManager.CurrentChanged += OnCurrentChanged;
				currencyManager.PositionChanged += OnPositionChanged;
				currencyManager.ItemChanged += OnItemChanged;
			}
			bindingManager.Bindings.CollectionChanged += OnBindingsChanged;
		}
	}
	internal class TabUtilities
	{
		internal static bool FocusControl(Control control)
		{
			try
			{
				return control.Focus();
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
				try
				{
					if (control.CanFocus)
					{
						NativeWindowMethods.SetFocusApi(control.Handle);
					}
				}
				catch (SecurityException)
				{
				}
			}
			return control.Focused;
		}
	}
	internal class VisibleTabsCollectionWrapper : IList, ICollection, IEnumerable
	{
		private VisibleTabsCollection visibleTabsCollection;

		int ICollection.Count => visibleTabsCollection.Count;

		bool ICollection.IsSynchronized => visibleTabsCollection.List.IsSynchronized;

		object ICollection.SyncRoot => visibleTabsCollection.List.SyncRoot;

		object IList.this[int index]
		{
			get
			{
				return visibleTabsCollection[index];
			}
			set
			{
				if (visibleTabsCollection[index] != value)
				{
					visibleTabsCollection.List[index] = value;
				}
			}
		}

		bool IList.IsFixedSize => false;

		bool IList.IsReadOnly => false;

		internal VisibleTabsCollection VisibleTabsCollection => visibleTabsCollection;

		internal VisibleTabsCollectionWrapper(UltraTabControlBase owningControl)
		{
			visibleTabsCollection = new VisibleTabsCollection(owningControl);
		}

		IEnumerator IEnumerable.GetEnumerator()
		{
			return visibleTabsCollection.GetEnumerator();
		}

		void ICollection.CopyTo(Array array, int index)
		{
			visibleTabsCollection.List.CopyTo(array, index);
		}

		int IList.Add(object value)
		{
			int result = visibleTabsCollection.List.Add(value);
			VisibleTabsCollection.TabControl.AccessibilityNotifyClientsInternal(AccessibleEvents.Reorder, -1);
			return result;
		}

		void IList.Clear()
		{
			VisibleTabsCollection.TabControl.AccessibilityNotifyClientsInternal(AccessibleEvents.Reorder, -1);
			visibleTabsCollection.List.Clear();
		}

		bool IList.Contains(object value)
		{
			return visibleTabsCollection.List.Contains(value);
		}

		int IList.IndexOf(object value)
		{
			return visibleTabsCollection.List.IndexOf(value);
		}

		void IList.Insert(int index, object value)
		{
			VisibleTabsCollection.TabControl.AccessibilityNotifyClientsInternal(AccessibleEvents.Reorder, -1);
			visibleTabsCollection.List.Insert(index, value);
		}

		void IList.Remove(object value)
		{
			VisibleTabsCollection.TabControl.AccessibilityNotifyClientsInternal(AccessibleEvents.Reorder, -1);
			visibleTabsCollection.List.Remove(value);
		}

		void IList.RemoveAt(int index)
		{
			VisibleTabsCollection.TabControl.AccessibilityNotifyClientsInternal(AccessibleEvents.Reorder, -1);
			if (index >= 0 && index < visibleTabsCollection.List.Count)
			{
				visibleTabsCollection.List.RemoveAt(index);
			}
		}
	}
	public class WizardNavigationButtonUIElement : UIElement
	{
		private UltraTabControlBase tabControl;

		private ImageUIElement imageElement;

		private bool previous;

		public override Border3DSide BorderSides
		{
			get
			{
				if (previous)
				{
					return Border3DSide.Left | Border3DSide.Top | Border3DSide.Bottom;
				}
				return Border3DSide.Top | Border3DSide.Right | Border3DSide.Bottom;
			}
		}

		public override UIElementBorderStyle BorderStyle => (UIElementBorderStyle)4;

		public bool Previous => previous;

		public UltraTabControlBase TabControl => tabControl;

		private ImageUIElement ImageElement
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_0037: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0041: Expected O, but got Unknown
				if (imageElement == null || ((DisposableObject)(object)imageElement).Disposed)
				{
					imageElement = new ImageUIElement((UIElement)(object)this, previous ? tabControl.ArrowLeftImage : tabControl.ArrowRightImage);
				}
				if (previous)
				{
					if (tabControl.SelectedTab == null)
					{
						((UIElement)imageElement).Enabled = false;
					}
					else if (tabControl.SelectedTab == tabControl.VisibleTabs.FirstSelectableTab)
					{
						((UIElement)imageElement).Enabled = tabControl.IsDesignMode && !(tabControl is UltraTabStripControl);
					}
					else
					{
						((UIElement)imageElement).Enabled = true;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					((UIElement)imageElement).Enabled = tabControl.SelectedTab != tabControl.VisibleTabs.LastSelectableTab;
				}
				return imageElement;
			}
		}

		public WizardNavigationButtonUIElement(UIElement parent, UltraTabControlBase tabControl, bool previous)
			: base(parent)
		{
			this.tabControl = tabControl;
			this.previous = previous;
		}

		protected override void InitAppearance(ref AppearanceData appearance, ref AppearancePropFlags requestedProps)
		{
			((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).BackColor = SystemColors.Control;
			((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).BorderColor = SystemColors.ControlDark;
			if (!((UIElement)imageElement).Enabled)
			{
				((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).AlphaLevel = 128;
				((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).ImageAlpha = (Alpha)1;
				((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).BackColorAlpha = (Alpha)2;
				((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).BorderAlpha = (Alpha)2;
			}
		}

		protected override bool OnMouseDown(MouseEventArgs e, bool adjustableArea, ref UIElement captureMouseForElement)
		{
			if ((e.Button & MouseButtons.Left) == MouseButtons.Left)
			{
				if (((Control)(object)tabControl).IsDisposed || ((Control)(object)tabControl).Disposing)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (previous && tabControl.IsDesignMode && tabControl.SelectedTab == tabControl.VisibleTabs.FirstSelectableTab && !(tabControl is UltraTabStripControl))
				{
					tabControl.ShowSharedControlsPage();
				}
				else
				{
					tabControl.PerformAction(previous ? UltraTabControlAction.SelectPreviousTab : UltraTabControlAction.SelectNextTab);
				}
				return true;
			}
			return ((UIElement)this).OnMouseDown(e, adjustableArea, ref captureMouseForElement);
		}

		protected override void PositionChildElements()
		{
			if (!((Control)(object)tabControl).IsDisposed && !((Control)(object)tabControl).Disposing)
			{
				((ArrayList)(object)((UIElement)this).ChildElements).Clear();
				Rectangle rect = ((UIElement)this).Rect;
				Size size = TabControl.ArrowLeftImage.Size;
				rect.X += (rect.Width - size.Width) / 2;
				rect.Y += (rect.Height - size.Height) / 2;
				rect.Size = size;
				((UIElement)ImageElement).Rect = rect;
				((UIElement)this).ChildElements.Add((UIElement)(object)ImageElement);
			}
		}
	}
	public class WizardNavigationUIElement : UIElement
	{
		private UltraTabControlBase tabControl;

		private TabPageNumberUIElement pageNumberElement;

		private WizardNavigationButtonUIElement leftArrowElement;

		private WizardNavigationButtonUIElement rightArrowElement;

		public UltraTabControlBase TabControl => tabControl;

		private WizardNavigationButtonUIElement LeftArrowElement
		{
			get
			{
				if (leftArrowElement == null || ((DisposableObject)(object)leftArrowElement).Disposed)
				{
					leftArrowElement = new WizardNavigationButtonUIElement((UIElement)(object)this, tabControl, previous: true);
				}
				return leftArrowElement;
			}
		}

		private WizardNavigationButtonUIElement RightArrowElement
		{
			get
			{
				if (rightArrowElement == null || ((DisposableObject)(object)rightArrowElement).Disposed)
				{
					rightArrowElement = new WizardNavigationButtonUIElement((UIElement)(object)this, tabControl, previous: false);
				}
				return rightArrowElement;
			}
		}

		private TabPageNumberUIElement PageNumberElement
		{
			get
			{
				if (pageNumberElement == null || ((DisposableObject)(object)pageNumberElement).Disposed)
				{
					pageNumberElement = new TabPageNumberUIElement((UIElement)(object)this, tabControl);
				}
				return pageNumberElement;
			}
		}

		public WizardNavigationUIElement(UIElement parent, UltraTabControlBase tabControl)
			: base(parent)
		{
			this.tabControl = tabControl;
		}

		protected override void PositionChildElements()
		{
			if (!((Control)(object)tabControl).IsDisposed && !((Control)(object)tabControl).Disposing)
			{
				((ArrayList)(object)((UIElement)this).ChildElements).Clear();
				Rectangle rect = ((UIElement)this).Rect;
				Rectangle rect2 = rect;
				rect2.Width = 15;
				((UIElement)LeftArrowElement).Rect = rect2;
				((UIElement)this).ChildElements.Add((UIElement)(object)LeftArrowElement);
				rect.X += 15;
				rect.Width -= 30;
				((UIElement)PageNumberElement).Rect = rect;
				((UIElement)this).ChildElements.Add((UIElement)(object)PageNumberElement);
				rect2.X = rect.Right;
				((UIElement)RightArrowElement).Rect = rect2;
				((UIElement)this).ChildElements.Add((UIElement)(object)RightArrowElement);
			}
		}
	}
}
